3. makkaḷum malarkaḷum
tamiḻ makkaḷ
tamiḻ makkaḷ intiyāvil oru taṉi iṉam. avarkaḷuk keṉṟu oru taṉi moḻiyum taṉip paṇpāṭum taṉi neṟiyum taṉik kalaikaḷum taṉi nākarikamum uṇṭu. avarkaḷ piṟa iṉattavarkaḷi ṉiṉṟu uṭaiyālum uṇavālum piṟavaṟṟālum vēṟupaṭṭa varkaḷ.
avarkaḷ mikat toṉmaiyāṉavarkaḷ. inta nāṭṭukku urit tāṉavarkaḷ. iṅku nāṉ tamiḻarkaḷ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭum poḻutu tirāviṭarkaḷāṉa malaiyāḷikaḷ, āntirarkaḷ, kaṉṉaṭarkaḷ tuḷuvarkaḷ pōṉṟa aṉaivaraiyum uḷḷaṭakkip pēcukiṟēṉ.
tamiḻarkaḷ pallāṇṭāka innāṭṭil vāḻntu inta nāṭṭil oru paṇpāṭṭaiyum kalaiyaiyum nākarikattaiyum vaḷarttu varukiṉṟārkaḷ.
malarkaḷ
tamiḻ makkaḷ vāḻvil nīṇṭa kālamāka, malarkaḷ toṭarpu peṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷ vāḻvilum tāḻvilum malar iṇaintiruppataip pārkkalām.
avarkaḷ vāḻvu arumpiya kuṟiñci nilattiliruntē avar kaḷōṭu malarum toṭarntu varukiṟatu.
varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭē tamiḻarkaḷ ceṭi, koṭi maraṅkaḷōṭum malarkaḷōṭum ilaikaḷōṭum neruṅkit toṭarpu koṇṭirukkiṟārkaḷ. tamiḻarkaḷ ātiyil kuṟiñci nilattilē vēṭṭuva vāḻkkaiyil īṭupaṭṭa nilaiyiliruntu mara vaṇakkattaiyum malar vaṇakkattaiyum ceytu varukiṉṟaṉar. ceṭikaḷilum koṭikaḷilum maraṅkaḷilum kiṇṇam pōṉṟa vaṭivil civappu, mañcaḷ, nīlam, ūtā vaṇṇaṅkaḷil eṇṇilāta malarkaḷ eṅkum kottuk kottāyp pūttuk kuluṅkiṉa. avaikaḷiṉiṉṟu naṟumaṇam eṅkum vīciyatu. malarkaḷiṉ maṇamum vaṇṇaṅkaḷiṉ aḻakum, makkaḷ uḷḷattaik kavarntaṉa.
vaḻipāṭu
tamiḻ makkaḷ vāḻnta ellā nilattilum ārampattil tāy āṭciyē nilaviyatu. ataṉ palaṉākat tāy vaṇakkamum eḻuntatu. tāy āṭciyil, tāy iṟantatum marattaṭiyil avaḷukku naṭukal nāṭṭi makkaḷ vaṇaṅka muṟpaṭṭaṉar. itaṉāl mikap paḻaiya kālattilē tamiḻakattil tāy vaṇakkam, āvuṭaiyār uruva vaṇakkam maravaṇakkam mutaliyavai eḻuntaṉa. appāl tāy āṭci kuṉṟic cēy āṭci eḻuntatu. cēy āṭciyilē cēy vaḻipāṭu muruka vaḻipāṭu muḷaittatu. cēyk kaṭavuḷukkum cēy iṟantapiṉ marattiṉaṭiyil kalnaṭṭu vaḻipāṭu ceyyappaṭṭu vantatu. itaṉait tamiḻp periyār tiru. vi.ka. avarkaḷ mika aḻakāka eṭuttukkāṭṭi yuḷḷārkaḷ. atu aṭiyil varumāṟākum.
paṇṭait tamiḻ makkaḷ iyaṟkai vaḻi vāḻvaic celutti ataṟku aṭippaṭaiyāka uḷḷa iṟaivaṉuṇmai kaṇṭu, avaṉ, aḻakāl iyaṟkai aḻaku peṟuvatu nōkki avviṟaivaṉai aḻakaṉ eṉṉum poruḷpaṭa murukaṉ eṉṉum peyarāl aḻait tārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ iyaṟkai vāyilāka murukaik kaṇṭu nukarnta iṉpattaik kalittokai pattuppāṭṭu tirukkōvaiyār mutaliya nūṟkaḷil kāṇalām.5
pūkkaḷum puṉita nilamum
tamiḻar vāḻvu malarnta iṭam malaiyēyākum atāvatu kuṟiñci nilamākum. aṅkē tāṉ avarkaḷiṉ paḻam perum paṇ pāṭu piṟantatu. aṅkētāṉ avarkaḷiṉ kalaikaḷum nākarikamum tuḷirttaṉa. aṅkētāṉ avarkaḷiṉ teyvaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. avarkaḷiṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷ arumpiṉa. avarkaḷ aṅkiruntē piṟa nilaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiṉar. avarkaḷ ātiyil vāḻnta nilam kuṟiñci eṉa aḻaikkap peṟṟatu.
tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil pulavarkaḷ ovvoru tiṇaiyiṉ karupporuḷaiyum naṉku ārāyntu arumperum uvamaṅkaḷai yum  uraittirukkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷāl aintiṇaikkum iṭap peṟṟa peyarkaḷ, avarkaḷatu aṟivut tiṟaṉaiyum iyaṟkaiyiṉ mītu avarkaḷ koṇṭa aṉpaiyum veḷippaṭuttuvatākum. tamiḻarkaḷ malaikkum malaicārnta iṭattiṟkum kuṟiñci eṉṟu peyar kūṟiṉar.
kuṟiñci malaināṭṭil niṟkum oru ceṭi. atu paṉṉiraṇṭu āṇṭiṟku orumuṟai pūkkum. atu pūttuk kuluṅki niṟkum kālattu malaiccāral muḻuvatum orē nīlaniṟamāka uḷḷaṅkavarum eḻiluṭaṉ oḷirum. tamiḻarkaḷ tāṅkaḷ vāḻnta nilattai aṅkuḷḷa akil, āram, vēṅkai mutaliya maraṅkaḷiṉ peyarāl aḻaiyātu kuṟiñci malariṉ peyarāl kuṟiñci nilam eṉak kuṟippiṭṭatu āḻntu cintittaṟkuriyatu. iḵtu maṭṭumiṉṟi avarkaḷ piṉṉar kuṭiyēṟiya ovvoru iṭattiṟkum āṇṭuḷḷa marañceṭi koṭikaḷiṉ malariṉ peyarālē mullai nilam, pālai nilam, marutanilam, neytal nilam eṉap peyariṭṭuḷḷatum kavaṉikkattakkatu. pūkkaḷ mikat toṉṟu toṭṭē tamiḻarkaḷukku oru iṉṟiyamaiyāta mukki yattuvam vāynta poruḷāka iruntu vantuḷḷaṉa. eṉavētāṉ avarkaḷ vāḻvu arumpiya mikap paḻaiya kālattilē tāṅkaḷ vāḻnta puṉita nilattiṟku pūkkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷai vaittuḷḷaṉar.
vāḻvum malarum
tamiḻarkaḷ kuṟiñci nilattilē tam vāḻvai ārampittaṉar. akkālattil avarkaḷiṉ kuṭumpat talaivi tāyāka iruntāḷ. mutaṉ mutalāka ap paḻam perum camūkattil tāy āṭciyum tāyt teyvamum eḻuntaṉa.
tamiḻarkaḷ kuṟiñci nilattilē iyaṟkai vāḻvaik kaṇṭaṉar. atilē iṉpuṟṟut tiḷaittu niṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ malaikaḷil malarkaḷiṉ maṇattai nukarntu makiḻntu vantaṉar. inta maṇam - atāvatu muruku (vācaṉai) eṅkiruntu evvāṟu taṅkaḷiṭam pōntu taṅkaḷukku iṉpam ūṭṭukiṟatu eṉpatai aṉṟu avar kaḷāl aṟiya muṭiyavillai. ētō oru kaṭ pulaṉukku eṭṭāta oru caktitāṉ namakku inta iṉpattai aḷikkiṟatu eṉṟu nampiṉar. murukai (maṇattai) uṇṭākkum anta caktikku murukaṉ eṉṟu peyariṭṭaṉar. atai vaḻipaṭavum ārampittaṉar. itu namatu paḻam perum nūlākiya tolkāppiyam tōṉṟuvataṟku paṉṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku  muṉpē eḻuntākum. tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷ 5000 āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉpē tamiḻ makkaḷ kuṟiñci vāḻkkaikku aṭikōliya ārampak kālattilē murukaṉ eḻuntu viṭṭāṉ eṉṟu kūṟuki ṟārkaḷ. atāvatu putukkaṟkālattiṉ iṟutiyil eṉṟu kūṟuki ṟārkaḷ.
tamiḻarkaḷ toṉṟu toṭṭē maraṅkaḷōṭum malarka ḷōṭum mānilattōṭum mika neruṅkit toṭarpu koṇṭavar kaḷāy iruntavarkaḷ āvārkaḷ. ātikālattil maraṅkaḷum ceṭi koṭikaḷum avarkaḷukku uṟaiyuḷāka - maṉaiyāka malarntiruntaṉa. avaikaḷē avarkaḷukku uyirūṭṭum kaṟpakat taruvāka (kāy, kaṉi, kiḻaṅku, ilai, malarkaḷait taruvaṉavāka) iruntaṉa. avaikaḷē avarkaḷiṉ yākkaikku uṟumpiṇikaḷai akaṟṟum māmaruntākat tikaḻntaṉa. avaikaḷē, avarkaḷukku makiḻcciyūṭṭum maṇattai yum, aḻaku ceyyum aṇikaḷaiyum (pūkkaḷaiyum) araiyiṟ piṇaikkum āṭaikaḷaiyum (marappaṭṭaikaḷaiyum) uyirkākkum āyutattaiyum (villaiyum) uḷḷaṅkaḷikkum icaikkaruvi kaḷaiyum (vilyāḻaiyum) tōṟṟuvittatu. avarkaḷ kiṇṇam pōṉṟa vaṇṇamalarkaḷaik kaṇṭum, uḷḷaṅkavarntu uvakai ūṭṭum maṇattai nukarntum kaḻipēruvakai eytiṉar. ataṉāl avarkaḷiṭaiyē malar vaṇakkamum maravaṇakkamum tuḷirttaṉa.
pūvum pūvaiyārum
tamiḻ makkaḷ malarkaḷ mītu koṇṭa aḷavu kaṭanta aṉpāl, pirikka muṭiyāta piṇaippāl taṅkaḷiṉ uyiraṉaiya makaḷiraik koṭi eṉṟaṉar. appāl peṇpālarkkup peṇ koṭi eṉṟu peyar cūṭṭiṉar. pūṅkoṭi eṉṟu aḻaittaṉar. mēlum avarkaḷaip pūvaiyar, pūviyalār, melliyalār eṉṟel lām kūṟi makiḻntaṉar. peṇ makkaḷukkup picci, kamalam mallikai mutaliya malarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷaiyum cūṭṭi makiḻntaṉar.
pūṅkoṭiyum koḻu kompum
pūṅkoṭi ciṟitāka irukkumpoḻutu yātoru cārpumiṉṟi tuḷirttu eḻum. atu naṉṟāka vaḷarntu paṭarum paruvam vantatum atu oru koḻu kompai nāṭum. atu aṇittē niṟkum oru valuvāṉa marakkompaip paṟṟip paṭarum. atu pōṉṟu paruvam aṭainta pāvaiyarkaḷ pūppeytiyatum oru valimaimikka iḷa vīraṉ tōḷ cērtalai eṇṇi āyntu teḷintu peṇṇiṟkup pūṅkoṭi eṉṟu peyar cūṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. itaṉ mūlam avarkaḷ peṇṇiṟkup pūṅkoṭi eṉṟu iṭṭappeyar ettuṇai ciṟappu vāyntatu eṉpatu naṉkaṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatallavā?
maṅkaiyum malarum
pūṅkoṭi paruvam vantuṟṟa poḻutu koṭi vīci oru koḻu kompaip paṟṟip paṭarntu kaḷittuc ceḻittu vaḷarntu pūttu maṇappataip pōṉṟu makaḷirum paruvam vantatum āṭavaṉ tōḷ cērvatai maṇattal eṉṟu kūṟiṉar. peṇkaḷ paruvam aṭai talaip pūttal eṉṟaṉar.
peṇṇaip pūṅkoṭi eṉṟa tamiḻar, avaḷ paruvam eytuvataip pūttal eṉṟum avaḷ oru āṭavaṉōṭu kūṭi vāḻap pōvatai maṇattal eṉṟum kūṟiyatu ettuṇai poruttamāṉatu? aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē tamiḻarkaḷ ettuṇai poruḷ tarum muṟaiyil taṅkaḷ moḻiyai amaittuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu itaṉmūlam naṉkaṟiyalām. mēlum tirumaṇac caṭaṅkil, peṇ kaḻuttil āṇ oru pūmālaiyaic cūṭṭuvatum piṉ peṇ taṉ kaḻuttil uḷḷa oru pūmālaiyai āṇ kaḻuttil cūṭṭuvatum mukkiya caṭaṅkākum. itaṟkut tamiḻar mālaiyiṭal, mālai māṟṟal eṉṟellām kūṟuvar.
maṇam nikaḻum poḻutu maṇamakkaḷ mītu avarkaḷiṉ uṟaviṉarkaḷum naṇparkaḷum pūkkaḷait tūvi vāḻttuvar. tamiḻarkaḷ taṅkaḷ vīṭṭiṟkō nāṭṭiṟkō varum viruntiṉar kaḷaip pūmālai cūṭṭi varavēṟpar. teyva uruvaṅkaḷukkup pūmālai cūṭṭi vaḻipaṭuvar. pūkkaḷ tūvi pukaḻntu pāṭuvar. tirumaṇattil maṭṭumaṉṟi vīṭṭil nikaḻum ellāc caṭaṅkukaḷilum viḻākkaḷilum eṅkum malarkaḷāl alaṅkarippar. muṟkālattil pāvalarum pāṇaṉum, pāṭiṉiyum viṟaliyum kūttarum pārvēntar pāl ceṉṟu tam kalaittiṟaṉaik kāṭṭiṉāl pūvēntarkaḷ puḷakāṅkitamuṟṟu avarkaḷukkup pūmālaikaḷai aṇivippar. piṟkālattil poṉṉāṟ ceyta tāmaraip pūvai aḷittum paricilāka ūrum vīṭum kaḷiṟum poṉ vaṭṭilum poṉ aṇi kaḷum piṟavum aḷittum ciṟappuc ceytaṉar eṉṟu tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ cāṉṟu tarukiṉṟaṉa.
pūvum iyaṟkaiyum
pūṅkoṭiyil paruvam vantapoḻutu mukai arumpum; ciṟiya mukaikaḷil maṇam eḻātu; aḻakum arumpātu, paruvam eytiṉa piṉṉarē mukaikaḷ mutirntu malarāka māṟum. pū malarnta piṉṉarē atil aḻaku oḷirum, maṇam poṅki eḻum, ataṉ aṭiyil tēṉ ūṟum. pūviṉ vaṇṇaṅkaḷaiyum aḻakaiyum maṇattaiyum kaṇṭu vaṇṭukaḷ vantu malar mītu amarntu ataṉai maṇantu ataṉ tēṉaiyuṇṭu cuvaikka nāṭivarum. vaṇṭukaḷ oru pūvil amarntu ataṉ tēṉaiyuṇṭatum aṭutta pūvai nāṭic cellum. ataṉ kālil paṭintuḷḷa makarantap poṭikaḷ aṭuttu amarum malaril paṭintu ataṉ karuppaikkuḷ ceṉṟu ammalaraic cūluṟac cey kiṟatu. inta vaṇṭu mūlam ceṭi koṭi maram mutaliyavai karuvuṟṟuk kāyttup paḻukkiṉṟaṉa. pakalil malarum malarkaḷ ellām palvēṟu vaṇṇaṅkaḷil irukkum. āṉāl, iruḷ niṟainta iravil malarum malarkaḷ peritum veṇmaiyāka irukkum. iravil veṇmai niṟamalarkaḷ virivatāl enta iruṭṭilum vaṇṭukaḷ eḷitil maṇattaiyum niṟattaiyum aṟintu ataṉ mītu amarntu maṇantu iṉpattēṉaiyuṇṭu cūluṟac ceytu viṭukiṉṟaṉa.
iyaṟkai kāṭṭum inta uṇmaiyai aṟintē akkāla makaḷir iravil veṇmai niṟamāṉa āṭaiyai aṇintu vantaṉar. veḷḷaic cēlai mālai āṭai eṉṟa peyaraip peṟṟatu. paruvam vāynta pāvaiyarkaḷum iḷaiñarkaḷum paṇṭaik kālattil iravil veṇmaiyāṉa āṭaikaḷai aṇintu vantaṉar. vaṇṭukaḷaip pōl paṇṭaikkālap paruvap peṇkaḷukkut tōḻiyar tuṇaiyāka iruntaṉar. iravu nēraṅkaḷil peṇ koṭi kaḷum avarkaḷiṉ kātalarkaḷāṉa kāḷaikaḷum iravil kātal maṇam puriya viḻaintu niṟpārkaḷām. iravil avarkaḷ cantikka vēṇṭiya iṭattiṟkuk kuṟiyāka malarkaḷai vaippārkaḷ eṉṟu tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. mukattilum (muttamiṭutalum) maṇattalum (tirumaṇam puritalum) makkaḷ vaṇṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu kaṇṭa iyaṟkai neṟi; iṉpaneṟi eṉṟu terikiṟatu. vaṇṭukaḷ pūvi luḷḷa tēṉaiyuṇpataippōl āṇkaḷ peṇkaḷiṉ muttattāl iṉpuṟṟu avar umiḻ nīrai uṟiñci iṉpantuykkak kaṟṟaṉar eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ viḷakkam tarukiṉṟaṉar. namatu vaḷḷuvap perun takaiyum itaṉai aṟintē.
	pāloṭu tēṉkalantaṟṟē paṇimoḻi
	 vāleyi ṟūṟiya nīr
eṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. tamiḻarkaḷiṉ āṇ peṇ kūṭṭu vāḻkkaiyum ataṉ palaṉāka eḻum iṉpamum iyaṟkaiyiliruntu tamiḻaṉ kaṟṟa pāṭamēyākum.
tamiḻ makkaḷ peṇkaḷaip pūṅkoṭiyāka, pūvaiyākak kaṇṭaṉar. cila pulavarkaḷ peṇkaḷiṉ uṟuppukaḷ aṉaittum tāmarai malarākak kaṇṭārkaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭāka,
	ceṅkamalam nāṭṭam
		ceḻun tāmarai vataṉam
	 paṅkayam cevvāy
		patumam pōl ceṅkaraṅkaḷ
	 ampōrukantāṉ aravintam māraṉār
		tampōrukan tāṉ taṉam
eṉṟum,
	tirumukaṅ kamalam iṇaiviḻi kamalam 
		ceyyavāy kamalam nittilam kamalam
	 varumulai kamalam tuṇaikkaram kamalam
		valampuri unti poṟkamalam
	 perukiya alkul maṇittaṭam kamalam
		piṭit taṭait tāḷkaḷum kamalam
	 uru avaṭ kavvāṟu ātali ṉaṉṟē
		uyarnta pūviṉuṭ kamalam
eṉṟa pāṭṭukkaḷai eṭuttiyampalām. ivai tamiḻarkaḷ pūviṉukku aḷitta mukkiyattuvattaiyum pūvaippaṟṟi naṭattiya āyvaiyum kāṭṭuvaṉavākum.7
āṉāl kārikaiyiṉ kaṇkaḷaik karuṅkuvaḷaip pūviṟkum kamala itaḻukkum kākaṇa malarukkum oppākavum mūkkai eḷḷiṉ pūviṟkum, kumiḻam pūviṟkum, vāyai kumuta malarukkum, pallai mullai arumpiṟkum, kātai vaḷḷai malarukkum, kaiyaik kāntaḷ pūviṟkum, koṅkaiyai paṅkaya mukaikkum, utarattai makiḻa malarukkum kālkaḷai aṉicca malarukkum, mēṉiyaic ceṇpaka malarukkum mukattai muṇṭakap pūviṟkum, uvamāṉamāka oru pulavaṉ eṭuttukkāṭṭit taṉatu kaṟpaṉait tiṟaṉai ellāp pulavariṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭutti uyarttikkāṭṭiyuḷḷāṉ. kampaṉ kārikaiyiṉ aḻakai eṭuttukkāṭṭuvatil atika āṟṟal peṟṟa vaṉ. avaṉ arivaiyiṉ aṅkaṅkaḷai maṭṭumaṉṟi oru ciṟu mayirai yum ataṉ iyalpukkēṟṟa eḻil kuṉṟātu eṭuttukkāṭṭupavaṉ. apperuṅkaviñaṉ,
	kai kāntaḷ vāy kumutam
		kaṇ neytal, kārikaiyīr
	 mey vār taḷir koṅkai
		meṉ kōṅkam - ivvaṉaittum
	 vaṉmai cōrntu āvi
		varuntuvatu mātavam oṉṟu
	iṉmaiyō aṉṟō emakku
eṉa maṅkaiyiṉ uṟuppukaḷ aṉaittaiyum ovvoru malarukku uvamāṉamāka oppiṭṭu uraittiruppatu namatu tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅ kaḷil paṭikkap paṭikkat tikaṭṭāta iṉpattaip perukkum.
tamiḻarkaḷ, malaikaḷil tōṉṟiya maram ceṭi koṭikaḷ aṭarnta vaṉaṅkaḷil vāḻntu, malarkaḷiṉ maṇaṅkaḷai nukarntu, avaṟṟiṉ aḻakiya amaippaik kaṇṭu kaḷittu, vaṇṇaṅkaḷai uṇarntu uvakaiyuṟṟu, malarkaḷiluḷḷa tēṉaiyuṇṭu iṉpuṟṟu vanta oru toṉmaiyāṉa iṉam. eṉavē avarkaḷukku maṅkai (pūvai) maṭṭum, malarākak kāṭciyaḷikkavillai. avarkaḷiṉ kaṇṇum, mukamum, mūkkum utaṭum kaikaḷum kālkaḷum piṟa melliya uṟuppukaḷum ovvoru malarākak kāṭci aḷittamai kaṇṭu ulakam eṉṟum uvantu pōṟṟum.
tamiḻarkaḷum nilamum
tamiḻarkaḷ ātiyil ulakattai nāṉilam eṉṟaṉar. appāl atai pū, pūtalam, pūtiyam, pūvalayam pūvulakam, pūmātēvi, pūmakaḷ eṉṟellām aḻaittu vantaṉar. tamiḻarkaḷ pūviṉ peyaril koṇṭa aṉpiṉ mūlamākavē tāṅkaḷ vāḻum periya nilavula kiṟkuk kūṭa ap peyarai aḷittuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu aṟikiṉṟōm.
maṅkaiyarkaḷukku malarkaḷ mītu maṭṭilāta matippuṇṭu, kavarcciyuṇṭu. mara vaṇakkamum malar vaṇakkamum malar aṇiyum māṇpum tamiḻakattil maṭṭumaṉṟit tamiḻarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōr kaḷāṉa tirāviṭarkaḷ vāḻnta ulakil oppaṟṟa nākarikattai niṟuviya arappā mokañcatārō mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum nilaviyatu eṉṟu car. cāṇ mārcal pōṉṟa aṟiñarkaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. tamiḻakattil maṅkaiyarkaḷ taṅkaḷ kūntalkaḷil naṟumaṇam nāṟum naṉ malarkaḷaic cūṭi varuvatu paḻamperum paṇpu. kārikaiyarkaḷ kaḻunīr, mallikai cevvanti picci mutaliya malarkaḷait taṅkaḷatu piṉṉiviṭap peṟṟa paṉiccaiyil pāṅkuṟa aṇintatai; oru pāṭṭil pulavar oruvar aḻakuṟa eṭuttukkāṭ ṭiyuḷḷār. 8 makaḷir  malarkaḷaip pukaḻntu vantaṉar; pōṟṟi vantaṉar; puṉitamāka eṇṇi vaḻipāṭṭiṟkup payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar. aḻakup poruḷāka niṉaittu aṅkaṅkaḷil aṇintu akamakiḻntu āṭip pāṭi vantaṉar. malarkaḷai maṅkalam malkum mānitiyāka matittu vantaṉar.
tamiḻ makkaḷ akavoḻukkattil malarkaḷaip payaṉpaṭutti vantataik kaṇṭu nām āccariyappaṭuvataṟkillai. āṉāl avarkaḷ puṟa oḻukkattilum pūkkaḷaip payaṉpaṭutti vantatai aṟiyap peritum viyappāka irukkiṟatu. tamiḻnāṭṭil paṇṭaikkālat til pōrkaḷil ovvoru nilaikkum ovvoru pū aṭai yāḷamāka irukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu avarkaḷiṉ pōr ilakkaṇam kūṟukiṟatu. pōr ārampittatum ovvoru aracaṉum taṉ paṭai vīrarkaḷukku, pūkkaḷai aḷippāṉ. veṭci, karantai, vañci, kāñci, uḻiñai, nocci tumpai vākai mutaliya pūkkaḷ aṭaiyāḷap pūkkaḷākum. ip pūkkaḷ pōrvīrarkaḷ ceyyum pōrt toḻi liṉ potu vakaiyiṉai uṇarttuvaṉavākum. pōril mutalnilai, pakaivarkaḷiṉ pacukkaḷaik kavartal; itu veṭci eṉappaṭum. kavarntu ceṉṟa pacukkaḷai mīṭkap pōriṭuvatu karantai eṉappaṭum. pakaivar nāṭṭiṉmītu paṭaiyeṭuttuc celluvatu vañciyākum. vantavarkaḷai māṟṟaracaṉ etirttut tākkuvatu kāñci eṉappaṭum. pakaivar nāṭṭiṉ araṇ arukē paṭaikaḷ pōntu nakarai muṟṟukaiyiṭṭut tākkutal uḻiñai eṉappaṭum. araṇiṟku uḷḷē irukkum aracaṉ araṇaik kāttup pōriṭutal nocci eṉappaṭum. tākka vanta aracaṉai māṟṟu maṉṉaṉ etir ceṉṟu kaṭum pōr āṟṟal tumpai eṉappaṭum. veṟṟipeṟutal vākai eṉappaṭum. iṅkuk kūṟiya veṭci, karantai, vañci, kāñci, uḻiñai nocci, tumpai, vākai eṉpaṉa aṭaiyāḷap pūkkaḷākum. ovvoru paṭaiyum ovvoru nilaiyilum taṅkaḷ nilaikkuriya pūkkaḷai aṇintu koḷḷum. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka mutaṉmutalāka pōril pakaivarkaḷiṉ āniraikaḷaik kavarum nilaiyil pōr vīrarkaḷ, veṭci malarai aṇintu koḷvar; veṟṟiyiṉpōtu vākai malaraic cūṭikkoḷvar. ilakkiyaṅkaḷil veṟṟi, vākai cūṭiṉāṉ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭum.9
pūkkaḷiṉ paruvaṅkaḷ
tamiḻ makkaḷ pūkkaḷiṉ mītu atikap paṟṟuṭaiyavarkaḷāy viḷaṅkiṉar. avarkaḷ vāḻvu pūvil arumpi, pūvil malarntu pūvōṭu neruṅkit toṭarpu koṇṭatāka irukkiṟatu. pūkkaḷ makkaḷiṉ piṟappu mutal iṟappuvarai ēṉ? - iṟappiṟkup piṉ naṭaipeṟum kāṭāṟṟu. kalleṭuppu nīttār niṉaivu nāḷ mutaliya aṉaittilum iṭam peṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa.
pūkkaḷ tamiḻarkaḷiṉ camayaṅkaḷil taṉiyiṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. piṟa iṉattavarkaḷaiviṭat tamiḻarkaḷ taṅkaḷ camayaṅkaḷil pūviṟku mutaliṭam aḷittuḷḷārkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ teyvaṅkaḷ ellām pūviṉ mītē tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru teyvattiṟkum ovvoru malar urittāṉatāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷ teyvam āṇāyiṉum peṇṇāyiṉum avaikaḷiṉ kaḻuttilum talaiyilum pūkkaḷ iṭam peṟṟirukkum. avarkaḷiṉ kōyilkaḷ eṅkum pūvē alaṅkārap poruḷāka irukkum. avarkaḷiṉ maṟaikaḷilum purāṇaṅkaḷilum, tōttirap pāṭalkaḷilum ākamaṅkaḷilum malarkaḷ ciṟappāṉa iṭattaip peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa.
tamiḻarkaḷiṉ ilakkiyaṅkaḷilellām pūkkaḷ ciṟappittup pāṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻarkaḷ pūviṟku ataṉ palvēṟu nilai kaḷukkum paruvattiṟkēṟpa palvēṟu peyarkaḷ cūṭṭi pāṅkuṭaṉ aḻaittu varukiṉṟārkaḷ. arumpu, mukai, pōtu, alar, malar ākiya aintu coṟkaḷum pūviṉ palvēṟu paruvaṅkaḷai uṇarttu kiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷaip payiṉṟār naṉkaṟivar. naṟumaṇam kamaḻum nantavaṉattiṟkuc ceṉṟāl aṅkup palatiṟap paṭṭa paruvam amainta malarkaḷaik kāṇamuṭiyum. aṅku arum pākavum  mukaiyākavum pōtākavum alarākavum malarākavum tikaḻu vataik kaṇṇāl kaṇṭukaḷikkalām. pūviṉ mutaṟ paruvam arumpu;  arumpu mutirum nilaiyai mukai allatu moṭṭu eṉṟu kūṟap paṭum. mukai aviḻum paruvam, pōtu eṉappaṭum. pōtu, malarum nilaiyil alar eṉappaṭum, pōtu naṉṟāka malarnta nilaiyai malar eṉṟu cuṭṭik kāṭṭappeṟukiṟatu.
malarum nilamum
iṟṟaikku mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē tamiḻakam kuṟiñci, mullai, marutam, neytal, pālai eṉṉum aintu nilap pirivukaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. inta nilappirivukaḷ iṭampaṟṟi eḻuntālum kāraṇap peyarākavē tōṉṟiṉa. ovvoru nilat tilum peruvāriyākak kāṇappaṭṭa malarkaḷiṉ kāṭciyaik kaṇṭē ippeyar cūṭṭappeṟṟatākat terikiṟatu. kuṟiñci malarkaḷ niṟainta iṭam kuṟiñcinilam eṉṟum, mullai malarkaḷ niṟainta iṭam mullainilam eṉṟum, maruta malarkaḷ niṟainta iṭam marutanilam eṉṟum, neytal malarkaḷ niṟainta iṭam  neytal nilam eṉṟum, pālai malarkaḷ niṟainta iṭam pālai nilam eṉṟum aḻaikkappaṭṭaṉa. inta nilaṅkaḷil vāḻum makkaḷai nilattiṉ peyarkaḷaiyiṭṭē aḻaittaṉar. ovvoru nila makkaḷukkum taṉittaṉiyē teyvaṅkaḷum, talaivarkaḷum yāḻum, paṇṇum, paṟaiyum puḷḷum poḻutum vilaṅkum paṟavaikaḷum piṟavum taṉittaṉiyākap pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ovvoru nilaṅ kaḷukkum uriya malarkaḷum taṉittaṉiyē pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kuṟiñci nilattiṟkuk kuṟiñci kāntaḷ, vēṅkai, cuṉaikkuvaḷai, pōṉṟavai urittāṉa malarkaḷākum. neytal nilattiṟku veḷḷi taṭkaitaiyum, neytalum pālai nilattiṟkuk kuravu, pātiri mutaliyaṉavum mullai nilattiṟku mullai, tōṉṟi mutaliyaṉavum maruta nilattiṟku marutam, tāmarai, kaḻunīr mutaliyaṉavum urittāṉa malarkaḷākum.
inta nilattil vāḻum makkaḷ nilapētam kulapētam etuvum iṉṟi virumpiya nilattavarkaḷaik kātalittu maṇantu koḷvar. namatu paḻaiya tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ, uruvilum, tiruvilum, kulatti lum, nalattilum, aṟivilum, parivilum otta taṉmai vāynta talaivaṉum talaiviyum taṉiyiṭattē cantittu kātal koṇṭu vantārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷ iravu allatu pakal cantikkum iṭattai uṇartta, kuṟippatu vaḻakkam. ataṉai iravuk kuṟi11 ā allatu pakaṟkuṟi 11a eṉpar. intak kuṟiyait talaivi taṉ tōḻi mūlam talaivaṉukkuk kūṟi aṉuppuvāḷ. antat tōḻiyiṉ vārttaippaṭiyē iravilō allatu pakalilō talaivaṉ kuṟippiṭṭa poḻilukku varuvāṉ. aṅkut talaivi kuṟippiṭṭa kuṟiyiṭattai aṟintu talaiviyiṉ varukaikkāka antak kuṟi yiṭattil kāttu niṟpāṉ. antak kuṟi pūvāka irukkum. oru talaivi pakaṟkālattil talaivaṉaic cantikkap pōntāḷ. kuṟiyiṭattil talaivaṉ niṟpatu kaṇṭu kaḷittāḷ. appāl avaṉ arukil ceṉṟu kātal uraiyāṭi kaḻipēruvakai koṇṭāḷ. itaṉai aḻakuṟa vāḻttic caṅkakkālap pulavar oruvar oru oppaṟṟa ōviyam tīṭṭiyuḷḷār. atil kuṟiyīṭṭumalar pēcappaṭuvatu kuṟippiṭat takkatu. atu aṭiyil varumāṟākum.
nīṇṭu parutta tōḷkaḷaiyuṭaiya talaiviyum yāṉum tiṉaippuṉaṅ kākka nāḷaic celvōm. aṅkē kuraṅkukaḷum ēṟa muṭiyātavāṟu aṭarnta maraṅkaḷuḷḷa iḷañcōlaiyil kuṉṟi ṉiṉṟu viḻum aruvikaḷ palavaṟṟiṉ kuḷircciyum iṉimaiyum poruntiya viruppantarum nīr nilaiyiṉ arukil oḷi mikka ceṅkāntaḷ pūkkaḷ malarntirukkum iṭattil pāmpiṉ māṇikka maṇiyiṉ oḷi viḷakkattil kariya kūntalaiyuṭaiya kārikaiyiṉ kaḷippup poruntiya kalavi iṉpattai aṭaivāyāka eṉṟu akanāṉūṟṟil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.11
ivvāṟu malarkaḷaik kuṟiyākavum aṇiyākavum marun tākavum maṅkalap poruḷākavum12 kātalaraik kāṇpataṟkuriya kuṟi malarākavum cuvaiyākavum, maṇamākavum eṇṇi aṉupavittu vantatu paḻam perum tamiḻ nākarikam. iṟṟaikku iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭaḷavil - atāvatu kaṭaiccaṅkak kālattil tamiḻarkaḷ koṭi, ilai, taḷir, arumpu mutaliyavaikaḷaiyum malar, kāy, paḻam, koṭṭai mutaliyavaikaḷaiyum taṉittaṉiyākavum oṉṟākavum cērttuk kaṭṭit talaiyilum kaḻuttilum kaikaḷilum aṇintu vantaṉar. iṉṟum ataṉ aṭiccuvaṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟu tamiḻnāṭṭu makaḷir aṇintuḷḷa poṉ aṇikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷē ataṟku nalla cāṉṟāka miḷirkiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟum tamiḻp peṇkaḷ taṅkaḷ poṉ aṇikaḷai malar vaṭivākavum, taḷir vaṭivākavum, taḻai vaṭivākavum arumpu vaṭivākavum malar vaṭivākavum, kāy vaṭivākavum paḻa vaṭivākavum koṭi vaṭivākavum ceytu aṇintu varukiṉṟaṉar.
tamiḻarkaḷ talaiyil aṇiyum mālaiyaik kaṇṇi eṉavum, kaḻuttil āṭavar aṇiyum mālaiyait tār eṉavum makaḷir kaḻuttil aṇiyum mālaiyaik kōtai eṉavum kūṟivantaṉar. peṇkaḷ pūviṉāl toṭutta pūvāṭaikaḷaiyum taḻaikaḷāl toṭutta taḻai uṭaikaḷaiyum aṇintu vantaṉar.
toṉṟu toṭṭu tamiḻ makkaḷ pūkkaḷōṭu koṇṭa toṭarpāl pūkkaḷaip palvēṟu vitamākak kōkkavum palvēṟu kāriyaṅkaḷukkup payaṉpaṭuttavum vaḻi kaṇṭaṉar. itaṉ payaṉāka avarkaḷ kaṇṭa aṟupattu nāṉku kalaikaḷil pūttoṭuppatu oru kalaiyāka iṭam peṟṟatu.
kuṟippukaḷ 
6.	Tree Snake Worship, Frgusson P. 20
	intiyāvil intukkaḷukkup puṉitamāṉa maram aracamaram ākum. aracu civaperumāṉ, tirumāl, nāṉ murukaṉ amcamāṉatu eṉṟu pōṟṟappaṭukiṟatu. peṇ makkaḷ, makkaḷ celvam vēṇṭi aracamarattai valam varukiṉṟaṉar. ūrtōṟum aracamaramum vēppamaramum vaittu vaḷarttu ataṉ aṭiyil cilai nāṭṭi makaḷir kuḷittu malarcūṭṭi valam vantu vaṇaṅkukiṉṟārkaḷ.
	vilva maram civaperumāṉukku ukanta maramāka eṇṇi makkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉar. ataṉ ilai teyvattiṉ āyutamākiya cūlāyutam pōṉṟatu. vēmpu caktiyiṉ vaṭivam. atai vaṇaṅkiṉāl aṉēka nōykaḷ akalum eṉṟu nampappaṭukiṟatu. vēmpu teyvīka maram eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. tuḷaci tirumakaḷ amcam eṉṟum tirumālukku ukantatu eṉṟum vaḻipaṭappaṭukiṟatu. ālamarattiṉ aṭiyil civaperumāṉ uṟaintār. avar ālamar celvaṉ eṉṟaḻaikkap peṟṟār. tamiḻarkaḷiṉ teyvaṅkaḷ ellām marattiṉ kīḻē iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. im maraṅkaḷ talaviruṭcam eṉappaṭum.
7.	kampa rāmāyaṇam (caṇmukam piḷḷai) 1924 kalaimakaḷ antāti - kampaṉ.	
carōruka mētiruk kōyiluṅ kaikaḷum tāḷiṇaiyu	
	murōruka muntiru valkulu nāpiyu mōṅkiruḷ pōṉ	
	murōrukañ cūḻnta vataṉamu nāṭṭamuñ  cēyitaḻu	
	morēruka mīrarai māttirai yāṉaivurai makaṭkē
8.	irattiṉac curukkam - pukaḻēnti	
mālai kaḻunīr mullai mallikaic cevvanti picci	
	yēlam vakuḷamilait toṭai tēṉ - cāla naṟum	
	puṉṉai pacumpāḷai polintu piṭittaṭakkai	
	piṉṉiviṭṭa kūntaleṉap pēcu.
9.	(a) tolkāppiyam - puṟattiṇaiyiyal	
(ā) puṟa nāṉūṟu	
(i) puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmālai
10.	aka nāṉūṟu - maturaip pālāciriyar naṟṟāmaṉār	
nerumaṉ paṇaittōḷ ivaḷum yāṉum	
kāval kaṇṇiṉam tiṉaiyē nāḷai	
mantiyum aṟiyā marampayil iṟumpiṉ	
oṇceṅ kāntaḷ aviḻnta āṅkaṇ	
taṇpala aruvit tāḻnīr oru ciṟai	
urumuc civantu eṟinta uraṉaḻi pāmpiṉ	
tirumaṇi viḷakkil peṟukuvai	
irumeṉ kūntal ēm uṟu tuyilē	(akam 92)
11.	(a) iṟaiyaṉār akapporuḷ urai. pak. 18
	iṟantupāṭṭu accattiṉāl iravukkuṟi nērum. nērntāḷ talaimakaṟku niṉṉāṭṭār eṉṉa pūviṉar, eṉṉa cāntiṉar, eṉṉa marattiṉ kīḻ viḷaiyāṭuvar eṉṉum. atu kēṭṭut talaimakaḷ muṉṉellām eṉakku maṟuttāḷaṉṟē, maṟuttāḷ itu colliṟṟu, oru kāraṇam nōkki eṉa uṇarntu, tāṉum niṉṉāṭṭār eṉṉa pūviṉar, eṉṉa cāntiṉar, eṉṉa marattiṉkīḻ viḷaiyāṭuvatu eṉṉum, eṉṟakkāl tōḻi, yām kāntaḷum vēṅkaiyum cūṭutum; cantaṉac cāntu pūcutum; piṇṭi marattiṉ kīḻ viḷaiyāṭuvatu eṉṉum. kuṟiñci nilamākaliṉ ivvakaic collum allā nilattukkum avaṟṟukkut takkavāṟu collum.
	(ā) pakaṟkuṟi tāṉē ikappiṉum varaiyār	(iṟaiyaṉār 20)
	(i) iravuk kuṟiyē ilvarai yikavātu	(iṟaiyaṉār 21)
12.	Tree Worship and Ophiolatry - G. Subramania Pillai, M.A., B.L., P. 2-3.
13.	“Extensive researches pursued after the excavation made at Mohenjo - Daro and Harappa having firmly established this fact that the Tamil race was occupying the whole of India from the Himalayas down to Cape Co morin in Pre - Aryan days. Sir. John Marshall has observed that tree worship was essensially a characteristic of the Pre - Aryan, not of the Aryan population and so the tree spirit has loomed far more important in pre - historic days among the people who originated this worship that it did later in an Aryanized India where Tree - worship inevitably became subordinated to other cults alien or seme - alien.”

4. aṇikalaṉkaḷum 
avaṟṟiṉ varalāṟum
aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ peyart tōṟṟam
aṇikalaṉ eṉpataṟku āparaṇam. nakai, taṭaiyam, paṇi, alaṅkāram eṉṟu pala poruḷkaḷ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ātit tamiḻarkaḷ mikat toṉmaiyāṉa kālattil vēṭṭuva vāḻkkaiyil pāmpaik kaḻuttilum kaiyilum talaimītum cuṟṟikkoṇṭu āṭippāṭi vantuḷḷārkaḷ. itai avarkaḷ aṇikalaṉkaḷ pōlavum matittirukkalām. ataṉāl tamiḻarkaḷiṉ ātip peṟṟōrkaḷāyum teyvaṅkaḷākavum pōṟṟap peṟum koṟṟa vaiyum civaperumāṉum pāmpai aṇikalaṉkaḷākap pūṇṭiruk kiṟārkaḷ pōlum. koṟṟavaiyum avaḷ kaṇavaṉākiya civaperu māṉum paṇṭaik kālattaip piratipalikkum paḻam perum teyva mākap pāmpu aṇikalaṉkaḷuṭaṉ iṉṟu kāṭci aḷittu varukiṉ ṟārkaḷ. piṟkālattil makkaḷ tātup poruḷkaḷaik kaṇṭu piṭittup poṉ veḷḷi aṇikalaṉkaḷaic ceytu aṇintirunta kālattilkūṭa aṇikalaṉkaṭkup paṇi eṉṟu peyar kūṟappaṭṭu vantuḷḷatu. paṇi eṉṉum patattiṟku pāmpu eṉpatu oru poruḷ. iṉṟum tamiḻarkaḷ taṅkaḷ teyvamākiya koṟṟavaikkum civaperumāṉukkum carppakuṇṭalam carppakaṅkaṇam pōṉṟa pāmpu vaṭiva aṇikaḷ ceytu pūṭṭi makiḻntu varukiṉṟārkaḷ.
aṇiyap paṭuvatāl ataṟku aṇikalaṉ eṉṟa peyar eḻuntatu. pāmpu, nakaiyāka eḻuntatāl aṇikalaṉkaḷukku paṇi (pāmpu) eṉṟu peyar vantatu. pūvarumpu aṇiyap peṟṟatāl pūvarumpiṉ peyarākiya nakaiyum aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ peyarāka amaintatu. taṭaiyiṉiṉṟu taṭaiyam eṉṟa peyar aṇikalaṉukku vantatu. muṟkālattil tamiḻ makkaḷukku pēy, picācu, nōy noṭikaḷ mutaliyavaikaḷukku atika accam. atait taṭai ceyya kaiyilum kālilum iṭuppilum oru nūl kaṭṭuvar. itaik kaṭṭi ṉāl nōy aṇukātu, pēy neruṅkātu eṉṟu periyōrkaḷ kūṟi vantaṉar. avvāṟē periyōrkaḷ aḷitta nūṟkaḷaik kaṭṭi makkaḷ accam aṟṟiruntaṉar.
iṉṟu namatu nāṭṭil naṭaipeṟum tirumaṇattiṉ pōtu kaiyil kurukkaḷ oru nūlaik kaṭṭuvār. atu kāppuk kaṭṭutal eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. tirumaṇak kālattil nōy aṇukāmalum pēy picācukaḷ neruṅkāmalum iruppataṟkē inta nūl kāppākak kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. piṟkālattil inta nūl poṉṉāl ceyyap peṟṟuk kaiyil kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. piṟkālattil kāppu oru aṇi kalaṉāka māṟiyatu. veḷḷi poṉ mutaliya ulōkaṅkaḷil palvēṟu vaṭivil kāppu ceyyap peṟṟu makkaḷāl aṇintu varappeṟṟatu. atē pōl muṟkālattil kaṭṭap peṟṟa taṭai eṉṟa nūlum taṭaiyam eṉṟa peyaril nakaikaḷaip paṟṟik koṇṭatu. vēṟu māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil illāviṭṭālum nellai, kumari māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil pēccu vaḻakkil taṭaiyam iruntē varukiṉṟatu. poṟ kollarkaḷum nakaikaḷait taṭaiyam eṉṟu aḻaittu varukiṉṟaṉar.
kaiyil kāppuk kaṭṭiyatu pōl kālilum muṟkāla makkaḷ taṭai kaṭṭi vantaṉar. attaṭai piṟkālattil veḷḷi aṇiyāka māṟit taṇṭaiyāyiṟṟu. aim poṉṉāl kālil taṇṭaikaḷ ceytu aṇintāl pēy piṇikaḷ aṇukā eṉṟu muṟkāla makkaḷ nampit taṇṭaikaḷ ceytu aṇintu vantaṉar. atēpōl kaḻut til taṭaiyāyk kaṭṭappaṭṭa nūlē piṟkālattil tāliyāyiṟṟu. ilakkiyaṅkaḷil nūl iḻantāḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭāl tāli iḻantāḷ - atāvatu kaimpeṇṇāṉāḷ eṉpatu poruḷ.
kuḻantaikaḷukku aimpaṭait tāli - atāvatu tirumāliṉ aintu paṭaik kalaṉkaḷai oru tāḷ allatu takaṭṭil eḻuti nūlil kōttu kaḻuttil aṇintāl pīṭaikaḷ aṇukā eṉṟu muṟkālat tamiḻarkaḷ aimpaṭait tāliyai aṇivittu vantaṉar. itaṉāl aimpaṭaittālikkum nūl eṉṟu peyar eḻuntatu.
ātiyil aṇikalaṉkaḷ malarākat tōṉṟi appāl nūl vaṭivil kāppākavum taṭaiyākavum tāliyākavum aimpaṭait tāliyākavum piṉṉar veṇmaiyāṉa caṅkukaḷum cōvikaḷum kal uruṇṭaikaḷum palvēṟu vaṇṇa maṇ uruṇṭaikaḷum aṇi kalaṉkaḷāka uruppeṟṟaṉa. veṇmaiyāṉa caṅku vaḷaiyalkaḷum caṅkut tōṭukaḷum caṅku mōtiraṅkaḷum muttumālaikaḷum pavaḷamālaikaḷum aṇikalaṉkaḷāka uruppeṟṟaṉa.
malar aṇiyum vaḻakkam kuṟiñci nilattilum, aimpaṭait tāli aṇiyum vaḻakkam mullai nilattilum vaḷai, cōvi, muttu, pavaḷam aṇiyum vaḻakkam neytal nilattilum marutaṅkāy mālai, miḷakumālai, kottamalli mālai, maṇṇāṟ ceyta uruṇṭai maṇikaḷ pōṉṟavaikaḷum  paḷiṅkupōṉṟa pala vaṇṇak kaṟkaḷ kōtta mālaikaḷum kāppu, taṭai, tāli pōṉṟa nūl aṇikaḷ vaḻakkam maruta nilattilum arumpiyirukkalām eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭu kiṟatu. oru nilattil arumpiya oru paḻakkam piṟa nilattiṟkum paravuvatuṇṭu. tiruñāṉacampantar, veḷḷai vaḷai cōra eṉ uḷḷam kavar kaḷvaṉ, eṉṟu kūṟuvataṉmūlam 6ām nūṟṟāṇ ṭiṟku muṉpē tamiḻakattil caṅkuvaḷai aṇiyum  vaḻakkam iruntatu eṉṟu terikiṟatu. caṅkakālap pulavarākiya nakkīraṉār tammaic caṅkaṟuppa teṅkaḷ kulam eṉṟu kūṟuvataṉ mūlam ki.mu. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṉpē tamiḻnāṭṭil caṅku vaḷaikaḷum caṅku mōtiraṅkaḷ pōṉṟavaikaḷum aṇiyappaṭṭu vantaṉa eṉṟu terikiṟatu. atōṭu caṅkukaḷ aṟukkum toḻil iyaṟṟivarum oru iṉattavar tamiḻakattil iruntu vantaṉar eṉṟum terikiṟatu. pāṇṭiyarkaḷiṉ tuṟaimukappaṭṭiṉamāy  viḷaṅkiya koṟkaiyil caṅkaṟukkum toḻil peritāka naṭaipeṟṟu vantatāka aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. iṉṟum koṟkaiyil akaḻntu pārttāl aṟukkappaṭṭa caṅkukaḷiṉ tuṇṭu tuṇukkukaḷ ērāḷamākak kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, muṟkālattil ceyyappaṭṭa caṅku aṇikaḷ cila kiṭaittaṉa eṉṟum intiya aracāṅka mīṉ toḻil tuṟai iyakkunarāyirunta ṭākṭar kōrṉal eṉpavar  intiyāviṉ puṉitac caṅkukaḷ eṉṟa nūlil eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. oru kālattil tamiḻakattil vāḻnta  ellā nila makkaḷum caṅkiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa vaḷai, mōtiram kātaṇi mutaliyavaikaḷai aṇintu vantuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu naṉku terikiṟatu. vaṭa intiyāvil periya celva makaḷirum caṅku vaḷaikaḷaiyum mōtiraṅkaḷaiyum iṉṟum aṇintu varukiṉṟārkaḷ. vaṅkam, kucarat mānilaṅkaḷiliruntu varum peṇkaḷiṟ palar, kai niṟaiyac caṅku valaiyalkaḷum viralil caṅku mōtiraṅkaḷum aṇintu varukiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ caṅku vaḷai aṇivatu oru matac caṭaṅku pōl eṇṇi varukiṉṟārkaḷ.
ātikāla aṇikalaṉkaḷ
ātikālattil mutaṉ mutalāka aṇikalaṉkaḷaik kaṇṭa varkaḷ kuṟiñci nila makkaḷēyāvar. viḷakkamākak kūṟuvatāṉāl aṇi ceyyum vaḻakkam mutaṉ mutalāka kuṟiñci nilap peṇka ḷiṭaiyē tāṉ malarntatu. paḻaṅ kālattilē avarkaḷ aḻakuk kalaiyil ārvaṅ koṇṭu viṭṭaṉar. malarkaḷait talaiyilum kaḻuttilum aṇiyat talaippaṭṭaṉar. nāriṟ kōttu mālaiyākak kaṭṭiyum aṇintu vantaṉar. arumpukaḷaiyum iḷantaḷirkaḷaiyum ilaikaḷaiyum koṭikaḷaiyum talai, kaḻuttu, kātu, kaikaḷ iṭuppup pōṉṟa uṟuppukkaḷilellām aṇintu vantaṉar. nāḷaṭaivil pūkkaḷaip palvēṟu vitamāka caramākavum mālaiyāka vum ceṇṭākavum pūvāṭaiyākavum ilaikaḷait taḷirāṭai yākavum kaṭṭum kalaiyil kaitērntu viḷaṅkiṉar.
pūttoṭukkum kalai vaḷarcciyaṭaintatuṭaṉ nillātu avarkaḷ ciṟu kāy, koṭṭai, paḻamkaḷaiyum kōttu ākiyavai aṇiya muṟpaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ aḻakuk kalaiyai naṉku uṇarntu ataic cuvaikka muṟpaṭṭatum veḷḷai civappākiya vaṇṇak kuṉṟi maṇikaḷaik kōttu malaiyāka aṇintu vantaṉar. civappuk kuṉṟimaṇiyil nalla kaṟuppu vaṇṇam naṭuvē oḷirvatu mika aḻakāka iruntatu. appāl avarkaḷ kaḻaṟcikkoṭṭai, nellikkāy, miḷakukkāy kottamalli pōṉṟavaikaḷaik kōttu mālaikaḷākavum kātaṇiyākavum puya aṇikaḷākavum kaiyaṇiyākavum iṭuppaṇiyākavum kāl aṇiyākavum aṇintu vantaṉar. ivvaḻakkam ellā nilaṅkaḷilum paraviyatu. marutam, neytal mutaliya nilaṅkaḷil vāḻpavarkaḷ āṅkāṅkuk kiṭaikkum poruḷkaḷaik koṇṭu aṇikaḷākac ceytu aṇintu vantā lum malar aṇikaḷum kuṉṟimaṇi, nellikkāy pōṉṟa aṇi kaḷum mikavum kavarcciyaḷittu vantaṉa.
aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟil ulōka uṟpattiyāṉatu oru putiya cakāptam eḻuvataṟku oru ārampa nilaiyāka iruntatu. vēṭṭaip poruḷkaḷāl aṇi ceyyappaṭṭa uṭampai, ulōkap poruḷkaḷ eḻuntatum avaṟṟuḷ oppaṉai ceyya vēṇṭum eṉṟa eṇṇam piṟantatu. āṇum peṇṇum taṅkaḷ kai, kāl pakutikaḷaiyum kaḻuttaiyum, ulōkaṅkaḷāl uruvākkap peṟṟa kaḻuttaṇikaḷālum (mālaikaḷālum) kātaṇikaḷālum araiyai araippaṭṭiyālum oppaṉai ceyya muṉ vantaṉar. muṉṉar iṟakkaikaḷum ciṟu kuccukaḷum vaikkōlum iṭam peṟṟirunta mūkku, kātu, utaṭu mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa tuvāraṅkaḷ  ulōkattālāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷāl nirampiṉa.
āppirikka nāṭṭu pōṅkō eṉṉum pakutiyil vāḻnta aḻakiya kāṭṭu iṉak kārikaiyarkaḷ, mūkkil mutaṉ mutalāka irumpu vaḷaiyattai aṇintu vantaṉar. annāṭṭiluḷḷa ceṉi kāmpi iṉappeṇkaḷ kūṭa inta irumpu vaḷaiyaṅkaḷaiyē virumpiyaṇintaṉar. pōṅkō nāṭṭu āppirikkap peṇkaḷ ovvoruvarum taṅkaḷ kātukaḷil kiṭṭattaṭṭa paṉṉiraṇṭu tuḷaikaḷ iṭṭiruppar. atil irumpu vaḷaiyaṅkaḷai māṭṭik koḷvārkaḷ. ivvāṟu avviṉap peṇkaḷ ovvoruvarum uṭampil nakai aṇivataṟkeṉṟu ceytu koṇṭa tuvāraṅkaḷ nūṟṟukku mēṟpaṭṭaṉavākum. itu avarkaḷ uyir vāḻvataṟku vēṭṭaiyāṭa muṟpaṭṭa ārampakāla vaḻakkamākum. uṟpatti cātaṉaṅkaḷ vaḷarcci peṟṟu uṇavup poruḷkaḷ malinta putiya cakāptattilkūṭa avarkaḷiṉ inta nilai māṟavillai. māṟṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟu avarkaḷ eṇṇiyatumillai. mēlum itu taṅkaḷ paḻamperum paṇpāṭāka irukkiṟatu eṉṟum itil pala tattuvaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum itaṉāl pala nōy akalkiṟatu eṉṟum kūṟi taṅkaḷ aṟiviṉ tiṟattaik kāṭṭukiṉṟārkaḷ.
makaḷir ātikālattil aṇintirunta nakaikaḷiṉiṉṟu māṟutal ceyya vēṇṭiya iṉṟiyamaiyāta cūḻnilai eḻuntatu. intac cūḻnilai aṭikkaṭi eḻuntu māṟutalai uṇṭākkiyatu. āṉāl āṇkaḷ atai virumpavillai. pūvaiyarkaḷ putiya cakāptattiṟ kēṟpa māṟutalkaḷai nāṭumpoḻutu āṇkaḷāl atu aṭātu eṉṟu atika nāḷ taṭuttu niṟutta muṭiyavillai. āṇ makkaḷ ātiyil aṇintirunta aṇikalaṉkaḷāṉa iṟakukaḷum elumpukaḷum nakaṅkaḷum kātilum mūkkilum utaṭṭilum iṭam peṟṟiruntaṉa allavā? anta aṇikalaṉkaḷ āṇ makkaḷiṉ vēṭṭaiyāṭiya tiṟaṉiṉ niṉaivuk kuṟiyāka avarkaḷiṉ avayavaṅkaḷil iṭam peṟṟaṉa. peṇkaḷ akkālattil vēṭṭaiyāṭun toḻilil īṭupaṭavillai. eṉṟālum avarkaḷiṉ aṉutāpattaip peṟuvataṟ kāka āṇmakaṉ avarkaḷiṉ aṅkaṅkaḷil aṇikalaṉkaḷai iṭam peṟac ceytāṉ. āṉāl peṇkaḷ aṇinta ulōkattālāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷ āṇiṉ vēṭṭaiyāṭun tiṟaṉai viḷakkuvaṉavāka illai. atu āṇkaḷiṉ celvac ciṟappaik kāṭṭuvaṉavāka iruntaṉa. ataṟkāka āṇ, peṇkaḷiṉ aṅkaṅkaḷil eṇṇaṟṟa nakaikaḷaip pūṭṭa muṉvantāṉ. nāḷaṭaivil oruvaṉuṭaiya maṉaivi avaṉuṭaiya karuvūlaṅkaḷai vaikkum peṭṭiyāka eṇṇappaṭṭāḷ. iṟutiyākap peṇkaḷ, āṇkaḷiṉ uṭaimai yāka māṟṟap paṭṭaṉar.
kāṭukaḷil vāḻnta muṟkāla makkaḷ taṅkaḷukkuk kiṭaitta cempu, veṇkalat tātupporuḷkaḷaiyellām urukkit tam maṉaivimārkaḷukku aṇikalaṉkaḷākac ceytu avaikaḷai avarkaḷukku aṇivittu akamakiḻntu vantaṉar. paḻaṅkālak kāṭṭu maṉitaṉ 10, 20, 30, 40 ēṉ nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa maṉaivikaḷaik kūṭa vaittiruntāṉ. itaṟkumēl maṟu maṉaiyāṭṭikaḷaiyum vait tiruntāṉ. tacarataṉ 60,000 maṉaivikaḷaip peṟṟiruntāṉ. kiṟitava maṟai kūṟum cālamōṉ ñāṉi 700 maṉaiyāṭṭikaḷaiyum 300 maṟumaṉaiyāṭṭikaḷaiyum peṟṟiruntār. pala mulim maṉṉarkaḷ 4 maṉaivikaḷaiyum 400 maṟumaṉaiyāṭṭikaḷai (curiyatkaḷaiyum) vaittiruntaṉar. itē pōl āppirikkarkaḷ pala maṉaivikaḷai vaittiruppatu carva cātāraṇa campavamākum. oru āppirikkak kāṭṭup paḻaṅkāla maṉitaṉ oruvaṉ nāṟpatukku mēṟpaṭṭa maṉaivikaḷaip peṟṟiruntāṉ. ataip paṟṟi avaṉ aṭikkaṭi perumaiyākavum pēcik koḷvāṉ. avaṉ ovvoru maṉai vikkum taṉittaṉiyākap pala pittaḷai nakaikaḷ ceytu avarkaḷukku aṇivittiruntāṉ. ōr āppirikka nīkirōvarkaḷiṉ  talaiva ṉuṭaiya maṉaivimārkaḷ cākumvarai kuṟaintatu 800 irāttal pittaḷaik kaḻuttaṇikaḷaic cumantu varuvārkaḷām. avaṉuṭaiya āṟu peṇ makkaḷ 120 irāttal pittaḷai nakaikaḷaiyum avaṉuṭaiya piriyamāṉa aṭimaip peṇ 200 irāttal pittaḷai nakaikaḷaiyum avaṉuṭaiya maṉaivikaḷum peṇ makkaḷum ovvoruvar 6 irāttal kaiyaṇikaḷaiyum aṇintiruntārkaḷ. āka mottam avaṉiṭam 1396 irāttal pittaḷai aṇikalaṉkaḷ iruntu vantatām.
avaṉiyil arumpiya cila ākkak kūṟukaḷiṉ celvākkiṉ payaṉāka arivaiyarkaḷiṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷil apiviruttiyum māṟṟamum eḻuntaṉa. nām itait toṉmaiyāṉa kālattiluḷḷa poruḷ uṟpatti cātaṉaṅkaḷil eḻunta ciṟu māṟṟaṅkaḷiṉ payaṉākat tuḷirtta ceyalkaḷiṉ oru pakuti eṉpatai niṉaiviṟ koḷḷa vēṇṭum. oru pakuti, maṉita iyaṟkaiyiṉ nilaiyāṉa cāyalkaḷum poruḷātārattiṉ nēraṭiyāṉa cāyalkaḷum poruḷātārattiṉ nēraṭiyāṉa celvākkiṟku vaḻiviṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum kūṟalām. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka āṇkaḷ taṅkaḷuṭaiya peṇkaḷiṉ mūlam celvac ciṟappaik kāṭṭutal taṅkaḷuṭaiya paṇap perumaiyai aṟivikkum akaṅkārac ceyalāka iruntatu. iṉṉum avarkaḷ tamatu āṉma tattuvat taṉmaikaḷaiyum piṟavaḻi kaḷil taṅkaḷait tāṅkaḷē veḷippaṭuttik koṇṭaṉar eṉṟum eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.
ulōkap poruḷkaḷāl uruvākkappaṭṭa aṇikalaṉkaḷ mītu peṇ makkaḷukkup pēravā, maṉitaṉ ulōkap poruḷkaḷai uṟpatti ceyya ārampitta piṉṉartāṉ eḻuntuḷḷatu eṉṟu nām iṅku meyppikka vēṇṭiyatu avaciyam iṉṟu. muṟkālattil maṉitaṉuṭaiya ācai, peritum, taṉṉuṭaiya celvap perukkai ulakiṟku kāṭṭik koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟu iruntatākat teri kiṟatu. celvam taṉṉaic ciṟappittuk kāṭṭum vaḻiyil makkaḷai iḻuttuc ceṉṟatu atu celvavāṉ, taṉṉuṭaiya maṉaivimār kaḷum maṟumaṉaiyāṭṭikaḷum ulōkap poruḷkaḷāl aṇi ceyyappaṭṭiruppatai ulakiṟku uṇartta vēṇṭum eṉṟu tūṇṭiyatu. itaṉai viḷakka nām pala eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷaik kāṭṭa muṭiyum. ivvaḷavu nakaikaḷaip peṇkaḷaippōl āṇkaḷum aṇintiruntārkaḷ eṉṟu meyppikka maṉitaṉ pala cāṉṟukaḷaik kāṭṭa muṭiyum; nīṅkaḷ cintaṉai ceyya vēṇṭām. mutaṉ mutalāka kaikaḷilum kālkaḷilum ulōka aṇikaḷ aṇiyap paṭṭaṉa eṉṟu mēlē kūṟiyavaikaḷai maṟuttu nīṅkaḷ eḻutalām. eṅkāvatu kāṇappaṭum oṉṟiraṇṭu vitivilakkukaḷai eṭuttuk kāṭṭi vivātikkalām. ataic ciṟappāka eṭuttuk koṇṭu evarum taṅkaḷ mūḷaiyaik kuḻappik koṇṭirukka vēṇṭām. ārampat til eḻunta nōkkam piṟkālattil māṟik kaikaḷum kālkaḷum aḻakāka iruppatākat tōṉṟiyapiṉ  atai mēlum aḻakuṟac ceyya vēṇṭum eṉṟa ārvattil aṇikaḷ pūṇappaṭṭirukka lām. itu, oru cila iṭaṅkaḷil eḻuntirukkalām eṉpatil evarukkum karuttu vēṟupāṭu irukkalām. allatu illāmalum irukkalām.
nāṭṭiyam tamiḻ makkaḷōṭu mikat toṉmaiyāṉa kālattilē tōṉṟi apivirutti aṭaintuḷḷatu. makkaḷ tam uṇavu uṟpattikkāka mikunta ciramappaṭṭu, tam muyaṟciyil perum veṟṟiyīṭṭiyatum eḻum niṟaivāṉa makiḻccip perukkāl arumpum āṭṭamē  nāṭṭiyamāka malarntatu eṉṟu nām muṉṉar viḷakkiyuḷḷōm. nāṭṭiyattil ilayap piramāṇam mika mukkiyam eṉpatum namakkut teriyum. āṭṭattil kaikaḷum (ataṅkaḷum) pātak kiramaṅkaḷum mika mukkiyamāṉavaikaḷākum. mikap paḻaṅkāla nāṭṭiyaṅkaḷil pūkkaḷum mālaikaḷumkūṭa mukkiyattuvam peṟṟiruntaṉa. nāṭṭiyattil, mikap piṟkālattil ulōkaṅkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu uracuvatāl eḻum oli mukkiyamāṉatāka irun tatu. kaikaḷilum kālkaḷilum aṇiyappaṭṭuḷḷa aṇikalaṉkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu mōti allatu kuluṅki eḻum aṇikaḷiṉ ōcai kaḷum maṇikaḷiṉ olikaḷum nāṭṭiyattaic ciṟappippatāka eṇṇappaṭṭatu. tamiḻar nāṭṭiyattil aṇikaḷukku mukkiyat tuvam aḷikkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu itaṉāl tāṉ eṉpatai nām maṟantu viṭuvataṟkillai. kālil kaṭṭiya cataṅkaikaḷiṉ ōcaiyum kāl viralkaḷil aṇintuḷḷa meṭṭi, miñci mutaliyavaikaḷil eḻum ōcaiyum iṉpam payappaṉavāy irukkiṉṟaṉa. itaṟkākavē ivvaṇikaḷ ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ulōka aṇikaḷ tōṉṟiya piṉṉarē muṟkālattil peṇkaḷ aṇintu vanta tōlāṭaikaḷum elumpu aṇikaḷum caṅku aṇikaḷum maṟaiyat toṭaṅkiṉa. muṉ ṉuḷḷa tōlāṭaikaḷum nakam, pal, kompu, malar, koṭṭai, elumpu, caṅku aṇikaḷ aṉaittaiyumviṭa ulōkattāl ceyyap paṭṭa aṇikalaṉkaḷ niṟattālum oliyālum kavarcciyai nalkiṉa. kaviṉpeṟa viḷaṅkiṉa. kaṇkaḷukkum cevikaḷukkum karuttiṟkum viruntāka iruntaṉa. āṉāl muṟkālattilirunta irumpu mōtiraṅ kaḷum vaḷaikaḷum oru cila iṭaṅkaḷil inta irupatām nūṟṟāṇ ṭiṉ piṟpakutivarai kāṭci aḷittu varukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻakattil cila tāḻttappaṭṭa makkaḷiṭam iṉṟum irumpu īyam pittaḷai veḷḷi cempu aṇikaḷ iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa.
tamiḻakattiṉ nāṭōṭi makkaḷiṉ nāṭṭiyattilaṉṟi, naṉi ciṟanta nākarikam peṟṟa makkaḷiṉ nāṭṭiyamāka navilappeṟum parata nāṭṭiyattilum palavita apinayak kaikaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaikaḷil pāmpup paṭakkai (carppacīrṣa atam), māṉtalaikku (mirukacīrṣa atam), pulikkai (viyākkirama atam), arimukakkai (cimmamuka atam), tēṉīk kai (piramara atam), kōḻik koṇṭaik kai (tāmpiracūṭa atam), aṉṉamukak kai (amcāciya atam), aṉṉattiṉ iṟakkaik kai (amcapaṭca atam), karimukakkai (kajamuka atam) āmaik kai (kūrma atam), tavaḷaik kai (maṇṭūka atam) iṭampuri caṅkukkai (caṅka atam) puṟākkai (kapōta atam), naṇṭuk kai (kaṟkaṭaka atam), mīṉ kai (matsaya atam), paṉṟikkai (varāka atam), yāṉaik kompuk kai (kajatanta atam), mayil kai (mayūra atam), kattirik kōl alakuk kai (kattirimuka atam), viḷāmpaḻak kai (kapitta atam), ūcik kai (cūci atam), mūvilaic cūlakkai (tiricūla atam), ampuk kai (pāṇa atam), ampumukak kai (cilimuka atam), kayiṟuk kai (pāca atam), kalayakkai (kalaca atam), arumpuk kai (mukuḷa atam), malarnta tāmaraik kai (alarpatuma atam), pūk koyyum kai (kāṅkula atam), pūkkūṭaik kai (puṣpapuṭa atam), kaṅkaṇakkai (kaṭakāmuka atam) pōṉṟak kaikaḷ mukkiyamāṉa kaikaḷ ākum. apinayak kaikaḷil perumpālāṉa kaikaḷ paṇṭaiyat tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ uṇavup poruṭkaḷāka irunta paṟavaikaḷaiyum, vilaṅkukaḷiṉ uṟuppukaḷaiyum, vilaṅku kaḷai vēṭṭaiyāṭuvataṟkuriya kompu, ampu, kayiṟu mutaliyavai kaḷaiyum kuṟippatāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. oru cila kaikaḷ paḻaṅkaḷaiyum malarkaḷaiyum kuṟippiṭuvatāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. paṇṭaiyat tamiḻarkaḷ taṅkaḷiṉ iṉṟiyamaiyāta uṇavup poruṭkaḷaip peṟṟatum makiḻcciyāl āṭiṉar. anta makiḻccikkuriya āṭṭattil paḻaṅ kaḷaiyum, koṭṭaikaḷaiyum taṅkaḷiṉ veṟṟic ciṉṉamāka uṭalil aṇintu koṇṭaṉar. mayiliṟakukaḷait talaiyiṟ cūṭiyum pūkkaḷait talaiyilum kaḻuttilum aṇintum koṭikaḷai kaikaḷil kaṅkaṇamākak kaṭṭiyum āṭiyum pāṭiyum vantaṉar. makkaḷiṉ nāṭṭiyam avarkaḷ tam uṇavup poruṭkaḷai niṟai vākap peṟṟatiṉ payaṉāka eḻunta ekkaḷippil arumpiyatu. icai yum inta iṉpa āṭṭattōṭu tuḷirttatutāṉ. uṇavup poruḷ kaḷākiya paḻaṅkaḷum koṭṭaikaḷum kāykaḷum ātiyil nāṭṭi yattaic ciṟappikkum aṇikaḷāy arumpiṉa. uṇavup poruṭ kaḷāṉa ilaikaḷum taḷirkaḷum avarkaḷiṉ āṭaikaḷāy amaintaṉa. kaṇṇaiyum mūkkaiyum karuttaiyum īrtta, vaṇṇam vāynta kiṇṇam pōṉṟa naṟumaṇa malar aṇikaḷum koṭiyaṇi kaḷum itaṟku muṉṉarē tōṉṟiyavai eṉṟālum avai maṟai yātu ivaṟṟōṭu kāṭci aḷittē vantaṉa. namatu nāṭṭil eḻunta nāṭṭiya aṇikaḷum āṭaikaḷum namatu muṉṉōrkaḷ pōtiya uṇavaic cēkarittu uṇṭu makiḻnta veṟṟik koṇṭāṭṭattil eḻuntavaikaḷē eṉṟu maṉita nākarika ārāycciyil talaiciṟanta mēṉāṭṭaṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. atai nām eḷitil taḷḷi viṭuvataṟkillai. atil uṇmai uḷḷatu. uṇavu uṟpatti kuṟiñci nilattil vāḻnta makkaḷukku uyir nāṭiyāka iruntatu. avarkaḷ kaṟkālattil vāḻntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ uḻavut toḻilai uṇarārkaḷ. uḻutu payiriṭṭu uṇavu uṟpatti ceyya vēṇṭum eṉṟa uṇarvē avarkaḷ uḷḷattil untavillai. iyaṟkaiyāy malaikaḷil uḷḷa maraṅkaḷil kiṭaikkum kāykaṉikaḷaiyum, vēṭṭaiyil kiṭaikkum vilaṅku paṟavai mutaliyavaikaḷiṉ ūṉaiyum pucittu avarkaḷ kālaṅkaḻittu vantaṉar. avarkaḷ kāykaṉikaḷai kallāl vīḻttavum kalleṭuttu vīci vilaṅkukaḷai yum paṟavaikaḷaiyum koṉṟu tiṉṉa vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṭa avarkaḷ piṟkālattiltāṉ aṟintārkaḷ. ataṟkum piṉṉartāṉ villaikkaṇṭārkaḷ. avarkaḷ neruppaip payaṉpaṭuttavum aṟiyārkaḷ. avarkaḷ piṟkālattilē ūṉaiyum kāy kaṉikaḷai yum vēkavaittuc cāppiṭat terintaṉar. kaṟkālam māṟi ulōka kālam eḻum poḻutē avarkaḷ arivāḷ maṇaiyaiyum vāḷai yum kattiyaiyum kaṇṭārkaḷ. avarkaḷ kāykaṉikaḷ niṟainta kuṟiñci nilattil, vilaṅkukaḷum paṟavaikaḷum niṟainta malaikaḷil tāṉ vāḻntārkaḷ. aṅku taṉi uṭaimai illai; ellām potu uṭaimaitāṉ. eṉṟālum cila kālaṅkaḷil avarkaḷ uṇṇu vataṟkuk kāykaṉiyum ūṉum kiṭaiyātu pala nāṭkaḷ tavittaṉar innilaiyilum avarkaḷukkut tiṭīreṉṟu vēṭṭaip poruḷ kaḷum kāykaṉikaḷum koṭṭaikaḷum kiṭaittuviṭum. appoḻutu iṉpam poṅkip perukkeṭuttōṭum. kāy kaṉikaḷai vēṇṭiya maṭṭum tiṉṟu paciyait taṇippārkaḷ. eñciya kāykaṉikaḷaiyum koṭṭaikaḷaiyum taḷirkaḷaiyum ilaikaḷaiyum  aṇintu āṭippāṭi makiḻvārkaḷ. ituvē namatu ārampak kāla aṇivakai kaḷiṉ piṟappu varalāṟākum.
kuṟippukaḷ
1.	‘The sacred chank of India - James Hornell. F.L.S. (Madras) 1914 : P. 3
2.	“This fishery is the only one that is carried on systematically and with a definite organization. As detailed else where (P. 43) references in Tamil classics make it clear that this fishery was being prosecuted with vigour under Pandyan rule as long as 1,800 years ago; in those days the head - quarters was at Korkai, an important city of traders, Jewerllers, Pearl - fishers and Chank - divers.”
	Ibid . 1. The Antiquity of the Industry P. 42
	“Reference to ancient Tamil classics furnishes evidence scanty but conclusive of the existence of an important chank cutting industry in the ancient Pandyan kingdom in the early centuries of the christian era. Similar evidence is also extant of a widespread use of carved and ornamenented chank bangles in formor days by the women of Pandyan Country.”.
2.	“The Tamil name kulai and todu now applied for ear ornaments made of gold or even stones sugest the very ancient and pre - historic times when leaves and flower were worn. The habit of small girls wearing a leaf shaped pendent made of silver and gold and of women wearing an ornament called mekalai round their waist is reminiscent of the times when they wore only leaves to cover their nakednes” - Ancient Jaffna. C. Rasanaygan. P. 169
3.	Marg. (Bombay) Vol XIII No. 1 dated dece. 1959 P. 56
