5. cintuveḷi nākarikam
mokañcatārō aṇikalaṉkaḷ
mokañcatārōvil pala muttiraikaḷum cuṇṇāmpu, veṇkalam ākivaikaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa paṭimaṅkaḷum pāṉai caṭṭikaḷum āyutaṅkaḷum cuṭumaṇ ceṅkallāl kaṭṭappeṟṟa pala māṭikaḷaiyuṭaiya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷum kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṅku 5000ām āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ vāḻnta tamiḻarkaḷiṉ muṉ ṉōrkaḷ allatu neruṅkiya uṟaviṉarkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟum paḻam perumakkaḷ poṉ, veḷḷi, cempu, veṇkalam veḷḷaik kaḷi maṇ, tantam, elumpu, kal, navamaṇikaḷ, caṅku, kaḷimaṇ mutaliyavai kaḷaik koṇṭu ceyta eṇṇaṟṟa vitamāṉa ērāḷamāṉa eḻiloḻukum aṇikaḷaic ceytu aṇintu vantuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu terikiṉṟatu. ēḻaikaḷ kaṟuppuk kaḷimaṇ, civappuk kaḷimaṇ, veḷḷaik kaḷimaṇ ākiyavaikaḷaik koṇṭu ciṟu maṇikaḷ pōl ceytu ataṉ naṭuvē tuḷaiyiṭṭu nūlāl kōttu aṇintu vantuḷḷaṉar. cilar cuṭṭakaḷimaṇ maṇikaḷai aṇintu vantuḷḷaṉar. cilar uyarnta vilai matippuḷḷa marakatam, māṇikkam, nīlam pōṉṟa maṇikaḷaip palavitamākat tēyttu merukiṭṭu tuḷaiyiṭṭu nūlil kōttu aṇintuḷḷaṉar. cilar poṉṉālum veḷḷiyālum potintu tuḷaiyiṭṭu nūlil kōttu aṇintu vantuḷḷaṉar. caṅkukaḷālāṉa vaḷaiyalkaḷum aṅkuk kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. paṉṉiṟac calavaik kaṟkaḷ, elumpu, kaḷimaṇ mutaliyavaikaḷāl ceytuḷḷa vaḷaikaḷum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. ivaikaḷ iṉṟaiya āntira makaḷir kālkaḷil aṇintuḷḷa veḷḷik kāl aṇikaḷ pōl kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṅkuḷḷa mālaikaḷil kāṇappaṭum maṇikaḷ cuṇṭaik kāy mutal koṭṭaippākkuvaraiyuḷḷa parumaṉil irukkiṉṟaṉa. ivai taṭṭaiyāyum vaṭṭamāyum nīḷamāyum uḷḷaṉa.
iṅkup pala poṉ koṇṭai ūcikaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. veḷḷik kātaṇikaḷum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. iṅku oru veḷḷip pāttirattil pala kaḻuttaṇikaḷum paḷapaḷappāka oḷi vīcum uyarnta maṇikaḷum poṉṉāṟ ceyta talai nāṭākkaḷum kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
talaiyaṇikaḷ
mokañcatārōvil kiṭaitta patumaikaḷ mūlam aṅkuḷḷa makkaḷ paṇṭaiyat tamiḻ makkaḷaip pōl talaiyil oru nāṭāvai cuṟṟik kaṭṭi vantaṉar eṉṟu terikiṟatu. itu cumēriyariṭamum  iruntu vanta paḻakkamākum. ivai poṉ takaṭṭāl arai aṅkula akalamum patiṉāṟu aṅkula nīḷamum uḷḷaṉa. nuṉi iḻuttuk kaṭṭuvataṟkēṟṟatāy nuṉiyil tuḷaikaḷ iṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. intat tuḷaiyil vēṟu aṇikaḷaik kaṭṭit toṅkaviṭṭum irukka lām. ivvaṇikaḷai āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum aṇintu vantatāka aṟikiṟōm. iṅku āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum talaimayirai vārik koṇṭai iṭuvatum curuṭṭik koḷvatum uṇṭeṉṟu terikiṟatu. talaimītu tamiḻ makkaḷaippōl talaiyaṇikaḷ cila aṇivatum uṇṭu.
talai mayirkaḷaic cuṟṟi kaṭṭik koṇṭālum avai nekiḻntu vīḻntuviṭātirukka iṉṟaiyat tamiḻakap peṇkaḷ pōṉṟu 5000 āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ vāḻnta mokañcatārō makaḷirum koṇṭai ūcikaḷaip payaṉpaṭutti vantatāka aṟiki ṟōm. koṇṭai ūcikaḷ, poṉ, veḷḷi, veṇkalam, tantam, elumpu mutaliya pala poruḷkaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭaṉavāy iruk kiṉṟaṉa. ik koṇṭai ūcikaḷ palvēṟu vakaikaḷākavum iruk kiṉṟaṉa. oru veṇkalak koṇṭai ūci cumār 4¼ aṅkula nīḷam uḷḷatu. ataṉ koṇṭaikaḷil tamiḻnāṭṭuk koṇṭai ūci kaḷaip pōl āṭu, māṭu, māṉ, puṟā pōṉṟa uruvaṅkaḷ iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. oru koṇṭai ūciyil iru māṉ talaikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ammāṉkaḷ vevvēṟu ticaiyai nōkkuva tāka amaintuḷḷaṉa. ataṉ kompukaḷ aḻakāka muṟukkik koṇṭiruppatu pōlat tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. oru ūciyiṉ talaippakuti maṭṭum kiṭaittuḷḷatu. anta ūciyiṉ talaipakkam mūṉṟu kuraṅkukaḷ tōḷkaḷil kaiyai vaittukkoṇṭu kuntiyiruppatu pōl kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉ ūci marattālāṉatupōl kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. vēṟu cila ūcikaḷ talaippuṟam veṇkallāl ceyyappaṭṭatu pōlavum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
nutal aṇikaḷ
arappāvilum mokañcatārōvilum iruntu pala nutal aṇikaḷ - atāvatu neṟṟic cuṭṭikaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. ivai talaiyaṇikaḷiṉiṉṟu neṟṟiyil toṅkum oru vakaip patakkamē yākum. ivai vaṭṭavaṭivil mukkōṇampōl kūmpiyaṉavākac ceyyappaṭṭavai. inta neṟṟic cuṭṭikaḷ iraṇṭu aṅkulac cuṟṟaḷavu uṭaiyaṉa. ivai poṉ, veḷḷi, cempu, pīṅkāṉ mutaliya poruḷkaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivai talai muṭiyil kōttuk kaṭṭuvataṟku ēṟṟavāṟu ceyyappaṭṭut talai nāṭāvil kōppataṟku ēṟṟavāṟu tuḷaiyiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. inta neṟṟic cuṭṭikaḷ mūṉṟu nāṉku cippikaḷai iṇaittuc ceyyap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṅku ērāḷamāṉa kaḷimaṇ patumaikaḷ kiṭait tuḷḷaṉa. ataṉ nutal aṇikaḷaip pōṉṟu ivai kāṇappaṭu vataik koṇṭē ivai neṟṟic cuṭṭikaḷ eṉpatu tīrmāṉikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. inta neṟṟic cuṭṭikaḷ neṟṟiyiliruntu toṭaṅki oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāka vaittu uccantalaivarai kūntalai aḻaku paṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. ivai mēṟpuṟam poṉṉālum aṭippuṟam veḷḷi yālum ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivai perumpālum 1¼ aṅkulam nīḷam uḷḷaṉavākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
iḵtaṉṟi paṭṭaiyākak kāṇappaṭum cila poṟṟakaṭukaḷ arappāvilum mokañcatārōvilum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. ivai neṟṟiyil aṇiyappaṭuvaṉavēyākum; neṟṟippaṭṭam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. inta aṇikaḷaip pōṉṟu neṟṟiyil paṭṭam kaṭṭiya cila patumaikaḷ iṅkuk kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. neṟṟiyil paṭṭam kaṭṭum vaḻakkam tamiḻakattil iṉṟu illai. muṉṉar iruntu vantatu. nellai māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa ātittanallūrp pukai kuḻikaḷil uḷḷa mutumakkaḷ tāḻiyil uḷḷa elumpuk kūṭu kaḷuṭaṉ poṉṉāl, ceyta inta neṟṟip paṭṭaṅkaḷ pala kaṇ ṭeṭukkappaṭṭuc ceṉṉai aracāṅkap paḻam poruṭkāṭci cālaiyil vaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. neṟṟic cuṭṭi aṇiyum vaḻakkam iṉṟum tamiḻakattil uṇṭu. nāṭṭiyamāṭupavarkaḷ inta aṇiyai viṭṭuviṭuvatē illai.
mūkkaṇikaḷ
arappāvilum mokañcatārōvilum kiṭaitta eṇṇi lāppatumaikaḷ oṉṟilkūṭa mūkkaṇikaḷ kāṇappaṭavillai. mēlum mūkkaṇikaḷ aṇiyum vaḻakkam tirāviṭarkaḷiṭam illai. tamiḻakattil uḷḷa cilaikaḷilum cempu, veṇkalap paṭimaṅkaḷi lum mūkkaṇikaḷ kāṇappaṭavillai. eṉavē muṟkālat tamiḻar kaḷiṭam  mūkkaṇikaḷ pūṇum vaḻakkam kiṭaiyātu eṉṟu aṟiñar kaḷāl kūṟappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl mūkkiṉ iru puṟaṅkaḷilum tuḷaiyiṭṭu mūkkuttikaḷ eṉṉum aṇikaḷ poṉṉāl maṭṭu miṉṟi maṇikaḷ patittum  aṇivatum, pala pōli maṇikaḷ patitta mūkkuttikaḷ aṇivatum mūkkiṉ naṭuttaṇṭiṉ naṭuvil tuvāra miṭṭu mūkkiṉ kīḻē atāvatu mēl utaṭṭiṉmītu toṅkum pullākku eṉṟa oru vakai nakai aṇiyum vaḻakkum irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭu ārampa kālam varai iruntatu.
arappāvilum mokañcatārōvilum viḷimpil paṟkaḷ pōṉṟa amaippuṭaiya cakkaram pōṉṟa poṟṟakaṭṭil ceyyap paṭṭa mūkkutti pōṉṟa aṇikaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. kē. eṉ. tīkcit pōṉṟa aṟiñarkaḷ mokañcatārō makkaḷum arappā makkaḷum mūkkaṇikaḷ aṇintataṟku uṟutiyāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭṭavillai eṉṟum ki.pi. 1200kkup piṉ mūkkaṇi aṇiyum vaḻakka muculim kaḷāl intiyāvil pukuttappaṭṭa paḻakkam eṉṟum kūṟiya piṉṉarumkūṭa, pēraṟiñar ci. ār. irāy pōṉṟa aṟiñarkaḷ cintu veḷiyil vāḻnta muntiya kāla makaḷir mūkkaṇikaḷ pūṇṭirun taṉar; avai kātil aṇinta tōṭukaḷuṭaṉ poṟcaṅkiliyāl iru puṟamum iṇaikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār. cintuveḷip pakutiyaic ciṟappāka ārāya intiya aracāṅkam niyamitta aṟiñar ṭākṭar mekkēy avarkaḷ cintuveḷiyil vāḻnta paḻaṅkāla makkaḷ nīlakkal patitta mūkkaṇikaḷai upayōkittu vantaṉar eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār. ivarai ātarittu pāṭrik kārliṭaṉ avarkaḷum eḻutiyuḷḷār. aṟiñar em.e. vāṭ eṉpavarum arappāvil akaḻntu ārāynta aṟiñaruḷ oruvarēyāvar. avarum arappā vil vāḻnta aṇaṅkukaḷ aḻakiya mūkkuttikaḷaip payaṉpaṭutti yuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.
kātaṇikaḷ
cintu veḷiyil kiṭaitta cila kaḷimaṇ patumaikaḷ kātaṇi kaḷuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē akkāla makaḷirkaḷ palavita māṉa kātaṇikaḷ aṇintirukkalām eṉa eṇṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. cila kātaṇikaḷ ciṟu kuḻāykaḷaiyuṭaiya kumiḻ pōṉṟaṉavākavum, cila taṅkaccuruḷ pōlavum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila tamiḻnāṭṭu makaḷir cumār 30 āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ kātukaḷil aṇiyum vaṭṭamāṉa vaḷaiyaṅkaḷ pōṉṟa kaṭukkaṉ mātiri poṉṉāṟ ceyyap paṭṭavaikaḷāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila kātaṇikaḷ tirukāṇi vaittuc ceyyappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟu terikiṉṟaṉa. intak kūmpiya pakutiyil kuḻāy pōṉṟa uruvam vaittu paṟṟavaikkappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. ituvum poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭatēyākum. aṟiñar vāṭ avarkaḷ arappāvil naṭattiya ārāycciyil cila veḷḷik kātaṇikaḷaiyum kaṇṭeṭuttuḷḷār.
kaḻuttaṇikaḷ
1. mokañcatārōvil, oru veḷḷik kalattil putaittu vaikkappaṭṭirunta aṇikalaṉkaḷ cilavaṟṟai ṭākṭar i.cē.em. mekkēy avarkaḷ kaṇṭeṭuttuḷḷār. ivaṟṟai kaṇṭeṭut tatil avar perum makiḻcciyaṭaintār. mālaiyoṉṟu poṉ ṉālum paccai niṟamāṉa uyarnta maṇikaḷāl kōkkappaṭṭatāy aḻakuṭaṉ viḷaṅkukiṟatu. poṉmaṇikaḷ taṭṭaiyākac ceyyap peṟṟu avaṟṟiṉ mītu aḻakuṟak kumiḻkaḷ vaikkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. pīppāy vaṭivamāka kaṇṇāṭi pōṉṟa paccaik kaṟkaḷ kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. oru poṉmaṇiyum aṭuttāṟpōṟ paccaik kallum atai aṭuttup poṉ maṇiyumāka muṟaiyākak kōkkappaṭṭa aḻakiya mālaiyākat tikaḻkiṟatu. immālaiyiṉ naṭuvil ēḻu aḻakiya patakkaṅkaḷ toṅkaviṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ovvoṉṟiṉ naṭuvilum oru vaiṭūriyamum, cūriya kāntak kallum vaittuc ceyyappaṭṭatāka terikiṟatu. immālaiyil oḷirum paccaikkal vaṭaparmāvilum tipēttilum kiṭaikkum kaṟkaḷaip pōṉṟatākak kaviṉ peṟak kāṭci aḷikkiṉṟatu.
2. iḵtaṉṟi maṟṟum iru kaḻuttaṇikaḷāṉa mālaikaḷ mokañcatārōvil akaḻntu kaṇṭeṭukkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. avaikaḷil oru mālaiyil kuḻavi vaṭivattilum, miḷaku pōṉṟa uruṇṭai vaṭivattilum, ceyyappaṭṭa poṉmaṇikaḷ kōkkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṭai iṭaiyē nīlappaḷiṅkuk kallāl ceyyap paṭṭa maṇikaḷum kōkkappaṭṭu eḻiluṭaṉ tikaḻkiṉṟatu. nīlap paḷiṅkuk kalmaṇikaḷ puttam putiyaṉapōl poṉṟāp poli vuṭaṉ paḷapaḷeṉa miṉṉik koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.
maṟṟoru mālai poṉṉāl āṉatu. atu taṭṭaiyākac ceyyap peṟṟa iraṇṭu maṇikaḷai iṇaittup poruttap paṭṭatākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. inta aḻaku mikka aṇivaṭaṅ kaḷiṉ ariya paṇiyiṉ vēlai nuṭpam pōṟṟattakkatākum.
arappāviṉ aḻakiya mālaikaḷ
mokañcatārō paṇpāṭṭiṟku muntiya paṇpāṭu arappaṉ paṇpāṭu. arappāvil arumpiya aṇikalaṉkaḷē mokañca tārō aṇikalaṉkaḷaiviṭat toṉmaiyāṉavai. eṉṟālum nuṭpamāṉa vēlaippāṭukaḷil arappā vēlaippāṭu mokañca tārōviṉataiviṭa evvitattilum kuṟaintaṉavalla. arappāvil uḷḷa orumālai nāṉku caraṅkaḷaiyuṭaiyatāy 240 maṇikaḷ kōkkappaṭṭatāy eḻil mikkatāy ilaṅkukiṉṟatu. maṟṟoru mālai 27 poṉmaṇikaḷai uṭaiyatāyp paṟpala aḻakiya vaṭivaṅ kaḷai uṭaiyatāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ivviraṇṭait tavirttu 50 allatu 60 maṇikaḷ kōtta oru aḻakiya mālaiyum kaṇṭeṭuk kappaṭṭuḷḷatu. inta mālai poṉmaṇikaḷālum navamaṇika ḷālum kōkkappaṭṭatu. itaṉ vēlaippāṭu mika aṟputamāka irukkiṟatu. immālaikaḷil aḻakiya patakkaṅkaḷum cērkkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivai oṉṟu mutal 13 patakkaṅkaḷ varai kōkkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉavāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. poṉmaṇikaḷ aṉṟi, muḻuvatum navamaṇikaḷālāṉa, mālaikaḷum uḷa. mokañca tārōvil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa mālaikaḷaiviṭa arappāvil atikamāṉa mālaikaḷ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
arappāvil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭa pala kaḻuttaṇikaḷ viyattaku vēlaippāṭukaḷ koṇṭavaikaḷāyt tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. cila mālai kaḷil civappu, mañcaḷ, nīlam, vaiṭūriyam, vairam, māṇikkam, amecāṉ eṉṟa oru vakaip paccaik kaṟkaḷuṭaṉ cūriyakāntak kaṟkaḷ paṭikak kaṟkaḷ patittavaikaḷāyum tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. inta maṇikaḷ āpkāṉicuttāṉ, kācumīr, tipēt, parmā piṟa intiya nāṭu kaḷiliruntu koṇṭuvarappaṭṭavaikaḷāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṅkuḷḷa maṇikaḷ nalla vaḻavaḻappākat tēykkappeṟṟu merukiṭappaṭṭiruppatum ivaikaḷait tuvāramiṭṭiruppatum  ariya ceyaleṉa aṟiñarkaḷāl matikkappaṭukiṟatu.
iṭuppaṇi
tamiḻarkaḷ toṉṟu toṭṭup palvēṟu vakaiyāṉa iṭup paṇikaḷai (nucuppaṇikaḷai) aṇintu vantuḷḷaṉar. ippoḻutu iṭaiyil aṇivatu oṭṭiyāṇam eṉappaṭum. muṟkālattil mēkalai, kalāpam, viricikai pōṉṟa palavakai aṇikaḷ aṇiyap paṭṭu vantaṉa. ittakaiya aṇikaḷ arappāvilum mokañca tārōvilum kaṇṭeṭukkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. araippaṭṭikai nalla vēlaippāṭuṭaiyavai. cila 4 caraṅkaḷaiyum cila 6 caraṅkaḷaiyum uṭaiyaṉa. ovvoru carattilum pīppāy pōṉṟa civappuk kalmaṇikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaikaḷil cila malarkaḷiṉ arumpukaḷ pōṉṟum cila oṭṭiyāṇam pōṉṟum cataṅkaikaḷ kōkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṅku muttukkaḷ kōtta nucuppaṇikaḷ cilavum kaṇṭeṭukkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivaikaḷil cilavaṟṟil nuṇṇiya vēlaippāṭukaḷ uḷḷa aḻakiya mukappukaḷum kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
inta nucuppaṇikaḷ cilavaṟṟil palvēṟu uyarnta navamaṇikaḷ kōkkap peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa. inta maṇikaḷiṟ pala veḷināṭukaḷi liruntu iṟakkumati ceyyappaṭṭaṉavāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻakattiluḷḷa koṟkai muttukaḷum iṅku iṭam peṟ ṟuḷḷaṉa.
cintu veḷi nākarikattaik kaṭṭi vaḷartta tirāviṭap peruṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ mutuperuṅ kuravarkaḷ naṉiciṟanta nākarikattai yuṭaiya makkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟa avarkaḷiṉ navamaṇikaḷ ilaṅkum nucuppaṇi oṉṟē nalla eṭuttukkāṭṭāka miḷirkiṉṟaṉa. maṇi kaḷai naṉṟākac cōtittu avaikaḷil kuṟṟamaṟṟa maṇikaḷaip poṟukki eṭuttut tēyttu merukiṭṭu, tuḷaiyiṭṭu iruppatu āccariyappaṭattakkatākum. avarkaḷiṭam inta ariya vēlai kaḷaic ceyya uyarnta karuvikaḷ irukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu naṉku terikiṟatu. palamaṇikaḷai oṭṭavaittu iṇaittut tēyttu aḻakiya pūppōlac ceytuḷḷaṉar.
kaiyaṇi
tamiḻarkaḷ ulōka kālattiṟku muṉpē atāvatu putiya kaṟkālattilē kāppu eṉṉum nūlai aṇiya muṟpaṭṭuviṭṭaṉar. arappāvil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa kaiyaṇi palāppaḻattiṉ mēl tōlaip pōṉṟu mēlē muḷ muḷḷāka cūriya katirkaḷaip pōl kūriya muṉaikaḷaiyuṭaiyatāka irukkiṟatu. ittakaiya kaik kāppukaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭil malaippakutiyil uḷḷa paḻam kuṭi makkaḷiṭamum uḷḷaṉa. iḵtaṉṟi palavitak kāppukaḷum vaḷaikaḷum iṅkuk kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. mēlum pala cempu, veḷḷi, poṉ kāppukaḷum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. cila melliya takaṭukaḷāl kuḻāy pōṉṟu ceyyappaṭṭu uḷḷē meḻuku aṭaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. mēlum iṅku ippi, kaḷimaṇ, mākkal, caṅku, paḷiṅku mutaliyavai kaḷāl ceyyap peṟṟa kai aṇikaḷum uṇṭu.
mokañcatārōvil palavitamāṉa mālaikaḷum kāppukaḷum kaṭakaṅkaḷum vaḷaikaḷum kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avai pōṟṟat takkavaiyākum. uruṇṭaiyāṉa poṉmaṇikaḷaik kōttu aḻakiya mukappukaḷ iṇaittuc ceyyappeṟṟa kolucu kaḷum uṇṭu.
aṉṟiyum civappu, kaṟuppuk kaṟkaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa ciṟiya kāppukaḷum uḷḷaṉa. iṅkuk kiṭaitta maṇ kāppukaḷ aṟputa māṉa vēlaippāṭuṭaiyaṉavāyt tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. maṇṇāl ceyyappeṟṟa ikkāppukaḷukku palvēṟu vaṇṇaṅkaḷ tīṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. mokañcatārōvil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa veṇkala nāṭṭiya maṅkaiyiṉ kaiyil aṭukkaṭukkāyp pala vaḷaikaḷ kāṇappaṭuvatu pārkkat takkatākum.
paṇṭutoṭṭut tamiḻ makaḷir taṅkaḷ kaikaḷil poṉ, veḷḷi, kaṇṇāṭi, meḻuku, caṅku, vaḷaikaḷ aṇivataip pōṉṟu cintuveḷi makaḷirum palvēṟu vitamāṉa kāppukaḷum kolukkaḷum vaḷaikaḷum aṇintu vantatu ārāyattakkatākum.
kaiviral aṇikaḷ
arappāvil poṉ, veḷḷi mutaliyavaikaḷāl ceyyap peṟṟa kaiviral aṇikaḷ - mōtiraṅkaḷ pala kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. mokañcatārōvil cempu, veṇkalam, caṅku mōtiraṅkaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. arappāvil aḻakiya pīṅkāṉ mōtiraṅkaḷ kūṭak kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. iṅkuc cila mōtiraṅkaḷ melliya uruṇṭaik kampikaḷālāṉa vaḷaiyaṅkaḷākak kiṭait tuḷḷaṉa. ivai mōtiraṅkaḷākavē karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iḵtaṉṟit taṭṭaiyākak kampikaḷāl taṭṭappeṟṟa ulōkattālāṉa mōtiraṅkaḷum uḷḷaṉa.
arappāvil kiṭaitta aḻakiya mōtiram oṉṟu catura mukappu uṭaiyatāyk kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. mukappiṉ mītu kuṟukkum neṭukku mākap pala kōṭukaḷ tīṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. mēlum iṅkuḷḷa caṅkuc cippi mōtiraṅkaḷ nalla eḻiluṭaṉ tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. iṅku aṟiñarkaḷ maṇikaḷ patitta mōtiraṅkaḷai evarum kaṇṭatākak kūṟavillai.
kāl aṇikaḷ
kāl aṇikaḷ, taṇṭai, cilampu, kāl kāppu eṉṟellām kūṟappaṭum. mokañcatārōvil kaṇṭeṭukkap peṟṟa pommai kaḷil vaḷainta kāl kāppukaḷ - atāvatu taṇṭaikaḷ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. itaṉ mūlam iṅkuḷḷa makkaḷ uṟutiyākak kāl aṇikaḷ aṇintirukkalām eṉṟu karuta muṭiyum. ittakaiya kāl kāppukaḷ tirāviṭa nākarikam paraviyiruppatāka aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟum kirīṭ tīvilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. moñcatārōvil kaṇṭa kālaṇikaḷ kirīṭ tīvil maṭṭumaṉṟi āntira nāṭṭilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. vaṭaintiyāvil cimlā pakutikaḷil vāḻum malai iṉa makkaḷiṟ palar iṉṉum intak kālkāppukaḷai viṭāmal aṇintu varukiṉṟārkaḷ. tamiḻarkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟup pukaḻpeṟṟa aṇiyākap pulappaṭum cilampukaḷum iṅkuk kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. mokañca tārōvil veḷḷiyāl ceyyap peṟṟa kāl kāppukaḷ pōṉṟa vaṭivil pala poruḷkaḷ kaṇṭeṭukkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
kālviral aṇi
makkaḷ kaiviralkaḷil aṇiyum aṇikalaṉai mōtiram eṉpar. cilar viralāḻi, kaṇaiyāḻi eṉṟum kūṟuvar. tamiḻarkaḷ kai viralkaḷil aṇiyum mōtiram pōṉṟu kāl viralkaḷilum palavita aṇikaḷ aṇivar. avai perumpālum veḷḷiyāka irukkum. āṉāl mokañcatārōvil tamiḻ nāṭṭu makaḷirkaḷaip pōl peṇkaḷ kālviral mōtiraṅkaḷil oṉṟāṉa meṭṭiyai aṇintu vantirukkiṟārkaḷ eṉṟu terikiṟatu. tamiḻakattaip pōṉṟu arappāvilum, mokañcatārōvilum palavitak kāl viralaṇikaḷ kiṭaikkavillai. āṉāl niccayamākak kāl viral aṇikaḷ aṇintuḷḷārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟa muṭiyum. ār. ṭi. pāṉarji pōṉṟa aṟiñarkaḷ mokañcatārōvil pīli pōṉṟa vaṭivuḷḷa veḷḷik kālviral mōtiraṅkaḷum kiṭaittiruppatākak kūṟukiṉ ṟārkaḷ.
itara aṇikaḷ
ivaiyaṉṟi arappāvilum mokañcatārōvilum poṉ; veḷḷi, veṇkalam, cempu, mākkal, caṅku, pīṅkāṉ, paṭikam, pavaḷam, maṇikkal, maṇ pōṉṟavaikaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa eṇṇaṟṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaikaḷiṟ palavaṟṟai iṉṉa aṇi eṉṟu kūṟuvataṟkillai. aṅkuk kiṭaitta pommaikaḷil tīṭṭappaṭṭa uruvaṅkaḷai vaittē iṉṉa aṇi eṉṟu tīrmāṉikka vēṇṭiyatirukkiṟatu. iṅkup pala tōṭukaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. avai pottāṉkaḷāka irukkalām eṉṟum cilar kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. inta aṇiyil aḻakiya iḷam piṟai vaṭivam kūṭak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. itaṉ naṭuvil oḷitarum maṇikaḷum cippikaḷum patikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṅkuk kiṭaittuḷḷa cumār 2-6 aṅkula nīḷamum, 2-3 aṅkula akalamum uḷḷa patakkaṅkaḷ taṉic ciṟappukaḷ vāyntaṉa. avai mākkallil ākkappeṟṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ mītu iḷam piṟaivaṭivam oḷirkiṟatu. erutu uruvamum uḷḷatu. itu camaya cammantamāṉatākak karutappaṭukiṟatu eṉṟu cila aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.5
kuṟippukaḷ :
1.	(A) The Civilization of Babylonia & Assyria - Jastrowe
	(B) The Excavation Babylon - Rebert Koldway.
2.	Mohenjo - Daro and the Indus Civilization - Sir. John Marshall 
Vol. I & II.
3.	Dravidian Civilisation - Prof. Rakhal Das Benerji, Modern Review Sep. 1927.
	“There are two different theories about the Dravidian invation or migration into India. One class of writers believe that the Dravidian migrated from India into Babylonia through Afganistan and Beluchistan. The similarity of Dravidian and Sumarian ethnic type was recongised by H.R. Hall long before the discoveries of Mohenja - daro and Harappa. It is of opinion that” It is by no means improbable that the Sumarians were an Indian race which passed certainly by land perhaps also by sea through Persia to the Valley of two Rivers?
4.	The wonders that was India - A.L. Basham New York - 1954. P. 24
	“The modern South Indian is usually a blend of Mediterranian and Proto - Australoid, the two Chief ethnic factors in the Harappa culture moreover the Harappa religion seems to show many similarities with these elements of Hinduism which are specially popular in the Dravidian Country.”
5.	(a) cintu veḷi makkaḷ kaṟkaḷaic cōtittu avaṟṟiṉ taṉmaik kēṟṟa muṟaiyil uruvākki vaḻavaḻappākat tēyttu merukiṭ ṭuḷḷaṉar. ivvēlaippāṭṭiṟkut tēvaippaṭṭa kaṭaical entiraṅkaḷ avarkaḷiṭam iruntirukka vēṇṭum. - mokañcatārō ṭākṭar. mā. ïuhrkhÂ¡f«, v«.V., ãv¢o.,
	(ā) poṉ, veḷḷi, cempu, veṇkalam yāṉaittantam, kaṇṇāṭi, ōṭukaḷ mutaliyavaikaḷāl cintuveḷiyil āparaṇaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa tamiḻ intiyā. na. ci. kantaiyā piḷḷai, ceṉṉai 1949. pak. 27.

6. aṇikaḷum paṇikaḷum
ātittanallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa poruḷkaḷil pala irumpu āyutaṅkaḷum veṇkalap pāttiraṅkaḷum poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷum maṭkalaṅkaḷum uḷḷaṉa. ātitta nallūr nākarikam cumār ki.mu. 1000ām āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉum ki.mu. 2000ām āṇṭukaḷukkup piṉṉum eḻunta nākarikamāka irukkalām eṉṟu putai poruḷ ārāycci aṟiñarkaḷ muṭipu kaṭṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ. itaṉ mūlam tamiḻakattil mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē aṇikalaṉkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭiruntaṉa eṉṟu tairiyamākak kūṟalām. atōṭu aṇikalaṉkaḷai ākkum poṟkollarkaḷum avarkaḷiṉ neruṅkiya uṟaviṉarkaḷāṉa taccar, kollar, kaṉṉār, kaltaccar pōṉṟavarkaḷum iṟṟaikku mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ piruntē tamiḻakattil nilaitta kuṭikaḷāy vāḻntu varukiṟārkaḷ eṉṟu uṟutiyāyk kūṟamuṭiyum. arappā nākarikattiṉ mūlam tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷāṉa cintu veḷik kammāḷarkaḷ ki.mu. 3000ām āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē arumperum āyutaṅkaḷai yum aḻakiya poṉ aṇikaḷaiyum ariya maṇikaḷ patitta aṇi kalaṉkaḷaiyum nāliṟku mēṟpaṭṭa māṭikaḷaiyuṭaiya oppaṟṟa uṟaiyuḷkaḷaiyum ariya vēlaippāṭukaḷaiyuṭaiya veṇkalap paṭimaṅkaḷaiyum  cuṇṇāmpiṉālum nuraikalliṉālum ceyyap paṭṭa eṇṇaṟṟa paṭimaṅkaḷaiyum muttiraikaḷaiyum paṭaittiruk kiṟārkaḷ eṉṟu meyppikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
teyva kammiyar 
taṭṭār, taccar, kaṉṉār, kollar, kaltaccar ākiya aintu piriviṉarum vicuvappirammaṉiṉ tirukkaṇṇil utayamāṉavarkaḷ eṉṟum, ivarkaḷ vicuvakarmā, mayaṉ ākiya teyvakkammiyar vaḻiyil vantavarkaḷ eṉṟum purāṇaṅkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. poṟkollarkaḷ tamiḻakattil kammiyar, kammāḷar, taṭṭār, ācāriyār eṉa aḻaikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl akarātikaḷilum nikaṇṭukaḷilum ivarkaḷukku, aḵkacālaiyar, aṟivar aṟputar, ōvar, kaṇṇāḷar, kaṇviṉaiñar, kammiyar kollar, ciṟpiyar, tapatiyar, tuvaṭṭar, pulavar, puṉaivar, yavaṉar, vittakar, vittiyar, viṉaiñar eṉṟu pala peyarkaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivarkaḷ aṉaivarum tamiḻarkaḷēyāvar. ivarkaḷ paṇṭait tamiḻar tolkuṭiyil utittavarkaḷ āvārkaḷ. ivarkaḷil cilar taṅkaḷai vicuvappirāmaṇar eṉṟu kūṟi āriyarkaḷōṭu iṇaintu koḷḷa muṉaintu iṭarppaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷai nākar eṉpārum uḷar. ivarkaḷ tamiḻakattiṉ uyir nāṭi eṉṟum nām eṇṇukiṟōm.
inta aintu toḻilāḷarkaḷum tamiḻarkaḷiṉ camūka vāḻ viṟku iṉṟiyamaiyātavarkaḷ. taccarkaḷum kollarkaḷum vīṭu kaḷaiyum vīṭṭiluḷḷa iṉṟiyamaiyāta taṭṭu muṭṭuc cāmāṉ kaḷaiyum ceytu utavupavarkaḷ. iḵtaṉṟi uḻutal, vitaittal, aṟuttal mutaliya makkaḷiṉ uyirōmpum toḻilukku iṉṟiyamai yāta kalappai, koḻu, arivāḷ mutaliyavaikaḷaic ceytu koṭuppavarkaḷ. aṉṟiyum pāṉaicaṭṭi, necavu mutaliyaṉa ceyyum toḻilāḷarkaḷukku iṉṟiyamaiyāta poruḷkaḷaic ceytu koṭuppavarkaḷ. kaṉṉārkaḷ cempu, pittaḷai, veṇ kalam, veḷḷi, īyam, poṉ mutaliya ulōkaṅkaḷil cempu, kiṇṇam, tāmpaḷam, kuṭam, caruvam, kumpā, vaṭṭil, koṭṭuk kaṭavai, aṇṭā, pōṇiccaṭṭi, tavalai, neyccaṭṭi, veṇ ṇeyccaṭṭi pōṉṟavaikaḷaiyum kaipiṭikaḷ, maṇikaḷ, vaṇṭi kaḷukku vēṇṭiya pala alaṅkārap poruḷ pōṉṟavaikaḷaiyum ceypavarkaḷ. kal taccarkaḷ, tapatikaḷ pōṉṟavarkaḷ  kal maravaikaḷ, kīraiccaṭṭi, ammi, ural, kuntāṇi pōṉṟavaikaḷai yum kōyilkaḷ, cilaikaḷ, cempup paṭimam pōṉṟavaikaḷaiyum ceypavarkaḷ. poṟkollarkaḷ tām āṭamparap poruḷ kaḷākiya aṇikalaṉkaḷai veḷḷi, poṉ mutaliya tātup poruḷkaḷilum maṇikaḷ iḻaittum ceypavarkaḷ.2 ivarkaḷ  makkaḷukkum teyvaṅkaḷukkum maṉṉarkaḷukkum aṇikalaṉkaḷaic ceypavarkaḷ. ivarkaḷ atika vilaiyuyarnta poṉṉil aṇikalaṉ kaḷaic ceyvatālum mika nuṇukkamāṉa vēlaippāṭukaḷaicceytu varuvatālum atikamāṉa kūli peṟupavarkaḷ. piṟa nāṉku vitamāṉa toḻilāḷarkaḷaiyum viṭa nalla nilaiyil iruppavarkaḷ.
poṟkollarkaḷ tamiḻakattil ciṟu kuḻantaikaḷukku, piṟantu oru āṇṭu niṟaivu peṟumpoḻutum, ēṉ ataṟku muṉpum kātu kuttukiṟārkaḷ. kātil kaṭukkaṉ allatu ciṟu kuccukaḷ ceytu pōṭukiṟārkaḷ. aṉṟiyum kuḻantaikaḷukku mūkkuk kuttu vatum uṇṭu. kaḻuttil uruttirākkam patitta kavuṭu, poṉ malai, iṭuppil poṟ koṭi, poṟcataṅkai, kālil taṇṭai, kolucu kaiyil ciṟu kāppu mutaliyavaikaḷum ceytu aṇivippārkaḷ. tamiḻarkaḷiṉ vīṭukaḷil kātu kuttuvatu oru viḻāvākavē naṭattap peṟukiṟatu. itaṟkup poṟ kollarkaḷukku taṭcaṇaikaḷum koṭukkappaṭum.
appāl naṭaipeṟum tirumaṇa viḻāvil mutaṟkaṭṭam tālikku poṉṉurukki viṭuvatākum. itu oru nalla nāḷil uṟṟār uṟaviṉar naṇparuṭaṉ akkampakkattu vīṭṭukkārarkaḷum cūḻa iruntu naṭakkum viḻāvākum. itil mukkiya paṅku vakippavarkaḷ tamiḻakap poṟkollarkaḷēyāvar. tirumaṇa viḻāvil atāvatu tirunāṇ pūṭṭal eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum maṅkalanāḷil tāli ceytu koṭukkum ācārikku taṭcaṇaikaḷ koṭuttut tāliyaip peṟṟup peṇ kaḻuttil aṇivikkappaṭukiṟatu.
inta camūkattil, irumpu vēlai ceyyum kollarkaḷē ellāt toḻilāḷarkaḷukkum tāy pōṉṟu iruppavarkaḷ. avarkaḷ ceytu koṭukkum uḷikaḷum cuttiyalkaḷum, koṭiṟu (kuṟaṭu)kaḷum piṟavum illāviṭṭāl ēṉaiya toḻilāḷarkaḷ toḻil iyaṟṟa iyalātu. āṉāl ivarkaḷ tām inta aintu vitamāṉa toḻilāḷarkaḷilum poruḷātāra nilaiyil kīḻē iruppavarkaḷ. ellōrilum kaṣṭamāṉa toḻil puripavarkaḷ. ivarkaḷilum koñcam kuṟaintiruppavarkaḷ kaltaccarkaḷum, kaṉṉārkaḷum āvar. maravēlai ceyyum taccarkaḷum, veṇkalap paṭimaṅkaḷ ceyyum tapatikaḷum poruḷātāra nilaiyil caṟṟu uyarntiruppavarkaḷ.2 āṉāl poṉ vēlai ceyyum taṭṭār kaḷē inta camūkattil uyarnilaiyil iruppavarkaḷ. ivarkaḷ maṟṟat toḻilāḷarkaḷaiviṭap poruḷātāra nilaiyil uyarntiruppavarkaḷ. atikak kaṣṭamillāta āṉāl nuṇmaiyāṉa toḻil ceytu vāḻpavarkaḷ.
tamiḻakattiṉ camūka vāḻvilum paṇpāṭṭilum inta aintu vitamāṉa toḻilāḷarkaḷukkum mukkiya paṅkuṇṭu.3 ivarkaḷil teyvat tiruvuruvaṅkaḷai vaṭikkum tapatikaḷ uruva vārpput tuṟaiyilum, avaikaḷil uyarnta pāvaṅkaḷai uruvākkuvatilum kaitērntavarkaḷāy irukkiṟārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ ciṟpa cāttiraṅ kaḷaiyum, kaṭṭiṭak kalai nūlkaḷaiyum (vāttu cāttiraṅkaḷai yum, civākamaṅkaḷaiyum) payiṉṟavarkaḷāy irukkiṟārkaḷ. camaya muṟaippaṭi āccāra aṉuṭṭāṉaṅkaḷaic ceypavarkaḷāyt tikaḻkiṟārkaḷ. tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ pōṟṟutalukkum matippiṟkum uriyavarkaḷāy irukkiṟārkaḷ.
āṉāl paḻamperum nākarikaṅkaḷ aṉaittilum poṟ kollarkaḷiṉ māperum kaittiṟam pōṟṟak kūṭiyatāka miḷir kiṟatu. atu paḻaṅkālap paṇpai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭatāka amaintirukkiṟatu. avarkaḷiṉ kaivaṇṇam meyvanta aṇikalak kalaiyiṉ uyarnta - oppaṟṟa apiviruttikku naṟcāṉṟākat tikaḻ kiṉṟatu. tamiḻarkaḷiṉ aṇikalakkalai, arappāviṉ tirāviṭa nākarikattiṉ mūlam 5000ām āṇṭu1 paḻamaiyuṭaiyatāka matikkap paṭukiṟatu; ātittanallūr tamiḻar nākarikattiṉ mūlam, mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muntiya paḻamai vāyntatāy matikkappaṭukiṟatu. ivaikaḷ tāṉ tamiḻarkaḷ āyirakkaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē ciṟanta nākarikam paṭaitta pēriṉam eṉṟu perumaiyōṭu pēcuvataṟkuc cāṉṟākum. tirāviṭap peruṅkuṭiyiṉ vaḻivanta kollarkaḷ camaitta aḻakiya maṇikaḷ patitta aṇikalaṉkaḷum pala aṭukkukaḷaiyuṭaiya vāṉṟōy māṭaṅkaḷum, mukil tavaḻum kōpuraṅkaḷum vimāṉaṅkaḷum āyutaṅkaḷum, veṇkala pāttiraṅ kaḷum paṭimaṅkaḷum nalla eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷākum.
aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ ceyyappaṭṭa kaḻuttaṇi kaḷum, kaiyaṇikaḷum, nucuppaṇikaḷum, talaiyaṇikaḷum, piṟa vaṇikaḷum iṉṟaiya nākarikam  paṭaitta aṇikaḷaiviṭac ciṟappuṭaiyaṉavāy viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟaik kaṇṭu namatu intiya aracāṅkamum ataṉ amaiccarum ulakil oppaṟṟa pala aṟiñarkaḷum pōṟṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. pāratanāṭu paḻam perum paṇ pāṭṭaip peṟṟa nāṭu eṉap pāriluḷḷa palarum pārāṭṭukiṉ ṟaṉar. itaṉ mūlam pārata tēvikkup pārpukaḻum perum pēṟṟait tēṭikkoṭuttavarkaḷ tamiḻakak kammiyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷāṉa tirāviṭap peruṅkuṭiyil vanta arappaṉ makkaḷāy irukkiṉ ṟārkaḷ eṉṟu nām perumaiyuṟukiṉṟōm;  tirāviṭak kammiyarkaḷ paṇṭu camaitta paḻam perum aṇikalaṉkaḷum maṇikalaṉkaḷum veḷit tōṟṟattil aḻaku vāyntaṉavāyum, uṟuti vāyntaṉa vāyum nuṇṇiya vēlaip pāṭṭil oppaṟṟaṉavāyum viḷaṅku kiṉṟaṉa. namatu poṟ kollarkaḷ āyiram āyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē poṉṉai mayiriḻai pōṉṟa kampiyāka iḻuppatilum poṟkaṭṭiyai veṅkāyaccaruku pōṉṟu melliya takaṭukaḷākat taṭṭuvatilum aṇikalaṉkaḷaik kottuvatilum aṟuppu vēlaikaḷ ceyvatilum takaṭukaḷai oṭṭip poṭi vaittu ūtuvatilum caṅkilikaḷākac camaippatilum koṭikaḷākap piṉṉi appāl atai iṟukkuvatilum iṟutiyāka merukiṭuvatilum, ciṟu ciṟu vaḷaiyaṅkaḷākac ceytu oṉṟāka iṇaittu ūci mālai kaḷākavum aṇi vaṭaṅkaḷākavum ceyvatilum tiṟamai vāynta varkaḷ. takaṭukaḷaic curuṭṭuvatilum  taṅkat takaṭukaḷaic ceytu appāl atai accil vaittu aṭittu - iṟukkik kottumāṉa vēlai(nakā vēlai)yiṉ mūlam puṭaippu uruvaṅkaḷaic ceyvatilum aṟuppumāṉa vēlaippāṭṭiṉ mūlam, ceṭi, koṭi, malar, arumpu, kāy, paṟavai, vilaṅku teyva uruvaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavaikaḷaip peritākavō allatu oru ciṟu eṟumpuk kaṇpōṉṟa aḷavil ciṟitākavō ceytu cittirap pūvēlaikaḷ miḷira eḻiluṟap paṇipuriya vallavarkaḷ. mañcaḷ, civappu niṟaṅkaḷil aṇikaḷukkut tiṟampaṭap paḻuppup pōṭuki ṟārkaḷ. poṉṉil kalanta veḷḷi, cempu pōṉṟavaikaḷaip puṭam pōṭṭu uyarnta paim poṉṉāyp paḻaṅkālattilē ceytu ataṉ māṟṟum uraiyum kuṉṟātirukkac ceytārkaḷ. navamaṇikaḷaip poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷil eḷitil kīḻē viḻuntuviṭāmal pūṇpittu vaṇṇa malarkaḷ pōṉṟum aḻakiya paḻaṅkaḷ pōṉṟum vaṭivāṉa ceṭi koṭikaḷ pōṉṟum ceyvatil piṟanāṭṭārkaḷaiviṭat tamiḻakap pattarkaḷ (kammiyarkaḷ) kaivaṇṇam vāyntavarkaḷāy iṉṟum ciṟappuṟṟu viḷaṅkukiṉṟārkaḷ.4
paṇikaḷ
aṇikalaṉkaḷukku, niṟattilum, tarattilum pacumaiyilum ēṟṟa tātupporuḷ poṉṉēyākum. cuṭiṉum cempoṉ taṉṉoḷi kuṉṟātu eṉpatu tamiḻar kaṇṭa poṉmoḻi. poṉ eṅkuk kiṭantālum mācēṟātu; veḷḷiyil kaṟaiyēṟum, cempil kaḷimpēṟum, irumpil turuppiṭikkum. pacumpoṉ ekkālattum turuvēṟātu; oḷi kuṉṟātu; māṟṟuk kuṟaiyātu; ataṉ niṟam uḷḷattaik kavarkiṟatu. poṉ vāḻvai vaḷampeṟac ceykiṟatu.
poṉ aṇikaḷ ceyvataṟku ēṟṟa, uyarnta taṉmai vāynta kaṉipporuḷāka irukkiṟatu. oru ciṟu tāṉiya eṭai niṟaiyuḷḷa poṉṉai 500 aṭi nīḷam uḷḷa poṉ iḻaiyāy nīṭṭi viṭalām. atu māperum karuvūlattiṉ carva viyāpakamāṉa ciṉṉamāka ilaṅkukiṟatu. muṟkālat tamiḻaka makaḷir āyiram poṉpeṟṟa poṉ iḻaikaḷālāṉa poṟcarikaip paṭṭāṭai kaḷaip puṉaintu vantuḷḷaṉar. poṉ iḻai vēynta tukilkaḷ mikutiyāka muṟkālat tuṇic cantaiyil viṟkap peṟṟaṉa. poṉ, veḷḷi iḻaikaḷait tūymaiyākap pārttu iṇaittu vilai yuyarnta carikaic cēlaiyāka māṟṟappaṭṭaṉa; uyarāṭaiyākap pōṟṟap peṟṟaṉa. teyvat tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ poṉṉāl vārkkap peṟṟaṉa. poṟṟakaṭṭāl capparaṅkaḷum, kūraikaḷum ceyyap paṭṭaṉa. citamparattiluḷḷa āṭavallāṉ āṭiya poṉṉampalam (kaṉakacapai) ulakap pukaḻ peṟṟatāyt tikaḻkiṟatu. vimāṉa mukaṭu kaḷil poṟ kalayaṅkaḷ ēṟṟap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻakattil ērāḷa mākap poṉ kiṭaittatu. tamiḻarkaḷil ēḻai mutal paṇakkārar varai poṉ aṇikaḷ pūṇpatil pērārvam koṇṭaṉar. poṉṉait teyvamākap pōṟṟiṉar. poṉṉiṉ mītu koṇṭa uyarvāṉa eṇṇattiṉālē iṟaivaṉaip poṉ pōṉṟa niṟam vāyntavaṉākak karutip pōṟṟiṉar. tēvārattil poṉṉār mēṉiyaṉē eṉṟum poṉṉē eṉṟum pōṟṟip pukaḻntu pāṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
tamiḻaka vaṇikarkaḷ veḷināṭukaḷukku, uḷnāṭṭup poruḷ kaḷai ēṟṟumati ceytu tamiḻnāṭṭil veḷināṭṭiliruntu ērāḷamākap poṉvantu kuviyumāṟu ceytu vantaṉar. vaṇikarkaḷ veḷināṭu ceṉṟu vaṇikam purintu ērāḷamāṉa poṉṉait tiraṭṭi vantaṉar. tamiḻaka maṉṉarkaḷ veḷināṭukaḷai veṉṟu aṅkirunta poṉṉaic cūṟaiyāṭi vantaṉar. veḷināṭṭu maṉṉar kaḷai veṉṟu avarkaḷiṭamiruntu āṇṭutōṟum ērāḷamāṉa poṉṉait tiṟaiyākap peṟṟu vantaṉar. yavaṉarkaḷ tamiḻaka muttukkaḷ, maculiṉ tuṇikaḷ miḷaku pōṉṟavaimītu mōkaṅ koṇṭu kōṭikōṭiyāyp poṟkācukaḷaiyum poṟkaṭṭi kaḷaiyum koṇṭuvantu koṭṭiviṭṭut tamiḻaka muttukkaḷaiyum tuṇikaḷaiyum piṟapaṇṭaṅkaḷaiyum vāṅkic ceṉṟaṉar. tamiḻakat tilum ataic cūḻnta nāṭukaḷilum ērāḷamāṉa poṉ tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭṭatu. pala maṇikaḷ curaṅkaṅkaḷiliruntum kaṭaliṉiṉṟum kiṭaittaṉa. ataṉāl tamiḻakam poṉ koḻikkum naṉṉāṭāy viḷaṅkiyatu. tamiḻaka makaḷirkaḷ poṉkāykkum maramō eṉṟu piṟanāṭṭār aiyuṟum vaṇṇam uccitoṭṭu uḷḷaṅkāl varai poṉ aṇikaḷaip pūṇṭiruntaṉar. ēḻai makkaḷ kūṭa ērāḷa māṉa poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷaip pūṇṭiruntaṉar.
aṇikalaṉkaḷ ākkun toḻil
caṅka kālattiṟku muṉpē tamiḻakattil poṉ veḷḷi mutaliya tātup poruḷkaḷil aṇikalaṉkaḷ ceytu aṇiyap paṭṭu vantaṉa. ataṟkup paṇṭait tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷum cep pēṭukaḷum teyvat tiru uruvaṅkaḷum cāṉṟu tarukiṉṟaṉa. caṅkak kāla maṉṉarkaḷum - ēṉ? teyvaṅkaḷum talaiyil muṭikaḷait (poṉṉālum maṇiyālum ceyyappeṟṟa makuṭaṅkaḷait) tarittiruntaṉar. avarkaḷ meyyil palvēṟu aṇikalaṉkaḷaiyum kaiyil maṇikaḷ patitta poṉṉālāṉa ceṅkōlaiyum pūṇ ṭiruntaṉar. avai mika nuṇṇiya vēlaippāṭukaḷ uṭaiyaṉavāy iruntaṉa. cilappatikārattil vēntar muṭimutaṟ kalaṉkaḷ camaip pōṉ yāṉeṉa varum ceyyuḷ mūlam akkālattilum ataṟku muṉṉum maṉṉarkaḷ poṉmuṭi puṉaintiruntaṉar eṉṟum avaikaḷait tamiḻakattiṉ poṟkollarkaḷē camaittārkaḷ eṉṟum aṟikiṉṟōm.
urukkut taṭṭār
caṅkakkāla maṉṉarkaḷ pala poṉ aṇikaḷaip pūṇṭirun taṉar. aracikaḷ muṭiyiṉiṉṟu aṭivaraip pala poṉ aṇikaḷai aṇintiruntaṉar. kāvalarkaḷ, pāvalarkkum, pāṇarkaḷukkum pāṭiṉikaḷukkum piṟarkkum poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷaip paricāka aḷittu vantaṉar. poṟkācukaḷai vārivāri vaḻaṅkiṉar. itaṟ kellām kāraṇam tamiḻakam peṟṟa poruḷātārac ceḻumaiyē yākum. ērāḷamāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷ ceyyap peṟṟatāl eṅkum poṟkollarkaḷ perukiṉar. akkālap poṟkollarkaḷ teyvakkammiyar eṉṟu pōṟṟap peṟṟaṉar. ivarkaḷil, aṉṟu urukkut taṭṭār, paṇit taṭṭār eṉṟa iru piriviṉar iruntaṉar. urukkut taṭṭār peritum poṉṉālāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷaic ceyvōrāy iruntaṉar. iṉṉum nākācu vēlaikkārar, aṟuppu vēlaikkārar eṉa iru piriviṉar tōṉṟi nuṇukkamāṉa vēlai kaḷaic ceytu vantaṉar.
tamiḻakattil toḻilkaḷ pala eḻuntamaiyāl toḻil kaḷukku vēṇṭiya karuvikaḷ palavum eḻuntaṉa. ivaikaḷil palavaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷ tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. yāṉaikkut toṭi (vaḷai)yiṭum kollaraippaṟṟi perum pāṇāṟṟup paṭaiyil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.5 toḻilāḷar
kaḷiṉ mukkiya karuviyākiya turutti6 koṭiṟu7 ulai8 cammaṭṭi9 mutaliya karuvikaḷaip paṟṟiyum toḻilāḷikaḷ ceyyum vēlai kaḷaip paṟṟiyum tamiḻ nūṟkaḷil naṉku viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
paṇittaṭṭār 
paṇikaḷ, atāvatu aṇikalaṉkaḷ ceyyum poṟ kollar kaḷukkup paṇittaṭṭār eṉṟa peyar uṇṭu. ivarkaḷil maṭṭa vēlaikkārar, kottumāṉa vēlaikkārar, (nakācu vēlaikkārar) aṟuppu vēlaikkārar pōṉṟa pirivukaḷ uṇṭu. ivarkaḷ aṉai varum poṉ allatu  veḷḷik kaṭṭikaḷai taṅkaḷukku vēṇṭiya aḷavu veṭṭi urukki appāl atai aṭittum taṭṭiyum aṟuttum paṇikaḷ ceytārkaḷ. ivarkaḷiṟ palar kāṟṟōṭṭa millāta ciṟu aṟaikaḷil taṅkaḷ kaikaḷaik koṇṭē aṭittut takaṭukaḷākac ceytum kampikaḷāka iḻuttum veṭṭiyum oṭṭiyum poṭi vaittup paṟṟavaittum arāviyum vāḷāl aṟuttum tamar ūciyāl tuḷaiyiṭṭum, uḷiyāl kottiyum aṇi kalaṉkaḷai ākkiṉārkaḷ. ivarkaḷiṭam iyantiraṅkaḷ etuvum kiṭaiyātu. mēlum ivarkaḷiṭam cila karuvikaḷē uḷḷaṉa. āṉā lum avai ivarkaḷuṭaiya paṇikaḷai niṟaivākac ceyvataṟkup pōtumāṉatāka irunta ivarkaḷuṭaiya taḷavāṭaṅkaḷil cūṭu uṇṭākkavum urukkavum ceyvataṟku uriya oru ciṟu ūtu kuḻalum oṉṟākum. ivarkaḷatu ulaikkaḷam oru cātāraṇa maṇkuṭattiṉ uṭaikkappaṭṭa mēṟpākamēyākum. maṭkuṭattai naṭuvil iraṇ ṭāka uṭaittu vāyppakkattaik kīḻē vaittu, aṭippakutiyuḷḷa uṭpuṟattai akaṉṟa ōṭṭuc cillāl mūṭi ataṉmītu nelumiyai nirappi mēlē marakkarikaḷaip parappi neruppu mūṭṭi ataṉmītu marakkariyil ciṟitu paḷḷamākat tōṇṭi vaittu atil taṅkam vaittu urukki appāl atai eṭuttu nīril pōṭṭu kuḷirntatum cuttiyalāl aṭippārkaḷ. cilavaṟṟai kotikkavaittum aṭip pārkaḷ. cilavaṟṟaik kuḻalāl ūti urukkuvārkaḷ cilavaṟṟait turuttiyāl ūti urukiyatum atai eṭuttu nīril pōṭṭu eṭuttu aṭippārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ āyirakkaṇakkāṉa kirām eṭaiyuḷḷa poṟkaṭṭikaḷaiyum urukki eṇṇaṟṟa nakaikaḷaic ceytu viṭukiṉṟaṉar. atikamāṉa poṉ tuṇṭukaḷ iruntāl avaikaḷai yellām kukai eṉṉum uṟutiyākac ceyyappaṭṭa oru kalavai maṇpāttirattil pōṭṭu urukkiviṭuvārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ peritum ūtu kuḻaliṉ tūṇṭutalālē poṉṉai urukkukiṉṟaṉar. periya aḷavil poṉ iruntāltāṉ turuttiyai upayōkippar. turuttiyai upayōkikkum poṟkollarkaḷ atikamāka aṇi kalaṉkaḷ ceypavarkaḷāyiruppārkaḷ.
āyutaṅkaḷ
poṟkollarkaḷ ulōkaṅkaḷai urukkumpoḻutu peritum koṭiṟai upayōkippārkaḷ. ikkoṭiṟu kukaikaḷai eḷitil eṭuttu ulaiyil vaikkavum eṭukkavum upayōkappaṭum. koṭiṟu nīṇṭa kaippiṭiyum muṉpakkam vaḷaintum irukkum irumpālāṉa oru karuviyākum. ulōkaṅkaḷai urukki ulai mukattiṉiṉṟu eṭuttatum nīril pōṭṭu kuḷiracceytu tēvaik kēṟṟāṟ pōla veṭṭirumpu eṉṉum āyutattāl veṭṭic cuttiyāl taṭṭiyum vaḷaittum aṭittum kampiyākavō, takaṭākavō iḻuttum taṭṭiyum aṇikalaṉkaḷaic ceyya ārampippārkaḷ. ulaimukattiliruntu eṭutta poṟkaṭṭiyai veṭṭuvataṟku mutalil nīṇṭa, kaṉatta marakkaṭṭai mītu caturamāṉa mēṟpakkam uḷḷa oru irumpuk kaṭṭiyāl ceyta oru paṭṭaṟaiyaip payaṉpaṭuttuvārkaḷ. ippaṭṭaṟai caturamāyum mēṟpakkam 10 aṅkulac cuṟṟaḷavum kīḻppakkam 7 aṅkulac cuṟṟaḷavum uḷḷa oru irumpuk kaṭṭiyēyākum. itu eppoḻutum poṟkollarkaḷ muṉṉar irukkum. ivarkaḷ oru ciṟu tātup poruḷaik kotikka vaikkavum kotikka vaittapiṉ eṭukkavum nīṇṭu vaḷainta melliya takaṭṭiṉālāṉa ulaikkatiraip payaṉpaṭuttukiṟārkaḷ. ulōkap poruḷai ulaikkatirāl eṭuttup paṭṭaṟai mītu vaittuc cuttiyāl taṭṭuvārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ arukē eppoḻutum ulaikkatirum ūtu kuḻalum irukkum. iḵtaṉṟi peritum ciṟitu māṉa cāmaṇaṅkaḷum poṭiveṭṭiyum araṅkaḷum palvēṟu vakaiyāṉa muṉaikkatir, paṟṟuk koṭiṟu, tiṇṇaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavai kaḷum irukkum. poṟkollarkaḷukku oru mukkiyamāṉa karuvi kampi iḻukkum nūl caṭṭamākum. itu oru takaṭṭuvaṭivāṉa karuviyākum. ittakaṭu perumpālum urukkut takaṭṭiṉāl allatu paḻaiya araṅkaḷiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭatāka irukkum. it takaṭṭiṉmītu ciṟitum peritumāka varicai varicaiyāka tuḷai ceyyappaṭṭirukkum. tuḷaikaḷ peritiṉiṉṟu varicaik kiramamākac ciṟuttuk koṇṭē pōkum. poṟkollarkaḷ ulōkak kampiyai periya tuvārattiṟ pōṭṭu paṟṟuk koṭiṟiṉāl mutal mutalāka iḻuppārkaḷ. appaṭi varicaik kiramamāka iḻuppatāl kampi ciṟuttu nīṇṭu koṇṭē pōkum. taṅkaḷukku vēṇṭiya nīḷamō allatu parumaṉō vantatum kampi iḻuppatai niṟuttik koḷvārkaḷ. itaṉ mūlam mika nuṇṇiya mayiriḻai pōṉṟa kampikaḷaik kūṭa eḷitil ceytuviṭukiṟārkaḷ. eḷitāka 3 aṅkula muḷḷa oru poṉ allatu veḷḷik kaṭṭiyai 300 aṭi nīḷamuḷḷa kampikaḷāka iḻuttu viṭukiṟārkaḷ. iḵtaṉṟi melliya kampikaḷ ceyya, vairac caṭṭattaiyum payaṉpaṭuttukiṟārkaḷ. itu oru rūpāy allatu arai rūpāy vaṭivil irukkum.
poṟkollarkaḷiṭam pala taramāṉa accukaḷ irukkum. avai uṟutiyāṉa kalavaip poruḷkaḷiṉāl ceyyap peṟṟaṉa vākum. avaikaḷil mēṟpakuti kīḻppakutiyeṉa iruvakai accukaḷ irukkum. inta accukaḷ oru kaṉatta takaṭṭil paḷḷamākat tōṇṭappaṭṭirukkum. appaḷḷattil taṅkaḷukku vēṇṭiya uruva vaṭivaṅkaḷ tōṇṭappaṭṭirukkum. anta acciṉmītu melliya poṉ allatu veḷḷit takaṭukaḷai vaittu ataṉmēl oru irumput takaṭṭai vaittup piṭittuc cuttiyalāl metu vākat taṭṭuvārkaḷ.  takaṭṭil avarkaḷ virumpum uruvam, puṭai ciṟpampōl uruppeṟum. pukalākac ceyya vēṇṭiya aṇikala ṉukku melliya takaṭṭai acciṉmītu vaittu, ataṉmītu cempāl ceyyappaṭṭa uruvattāl aḻuttuvārkaḷ. uruvam takaṭṭil patintu viṭum. oru pāti uruvāṉatum atē pōṉṟu maṟṟumoru pātiyaiyum accil vaittu muṉpōl aḻuttiyum aṭittum maṟu pātiyaiyum uruvākkuvar. appāl iraṇṭaiyum iṇaittō allatu iṇaiyāmalō meḻukaṭaittut tiṇṇam pōṭṭu nakā paṇṇi merukiṭṭu vaippārkaḷ. accukaḷil meḻuku accukaḷ eṉṟum, veṇkala accukaḷ eṉṟum, urukku accukaḷ eṉṟum, pala vakaikaḷ uṇṭu. aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ taṉmaikku ēṟṟavāṟu palavitamāṉa uruvamuḷḷa accukaḷuṇṭu. takaṭukaḷai acciṉmītu vaittu aḻutti oḻuṅkupeṟat  taṭṭum tiṇṇaṅ kaḷum irukkum. aṇikalaṉkaḷ ceyvataṟkuriya paṭiva accukaḷai muṉpu vaittuk koṇṭu ulōkat takaṭukaḷaiyō kaṭṭi kaḷaiyō neruppiliṭṭu cenniṟam varumvarai paḻukkak kāycci ulaik karutiṉāl eṭuttu acciṉmītu vaittuc cuttiyāl aṭittu aṇikalaṉkaḷai uruvākkuvārkaḷ. nakā pōṭa veṇkalam allatu urukku accum upayōkappaṭuttappaṭum. niṟka, cūṭuṇṭāṉa tātup poruḷkaḷ acciṟ patippikka eḷitāṉa kuḻaivu uṭaiya tāka irukkum. aṇikalaṉkaḷai uruvākkum paṭiva accukaḷ aṉēkam uṇṭu. avaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷ ellām atu uruvākkum aṇikalaṉ kaḷiṉiṉṟu piṟantavaikaḷākavē irukkum. eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka nākar accu, muruku accu, viciṟi muruku accu pōṉṟavai kaḷākum.
potuvākat tamiḻnāṭṭup poṟkollarkaḷ *(pattar allatu ācāri) payaṉpaṭuttivarum mukkiyamāṉa āyutaṅkaḷiṉ vivaram aṭiyil varumāṟu.
1.	periya paṟṟuk koṭiṟu (paṟṟukkuṟaṭu - kampi iḻukkum periya paṟṟukkuṟaṭu)
2.	ciṟiya paṟṟuk koṭiṟu (pattukkuṟaṭu - kampi iḻukkum ciṟiya pattukkuṟaṭu)
3.	vāḷ caṭṭam
4.	āṭu tamar (cuḻal tamar)
5.	piṭittarāvi (puṭuccurāvi - arāvuvataṟku uriya poruḷaip piṭittuk koḷḷum)
6.	nūl caṭṭam (kampi iḻukkum tuvāramuḷḷa takaṭu)
7.	tiṇṇam (pūvēlaippāṭu ceyyum irumpu uḷi)
8.	paṉṟi muṭi (paṉṟi muṭiyāl ceyta kait tūrikai)
9.	pittaḷait tūrikai (piraṣ - nuṇukkamāṉa miṉukku vēlaippāṭṭiṟkāka maikkāt takaṭṭiṉ mītu kampikaḷ poruttic ceyyappaṭṭa tūrikai)
10.	poṭiveṭṭi (ciṟu poṉ veḷḷik kampikaḷai veṭṭum irumpu kattirikkōl)
11.	vaḷainta kuḻal (itu perumpālum caṅkilikaḷaip paṟṟa vaikkappayaṉpaṭuvatu)
12.	vaḷainta nākam (turutti vaittu ūtutal)
13.	metukatir (meruku katir)
14.	veṭṭirumpu (irumpiṉālāṉa uḷi.  itu veḷḷi, taṅkam mutaliyavaikaḷai veṭṭa upayōkappaṭum)
15.	aram (ulōkaṅkaḷai arāvap payaṉpaṭum)
16.	vāḷ (ulōkaṅkaḷai aṟukkum karuvi)
17.	cāṇai (tiṇṇaṅkaḷait tīṭṭum kal)
inta  āyutaṅkaḷ, tamiḻakattil palvēṟu māvaṭṭaṅkaḷilum tamiḻarkaḷ kuṭiyēṟiyuḷḷa īḻakam, kāḻakam, cāvakam pōṉṟa pakuti kaḷilum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. ivaikaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ oṉṟupōl kāṇappaṭṭālum cila iṭaṅkaḷil peyarkaḷ māṟupaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
vētiyal poruḷkaḷ
tamiḻakattiṉ taṉic ciṟappu vāynta poṟkollarkaḷ, aṇikalaṉkaḷaic camaippataṟku oru cila ayalnāṭṭu irācayaṉap poruḷkaḷai upayōkittu varukiṉṟārkaḷ. āṉāl, muṟkālat til ivaṟṟiṟkup patilāka uḷnāṭṭil tayāritta poruṭkaḷaip payaṉpaṭutti vantārkaḷ. ellā iṭaṅkaḷilum iṉṟu poṉṉai urukkak kaṭṭikkārattaip payaṉpaṭutti (Borax) varukiṟārkaḷ.
aṇikalaṉkaḷaic ceytu muṭittapiṉ, avaikaḷait tuppuravu ceyyavum  oḷiyuṇṭākkavum māṅkāy  pēriṅ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum, oru karuviyaip perumpālum upayōkappaṭuttukiṟārkaḷ. ataṉpiṉ uppaiyum upayōkappaṭuttukiṟārkaḷ. piṉ, cālṭ ammōṉiyā (uppu navaccāra āvikkuriyatu), paṭikāram mutaliyavai kaḷai mēlē taṭavi ataṉmītu civapput tūḷait tūvi merukiṭu kiṟārkaḷ. kaṉip poruḷkaḷiṉ kaḷimpaip  pōkkavum turuvai akaṟṟavum kantakakkāṭiyai (calprik āciṭai)p payaṉpaṭuttu kiṟārkaḷ. poṉ pōṉṟa tātup poruḷkaḷil kalantuḷḷa vēṟu tātup poruḷkaḷai akaṟṟip poṉṉait tūymaippaṭuttu vataṟku veṭiyakkāṭiyai (naiṭrik āciṭṭai) upayōkappaṭuttu kiṟārkaḷ. muṟkālattil poṉṉaic cuttappaṭutta ataip puṭammiṭṭu vantaṉar.
puṭamiṭal 
māṟṟuk kuṟainta poṉṉaic cuttikarikka mutalil atait takaṭāka aṭittu appāl atai 3/8 x 2 aṅkulat takaṭukaḷāka aṉaittaiyum oṉṟupōl veṭṭa vēṇṭum. vīṭṭuk kūṟai mēlē pōṭum ōṭṭait tūḷ ceytu atil upput tūḷaik kalakka vēṇṭum. piṉ puḷiyaik karaittu atil takaṭukaḷai naṉṟāka naṉaittu eṭuttu ōṭṭut tūḷil puraṭṭi varicaiyāka oṉṟaṉmītu oṉṟāka aṭukkip pāṉai ōṭṭil 2½ x 4 aṅkula nīḷamuḷḷa iru ōṭṭai mēlum kīḻum vaittuk kavacam ceytu (potintu) periya pāṉaiyil eruvai (varaṭṭiyai) aṭukkip puṭam iṭa vēṇṭum. ivvāṟu mūṉṟu allatu nālu puṭam pōṭṭāl poṉ tūyatāki viṭum. appoḻutu ataṉaic cokkap poṉ allatu cokkat taṅkam eṉpārkaḷ.
paḻuppiṭal 
vēlai muṭinta aṇikalaṉkaḷai, paḻuppiṭa, turucu, uppu, veṭiyuppu ākiyavaikaḷai araittu atil aṇikalaṉkaḷaip pōṭṭup puraṭṭi neruppil vaittuc ciṟitu cūṭēṟṟi taṇṇīril naṉaitteṭuppar. aṇikalaṉkaḷ ellām nalla mañcaḷ niṟamāka ilaṅkum. iḵtaṉṟip palavitap paḻuppukaḷum pōṭuvārkaḷ. paḻuppup pōṭṭa piṉṉarē nakaikaḷukku merukiṭuvārkaḷ.
merukiṭal
merukiṭuvataṟkup paḻuppup pōṭṭa nakaikaḷ mītu oruvitac civapput tūḷait taṭavi appāl nāyttōlāl tuṭaippārkaḷ. ataṉāl aṇikalaṉkaḷukku nalla miṉumiṉuppu eḻum. merukiṭa merukuk kampiyāl naṉṟākat tēyppatu uṇṭu.
kāram
aṇikalaṉkaḷaic ceyya ārampikkumpoḻutu mutalil poṉ takaṭṭaiyō kampiyaiyō nakaiyiṉ aḷaviṟkēṟpa veṭṭi oṉṟōṭoṉṟaip porutti atai ilēcāka oṭṭuvārkaḷ. oṭṭuvataṟku upayōkikkappaṭum pacaiyaik kāram eṉpārkaḷ. kāram, kaṭṭikkāram eṉpaṉa tōl nīkkiya kuṉṟiṉ maṇittūḷ mutaliyavai cērttuc ceyyappaṭṭatākum. itaṉaic cempu ṭappiyil vaittiruppārkaḷ.
poṉṉāl aṇikaḷ ceyyumpoḻutu irutakaṭukaḷai allatu kampikaḷaik kārattiṉāl maṭṭum oṭṭiviṭa muṭiyātu. atu ulaiyil vaippataṟkuc cariyāṉa muṟaiyil amaittup pārppataṟkē payaṉpaṭum. āṉāl, palamāka ētēṉum oru poru ḷāl tākkappaṭṭāl iṇaippup pirintuviṭum. acaiyāmal irutakaṭukaḷ allatu kampikaḷ oṭṭuvataṟkup poṭi vaittal eṉpārkaḷ. iraṇṭu takaṭukaḷukkum iṭaiyē kāram taṭavi poṭi vaittu ūtiviṭṭāl iraṇṭu takaṭukaḷum oṉṟāka iṇaintu viṭum. intap poṭikkuc cuttamāṉa veḷḷi 1 paṅkum atē eṭai cempum cērttu urukki vaittuk koḷvārkaḷ. itaṉai maṭṭam eṉpārkaḷ. taṅkam 3 paṅkum maṭṭum 1 paṅkum cērttu urukkap paṭṭataṟkup poṭi allatu caṉṉam eṉpārkaḷ. cilavaṟṟiṟkuk kūṭutalākavum kuṟaitalākavum cērppatumuṇṭu.
iraṇṭu poṉ takaṭukaḷaiyō poṉ kampikaḷaiyō poṉ tūḷkaḷaik koṇṭu oṭṭiviṭa muṭiyātu. oṭṭuvataṟku mēlē coṉṉa poṭi allatu caṉṉaṅkaḷē vēṇṭum. ōṭṭa vēṇṭiya iraṇṭu poṉ takaṭukaḷ mītum poruttil caṉṉattai vaittu ulaiyiliṭṭu ūtiṉāl caṉṉam uruki iraṇṭu takaṭukaḷai yum oṉṟāka iṇaittuviṭukiṟatu. appāl caṉṉattai arāviyō allatu cīviyō vaittuviṭṭāl inta oṭṭu ciṟitum teriyātu. aṇikalaṉkaḷ vārppuppōl aḻakāy viḷaṅkum. itaṟkup poṭi vaittal allatu caṉṉam vaittal eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum.
tamiḻnāṭṭup poṟkollarkaḷ eṅkum orē vitamāṉa āyutaṅkaḷaiyē payaṉpaṭutti varukiṉṟārkaḷ. 30 āṇṭukaḷāka oru cilar takaṭukaḷai araippatu kampikaḷai iḻuppatu pōṉṟa kāriyaṅ kaḷukku entiraṅkaḷaip payaṉpaṭutti varukiṉṟārkaḷ.
imayantoṭṭuk kumarivarai niṟuppataṟku muḻu rūpāyum ēṉaiya veḷḷik kācukaḷaiyum paṭikkallāka upayōkappaṭuttu kiṟārkaḷ. namatu nāṭṭil ovvoru pakutiyilum ovvoru paṭik kaṟkaḷaip payaṉpaṭutti varukiṉṟārkaḷ. tamiḻakam, pampāy, ākkirā pōṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil oru rūpāy oru tōlāvākap payaṉ paṭutti vantārkaḷ. iṉṉum palaviṭaṅkaḷil kaḷañcikkoṭṭai, mañcāṭi, kuṉṟimaṇi arici pōṉṟavaikaḷai upayōkittup poṉ, eṭaiyaip pārkkiṉṟārkaḷ. cila iṭaṅkil oru mañ cāṭikku eṭṭu arici eṭaiyākak kaṇakkiṭappaṭukiṟatu. ip poḻutu eṅkum orē vitamāṉa eṭai naṭaimuṟaikkuk koṇṭu varappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
iṉippoṟkollarkaḷ poṉṉiṉ māṟṟuk kāṇpa taṟkup poṉṉai uraittup pārkkap perumpālum eṅkum upayōkappaṭuttuvatu uraikallēyākum. itu oru ciṟiya metu vāṉa kaṟuppuk kallāka irukkiṟatu. itil poṉ allatu veḷḷiyai uraittāl atil ataṉ niṟampaṭiyum. atai vaittup poṉ allatu veḷḷiyiṉ māṟṟut tīrmāṉikkappeṟukiṟatu. poṉṉil atikamāṉa cempō veḷḷiyō kalantiruntāl aḷaviṟku ēṟpap poṉ veṇmaiyāṉa niṟaṅkāṭṭum. itaṉ māṟṟait tiṭṭavaṭṭamākak kaṇakkiṭa maccam allatu mātirip poṉkuccum vaittiruppārkaḷ. atai arukil uraittup pārttu māṟṟai uṟutip paṭuttuvārkaḷ.
poṉ aṇikaḷ ceyvataṟkuc cuttamāṉa taṉip poṉ payaṉpaṭātu. eṉavē atōṭu cempu allatu veḷḷi, allatu nākam kalappārkaḷ. evvaḷavu kalappatu eṉpatu ovvoru mānilattiṟkum, ēṉ? - ovvoru māvaṭṭattiṟkum aḷavil vēṟṟumai uṇṭu.
cila nāṭukaḷil ceyyum aṇikalaṉkaḷukku mātiri vait tiruppārkaḷ. cila iṭaṅkaḷil vēṟu tātup poruḷkaḷil mātiri nakai ceytu kāṭṭi, atu aṅkīkarikkappaṭṭa piṉṉarē poṉ aṇi ceyvārkaḷ. tamiḻnāṭṭup poṟkollarkaḷiṭam  mātiri nakaikaḷō, mātirippaṭaṅkaḷō kiṭaiyā; niṉaivilē vaittuk koṇṭē aḻakāka aṇikalaṉkaḷaic ceytu viṭukiṟārkaḷ.
pala nakaikaḷ kaṭṭiyākac ceyyappaṭuvatillai. pukalāka atāvatu takaṭākac ceytu atai accil vaittu aḻutti appāl kottumāṉa vēlaikaḷ ceykiṟārkaḷ. itaṉ uḷḷē meḻuku irukkum. piṉpakkam perumpālum poṟṟakaṭu potiyamāṭ ṭārkaḷ; veḷḷit takaṭē potikiṉṟārkaḷ. pala poṉ aṇi kalaṉkaḷaic ceytu muṭittuk koṇṭu oru ciṟu tuvārattiṉ mūlam uṭpakkam meḻukaic celutti viṭukiṟārkaḷ.
vaṭa intiyāvilum ilaṅkaiyilum poṉ mulām  pūciya aṇikalaṉkaḷai aṇintu varukiṟārkaḷ. tamiḻarkaḷ eṅkirun tālum poṉ mulām pūciya aṇikalaṉkaḷai aṇiya virumpuvatē illai.
āṉāl irupatu āṇṭukaḷāyp poṉ aṇikalaṉ mītu aḻakiya civappu, paccai, nīlam, mañcaḷ, karuppu, ūtā vaṇṇaṅ kaḷil mēl pūccu (eṉāmal) vēlai naṭaipeṟukiṟatu. atai makkaḷiṟ palar virumpukiṉṟārkaḷ. perumpālum inta eṉāmal pūccu atikamāka mōtiraṅkaḷukkē payaṉpaṭukiṟatu.
tamiḻakap poṟ kollarkaḷ aṇikalaṉkaḷ ceyvataṟku mikap paḻamaiyāṉa, ceppamuṟāta karuvikaḷaiyē iṉṟu varaip payaṉpaṭutti varukiṟārkaḷ. āṉāl avarkaḷiṉ kai vaṇṇattāl ulakil enta nāṭṭut toḻilāḷarkaḷ ceyyum aṇikalaṉ kaḷaiyumviṭa mikac ciṟanta aṇikalaṉkaḷaic ceytu viṭukiṟārkaḷ. tamiḻakap poṟkollarkkoppa, ēṉ? avarkaḷukku aṭutta paṭiyil niṟpataṟkukkūṭa ulakil evarum illai. avarkaḷ entira utaviyiṉṟiyē veṅkāyac carukupōṉṟa poṉ takaṭukaḷālum, cilanti valaiyiṉ nūlpōṉṟa poṉ kampikaḷālum aṇikalaṉkaḷ camaittu varukiṉṟārkaḷ. inta paṇpaṭṭa naya nākarikamuṭaiya muṟpaṭṭa kāla muṟaiyil ceyyappaṭṭa nakaikaḷiṉ tiṉucukaḷ naṭai nayattil ataṉuṭaiya ucca nilaiyai eṭṭiyuḷḷatu. iṉṟaiyat tamiḻakap poṟ kollarkaḷ, niṉaivuk keṭṭāta paḻaṅkālattaic cērnta marapiṉ nēraṭiyāṉa vaḻittōṉṟalkaḷ. taṅkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ kaṇṭa muṟaikaḷaiyum, taṉit tiṟattaiyum marapurimaiyākap peṟṟirukkiṟārkaḷ eṉṟu mēṉāṭṭaṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ.12
poṟkollarkaḷ uḻaippāḷar iṉattavarkaḷēyāvar. āṉāl camūkattil oru kauravamāṉa uṟuppiṉarāka iruntu varukiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ tōḷil pūṇūl pōṭṭu irukkiṟārkaḷ. taṭṭār (poṟkollar) eṉṟa oru iṉam pallāṇṭākat tamiḻakattil iruntu varukiṟatu. āṉāl tamiḻnāṭu campantappaṭṭa maṭṭil vēṟu iṉattavarkaḷ ittoḻilil atikam īṭupaṭṭiruppa tākat teriyavillai. vēṟu mānilaṅkaḷil piṟa iṉattavarkaḷ itai oru matippāṉa toḻilāka eṇṇi itil īṭupaṭṭiruk kiṉṟaṉar.
poṟkollarkaḷ vaḻakkamākap paṇṭutoṭṭu ittoḻi lil nāṇayamāka naṭappatillai eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭu varukiṟatu. namnāṭṭil itaippaṟṟip pala paḻamoḻikaḷum uṇṭu. taṭṭāṉuk kañciyaṉṟō aṇintāṉ civaṉ carppattaiyē eṉṟa pulavar vākkum eḻuntuḷḷatu. taṭṭāṉ tāyp poṉṉilum māp poṉ eṭuppāṉ eṉṟum kammāḷaṉ kaṭāvaik kātaṟuttup pārttālum cevvarakkup pāyntirukkum, eṉṟellām paḻa moḻikaḷ uṇṭu. itaip pōṉṟa paḻamoḻikaḷ vaṭa intiyā vilum āṅkila nāṭṭilum uṇṭu. iṉṟu inta nilai māṟi varukiṟatu eṉṟa kūṟalām. poṟ kollarkaḷ kuṟitta kālattil aṇikalaṉ kaḷaic ceytu koṭukkātu nāḷkaṭattip pukal colli varuvatum poṉṉōṭu aḷaviṟkatikamākac cempaik kalantu makkaḷai ēmāṟṟuvatum, cētārattiṉ pērāl, niṟuttal aḷavaiyiṉ pērāl makkaḷai ēyppatum paṟṟip pala kataikaḷ nāṭeṅkum  kūṟappaṭuva tuṇṭu. ivaikaḷ kālap pōkkil māṟi varukiṉṟaṉa. ittoḻi lāḷikaḷ mītu pala kuṟṟañcāṭṭap paṭṭiruppatu meytāṉ. āṉāl avarkaḷ iṉṟum vaṟumaiyilēyē vāṭi vataṅkukiṟārkaḷ; avarkaḷ nilai uyaravillai. taṭṭiṉāl taṭṭāṉ taṭṭāviṭil keṭṭāṉ eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
poṟkollarkaḷiṉ viṉainuṭpam
iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē atāvatu kiṟittava ūḻi arumpuvataṟku muṉpē tamiḻnāṭṭup poṟ kollarkaḷ mikunta nuṭpamāṉa toḻil tiṟaṉ vāyntavarkaḷāy viḷaṅkiṉar. tamiḻ nāṭṭup poṟkollarkaḷ pacum poṉṉait tamatu kūriya uḷiyālum, katirālum koṭi, ilai, pū, naṇṭu, makaram, tavaḷai pōṉṟa nēriya palvēṟu vaṭivam peṟṟilaṅkumāṟu ceytaṉar. avarkaḷ aṇikaḷai aṭittum arāviyum aṟuttum  kottu māṉaṅkaḷ ceytum aḻakuṟac ceytu muṭittaṉar. muttukaḷaiyum, maṇikaḷaiyum tuḷaiyiṭṭaṉar. vairam pōṉṟa uṟuti vāynta maṇiyilum ciṟiya tuḷaikaḷaiyiṭṭaṉar. cāṇaipiṭittaṉar. palvēṟu vaṇṇam peṟappaṭṭai tīṭṭiṉar. maṇikaḷiṉ mācu nīkkiṉar. muttukaḷum maṇikaḷum tuḷaiyiṭap peṟṟataiyum, cāṇaikkal payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭataiyum,
	tōḷā maṇikuvittāṟ pōṉṟilaṅku tolkulattu
eṉṟa cintāmaṇic ceyyuḷ naṉku viḷakkukiṉṟatu. atōṭu maṇikaḷ ciṟanta uruvattaiyum vaṇṇattaiyum peṟac cāṇai piṭikkappaṭṭatu. toṉmaiyāṉa kālattilē tamiḻakattil cāṇai piṭikkum kal iruntatu eṉpatai
	ciṟukā rōṭaṉ payiṉoṭu cērttiya
	 kalpōl piriyalam 	             (akam : 1 : 5-6; 356 : 9- 10)
eṉṟa akanāṉūṟṟu aṭikaḷ meyppikkiṉṟaṉa. aṇikalaṉkaḷai mācu nīkkak kolli eṉṟa karuvi payaṉpaṭuttap peṟṟatu. itaṉaip peruṅkatai,
	matimācu kaḻīiya vaṇṇam pōlak
	 katirmēl ilaṅkak kaiviṉai muṭittapiṉ
	 aṭiviṉaik kammiyar veṭipaṭa aṭukkiya
	 uyarnalak kōlattu oḷḷoḷi tikaḻa
	 vakaiyamai kolliyiṉ vacaiyaṟat tuṭaittu 									           (peruṅ. 2:4:181-185)
paḻantamiḻakattup poṟkollarkaḷ oru aricip paruma ṉuḷḷa poṉṉil civaperumāṉiṉ tiru uruvattaic cemmaiyuṟac cetukki eḻil peṟac ceytuḷḷaṉar. avarkaḷ toḻil nuṭpam maṉṉarkaḷum aṟiñarkaḷum makkaḷum kaṇṭu viyantu pārāṭṭaṟ kuriyatāy iruntatu.
poṟ kaṭṭiyai melliya cilanti valaiyiṉ iḻai pōl - mayiriṉum melliyatāyc ceytu aṇikaḷ ceytaṉar. poṟṟa kaṭṭai aṭittu īruḷḷic carukiṉum melliyatākac ceytu atil aṇikalaṉkaḷ ākkiṉar. oru ciṟu irākkuṭiyil civaperumāṉ tiripuram erinta kataiyai ciṟitum piṉṉamiṉṟit tīṭṭiṉar.
mācillāta maṇikaḷaik kaṇṭeṭuttu avaṟṟaip poṉ aṇiyil patippittu palvēṟu ilaikaḷum pūkkaḷum, kāykaḷum ilaṅkumpaṭi ceytu vantaṉar. ivaikaḷil naṉku avarkaḷ toḻil nuṭpam viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu.
āṉāl, ivarkaḷ ōraḷavutāṉ piṟitoṉṟaip pārttup piṉpaṟṟāta muṟaiyil ceyya muṭikiṟatu. ivarkaḷiṭam putiya mātirikaḷai uruvākkikkāṭṭum entak kalaiñarkaḷum iruppa tākat teriyavillai. airōppāvaippōl kalaiñarkaḷ ivarkaḷ mātirikaḷaiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷaiyum varaintō uruvam amaittō koṭuppatillai. airōppāvil poṉ aṇiyil ceyya vēṇṭiya aṇikalaṉkaḷukku, kalaiñarkaḷ, paṭam, uruvam, eṭai, māṟṟu, vilaikaḷ mutaliyavaikaḷaiyellām pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka amaittuk koṭuttu varukiṉṟārkaḷ. airōppāvil kalaiñarkaḷē putiya putiya pāṇiyil ellā nakai kaḷaiyum uruvākki varukiṉṟārkaḷ. aṅkuḷḷa poṟkollarkaḷ avarkaḷiṉ tiṭṭappaṭi entirampōl aṇikalaṉkaḷaic ceytu koṭuttu varukiṟārkaḷ. tamiḻakattil aṇikalaṉkaḷaiyum, paṭimaṅ kaḷaiyum uruvākkat  tiṭṭamiṭum kalaiñarkaḷum uruvākkum toḻilāḷikaḷum oruvarēyākum. tamiḻakap poṉ aṇikala vēlai enta nāṭṭaiyum viṭa, cari etirppuk kūṟiṉiṉṟu māṟupaṭṭatāy piṟar kaṇkaḷāl kavaṉikkum poḻutu uḷḷattiṉ niṉaiviṉiṉṟu veḷivarac ceyyum kalaiyuṇarcciyuṭaiya paṭṭaṟi vāka miḷirkiṟatu. tamiḻk kalaiyiṉ uṭkaruttum kuṟikkōḷum taṉippaṭṭaṉavākum. muṟaiyum  vēṟāṉatākum. nōkkamum tamiḻakak kalaiyum, pāṇiyum aḻaku uṇarcci cārnta aṉupavattōṭu karuttuk koṇṭirukkavillai. mikac ciṟanta uṭkaruttu koṇṭa tāka eṇṇappaṭṭa mokalāya pāṇiyaikkūṭa tamiḻakap paṇi taḻuva maṟuttuviṭṭatu. āriyap pāṇiyaikkūṭa utariviṭṭatu.
kuṟippukaḷ
1.	“The objects found comprised gold, bronze, and iron articles and pottery. The gold articls which were probably used as diadems, vary in size and are oval in shape; some have a stripe extending beyond the two extremities with a small hole for a wire or string at each end. They are thin plates ornamented with triangular and linear dotted designs and all were found folded up in a manner which suggest that some symbolical meaning may have been attached to the practice” - Catalogue of Prehistoric Antiquities, Adichanallur and Prumbair by Alexander Rea. F.S.A. (Scot) P. 3-4
2.	arumaippāṭuṭaiya viṉaittiṟam amaiyap poṉṉiṉāl palavakai aṇikalaṅkaḷaic ceypavar poṟkollar eṉappaṭuvar. poṉṉilē nalla maṇikaḷaip patikkum iyalpaṟintu aṇikalaṉkaḷamaikkum irattiṉap paṇittaṭṭārkaḷum tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ peruka vāḻntārkaḷ. tirumaṇi kuyiṟṟunar eṉpār muttukkōppavarāvar caṅka kālat tamiḻ makkaḷ - tiru. ka. veḷḷaivāraṇaṉ (1950) pa. 133
3.	The five classes of artisans are :-
	1. Manu, Kollar ........... (kollar) Black - smiths.
	2. Maya, Taher (taccar) ............................ Carpentars.
	3. Tuvashta, Kannar (kaṉṉār) Brass - founders.
	4. Silpa Sirpi (á‰ã)...................... Mason, Stonecutters, sculptors, Architects, Immage makers and Painters.
	5. Visvanna, Tattar (j£lh®)...................... Goldsmiths.
	These firve artisans were born out of the five faces of Visvakarma - (Foot Note:) Ancient Jaffna. P. 15
