12.	This elegant primitive form of ornament probably reached its limits for delicacy and design at a very arehaic period. The Hindu artisan of our day inherits the methods and skill he uses by direct descent of immemorial tradition” - Report on jewellery and precious stones in the French Exhibition of 1866.”	- by Mr. Maskelyne.

7. oṉpatumaṇikaḷ (navarattiṉaṅkaḷ)
aṇikalaṉkaḷum maṇikaḷum
tamiḻ makkaḷ ātiyil malar aṇikaḷai aṇintu vantārkaḷ. appāl kuṉṟimaṇi, mañcāṭi, kaḷaṅcik koṭṭai, miḷaku, kottamalli, nellikkāy, marutaṅkāy, kaḷimaṇ uruṇṭai, mākkalmaṇi, ceṅkalmaṇi, cippimālai, caṅkumālai pōṉṟavai kaḷai aṇintu vantaṉar. avarkaḷ tātupporuḷkaḷaik kaṇṭa tum poṉ, veḷḷi, cempu, veṇkalam pōṉṟa tātup poruḷ kaḷil aṇikalaṉkaḷai amaittu vantaṉar - appāl maṇikaḷ iḻaitta poṉ aṇikaḷai aṇintu vantaṉar. tamiḻarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷāṉa arappā makkaḷ aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē poṉ aṇikaḷaiyum maṇikaḷ patitta poṉ, veḷḷi aṇikaḷaiyum pūṇṭiruntaṉar eṉpatu intiya varalāṟu kūṟum uṇmai. āṉāl tamiḻaka makkaḷ iṟṟaikku mūvāyiram āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉpē maṇikaḷ iḻaitta poṉ aṇikaḷaip pūṇ ṭuḷḷārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. kaṭaic caṅkak kālattiṟku muṉṉar tamiḻakattil ērāḷamāṉa poṉ aṇikaḷ nilaviyiruk kalām eṉṟu kūṟuvataṟku illai. kiṟittava ūḻi arumpiya kālattiliruntu tamiḻakattil poṉ, veḷḷi aṇikalaṉkaḷum maṇikaḷ patitta aṇikalaṉkaḷum arumpi viṭṭaṉa eṉṟu collalām. itaṟku nām kalveṭṭuc cāṉṟukaḷum ilakkiyac cāṉṟukaḷum kāṭṭamuṭiyum. kiṟittava ūḻiyil nilaviya poṉ aṇikaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷai nammāl eṭuttuk kāṭṭa muṭiyātu. ēṉeṉil iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar tamiḻarkaḷ vāḻnta nakaraṅkaḷ etuvum putai poruḷ ārāyccit tuṟaiyiṉa rāl akaḻntu ārāyappaṭṭu avaikaḷil aṇikalaṉkaḷō pommaikaḷō kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭu ārāycci ceyyappaṭṭiruppa tāka nām aṟiyavillai; kēḷvippaṭavum illai. āticcanallūr akaḻvārāycciyil atikamāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷ kiṭaikkavillai.
kiṟittava ūḻikkup piṉ eḻunta ilakkiyaṅkaḷil pala cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷ aṇiyap peṟṟu vantaṉa. ataṉāl poṉṉaip paṟṟi ārāyap peṟṟatu. maṇikaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. maṇikaḷiṉ uyarvum kuṟṟamum atai merukiṭum muṟaiyum paṭṭan tīrkkum vaḻikaḷum eḻuntaṉa. maṇi vakaikaḷum avaṟṟaip paṟṟiya ārāycciyum ilakkaṇaṅ kaḷum arumpiṉa. poṉṉaiviṭa maṇikaḷukku matippu uyarntatu.
maṇikaḷ eṅkellām kiṭaikkum eṉpatu ārāyap peṟṟatu. periya maṇikaḷ veḷināṭukaḷil iruntellām koṇṭu varap peṟṟaṉa. vilai matikka voṇṇāta maṇikaḷ pala, ulakil kiṭaittaṉa. maṇikaḷ aṇikaḷil maṭṭum iṭam peṟavillai. vēṟu pala kāriyaṅkaḷukkum payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. maṇikaḷiṉ teyva uruvaṅkaḷ kūṭa ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.  uyarnta maṇikaḷai aṭaivataṟ kāka maṉṉarkaḷ oruvarukkoruvar pōriṭṭuk koṇṭatum uṇṭu. maṉṉarkaḷum vaṇikarkaḷum uyarnta maṇikaḷukku atika vilai koṭuttu vantaṉar. maṉṉarkaḷum perum vaṇikarkaḷum vilaiyuyarnta maṇikaḷ iḻaitta poṉ aṇikaḷai teyvaṅ kaḷukkuk kāṇikkaiyāka aḷittu vantaṉar.
namatu ilakkiyaṅkaḷil (1) vacciram (2) intira nīlam (3) marakatam (4) karkētaṉam (5) patumarākam (6) uruttirākkam (7) vaiṭūriyam 
(8) vipulam  (9) vimalakam (10) irācamaṇi (11) paṭikam (12) cantira kāntam (13) caukantikam (14) kōmētakam (15) caṅkam (16) makānīlam (17) puṭparākam (18) piramamaṇi (19) cōtiracam (20) cīvayakam (21) muttu (22) pavaḷam eṉṟu palavakai maṇikaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉṟu mēṉāṭṭil nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa maṇivakaikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. atil tamiḻ nāṭṭār maṇikaḷil ciṟantavaṟṟaip poṟukki eṭuttu avaikaḷai maṭṭum payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar. avai navarattiṉam - oṉpatu maṇikaḷ ākum. navamaṇikaḷil iraṇṭu nīril viḷaivaṉa; ēḻu nilattil viḷaivaṉa. nīril viḷaivaṉa muttu, pavaḷam ākiya iraṇṭē ākum. nilattil viḷaivaṉa vairam, marakatam, māṇikkam, puṭparākam, vaiṭūriyam, nīlam, kōmētakam eṉum ēḻum ākum.
ilakkiyaṅkaḷil maṇikaḷ
mikat toṉmaiyāṉa kālattilē, maṇi ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ pala, tamiḻil iruntaṉa. iṉṟu avaikaḷil oṉṟaik kūṭak kāṇa muṭiya villai. tamiḻar kaṇṭa aṟupattu nāṉku kalaikaḷil maṇic cōtaṉai (iratṉap parīṭcai)yum oṉṟu. iṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyil irattiṉap parīṭcai eṉṟu oru nūl irukkiṟatu. ataṉ tamiḻākkam namakku iṉṟu kiṭaittuḷḷatu. tiruviḷaiyāṭaṟ purāṇattil maṇikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cilappatikārattilum  ataṉ ciṟappuraikaḷilum maṇikaḷaip paṟṟiya viḷakkaṅkaḷ kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. kallāṭam, piḷḷait tamiḻ pōṉṟa nūṟkaḷilum navamaṇikaḷaip paṟṟiya viḷakkaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
maṇikaḷiṉ nalaṉkaḷ
namatu maruttuva nūṟkaḷilum cōtiṭa nūṟkaḷilum maṇi kaḷiṉ perumaiyaiyum iṉṉa ilakkaṉattil piṟantōrkku iṉṉa maṇi aṇintāl nalam viḷaiyum eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. maruttuva nūṟkaḷil maṇikaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa pamaṅkaḷ piṇikaḷai nīkkum viparaṅkaḷ viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷ aṇivatum, poṟkalaṅkaḷil pucippatum iṉṉiṉṉa nōykaḷai akaṟṟum eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. maṇikaḷ iḻaitta poṉ aṇi kalaṉkaḷai aṇivatāl pala nōykaḷ akalvatōṭu poṉṉum maṇikaḷum kūṭi iraṭṭippāṉa nalaṉkaḷaic ceykiṉṟaṉa; nōy akalkiṟatu; uṭal palam peṟukiṟatu eṉpatu viḷakkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
mēṉāṭṭu viññāṉa viṟpaṉṉarkaḷ maṇikaḷaip paṟṟi ārāyntu pala moḻikaḷil nūṟkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ. maṇi kaḷaip paṟṟiya āyvut tuṟaiyai mēṉāṭṭār jemmālēji (Gemology) eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. mēṉāṭṭār maṇikaḷ ēṉ kaṉamākavum uṟutiyākavum irukkiṉṟaṉa? maṇikaḷiṉūṭē pukum oḷikkatir ēṉ vēṟupāṭuṭaiyatāy viḷaṅkukiṟatu? eṉpataiyum iṉṟu mika nuṇukkamāka āyntuḷḷārkaḷ. oḷi ārāyccit tuṟaiyil ulakap perumpukaḻ īṭṭiya tamiḻnāṭṭu viññāṉa viṟpaṉṉar car.ci.vi. irāmaṉ maṇikaḷiṉ  oḷikaḷaip paṟṟi oppaṟṟa ārāycci ceytuḷḷār.
maṇikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇam
navamaṇikaḷ aṟiñarkaḷ maṇikaḷai, talai, iṭai, kaṭai, eṉṟu mūṉṟu taramākap pirippar. talaimaṇikaḷ vairam, muttu, marakatam, māṇikkam eṉṉum nāṉkum ākum. iṭaimaṇikaḷ nīlam, puṭparākam, vaiṭūriyam eṉum mūṉṟum ākum. kaṭai maṇikaḷ kōmētakam, pavaḷam eṉṉum iraṇṭum ākum. ivvakaiyaṉṟi navamaṇikaḷai, makārattiṉam uparattiṉam eṉṟum pirippar.
navamaṇikaḷil tamiḻnāṭṭu muttukkaḷum intiya nāṭṭu vairamum ulakap pukaḻ peṟṟavai. iṉṟu āṅkila nāṭṭu araciyiṉ maṇimuṭiyiliruntu aṇi ceyvatu intiya nāṭṭil viḷainta kōkiṉūr eṉṉum ulakap piracitti peṟṟa vairamaṇiyākum. kiṟittava ūḻi eḻum kālattilē pāṇṭiya nāṭṭu muttukkaḷ urōm nāṭṭu aracikaḷiṉ maṇimuṭiyilum paṭṭāṭaiyilum, oḷi vīciyatōṭu avarkaḷiṉ melliya uṭalaiyum aṇi ceytu niṉṟaṉa. kiḷiyōpattirā eṉṟa ekiptiya araciyiṉ uḷḷattaik kavarntu niṉṟatu koṟkai muttēyākum. avvaraci koṟkai muttai, āṭaiyil piṇaittum aṇikaḷil iṇaittum karaittuk kuṭittum vantāḷ. iṉṟum ceyappūr marakatam cekameṅkum pukaḻ parappi varukiṟatu. oru kālattil tamiḻ nāṭṭup pavaḷam veḷi nāṭṭiṟkellām ceṉṟu namatu nāṭṭiṟkup perumai viḷaivittu vantatu.
talaimaṇikaḷ
vairam (Diamond)
ulakam tōṉṟip pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷ ākiṉṟaṉa. muṟkālattil, kālakkōḷāl pūmiyiṉ mītuḷḷa maraṅkaḷ vīḻntu ataṉ aṭiyil putaiyuṇṭu pōyiṉa. pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷākap pūmiyiṉuḷ putaintu kiṭanta inta maraṅkaḷ ceṟivuṟṟu nilakkariyāy māṟiyatu. itaṉōṭu mēlum mēlum maṇṇum kallum cērntu putaiyuṇṭa nilakkariyaip palavita māṟutalkaḷukku āḷākkiyatu. mēlē irunta poruḷkaḷ atai aḻutti vantaṉa. uḷḷirukkum veppam atai atikamākat tākkiyatu. kariya niṟam vāynta nilakkari veṇmai niṟam peṟṟatu. karikkuṉṟu vairak kuṉṟāy miḷirntatu.
viññāṉa viḷakkam
vairattai viññāṉikaḷ paricōtittup pārttaṉar. atu karipporuḷāl āṉatē eṉṟu avarkaḷiṉ cōtaṉaiyil kaṇṭupiṭikkap peṟṟatu. karipporuḷai urukkip pārttaṉar. atu vairamāka oḷi vīciyatu. uṭaṉē ulakiṟku taṅkaḷatu kaṇṭupiṭippai veḷiyiṭṭaṉar.
vairam amilaṅkaḷil (tirāvakaṅkaḷil) karaiyātu; cātāraṇac cūṭṭiṉāl nekiḻātu; atikamāṉa cūṭēṟṟiṉālum atu iḷakātu. āṉāl taṉṉantaṉiyāṉa pirāṇavāyuviṉ cuvālaiyaik koṇṭu atait tākkiṉāl erintu pōkiṟatu. appoḻutu vairattiṉiṉṟu kariyamila vāyu  oṉṟu tāṉ kiṭaikkum. itaṉāl vairamāṉatu taṉik karipporuḷē tavira vēṟaṉṟu eṉat tiṭṭa vaṭṭamākak kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. aṭuppuk kari, nilakkari ākiyavaikaḷai viṭa nalla kalappaṟṟa karip poruḷaik kuṟukkiṉatu tāṉ vairam eṉpatu.
marakkari, nilakkari, kārīyakam (Graphite) vairam ākiyavai aṉaittum karipporuḷiṉ uruvamāṟupāṭukaḷēyaṉṟi vēṟalla. ipporuḷkaḷiṉ pautīkak kuṇaṅkaḷ māṟupaṭṭaṉavāyk kāṇappaṭṭālum aṉaittum karipporuḷākiya irācāyaṉap poruḷēyākum. eṉavē ivaṟṟiṉ irācāyaṉa kuṇaṅkaḷum amaippum oṉṟāka amaintuḷḷaṉa. karipporuḷiṉ tiṇmai yāṉatu nāḷaṭaivil ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaiviṭa maṟṟoṉṟil atika mākik koṇṭē vantu iṟutiyil uṟutiyāṉatum veṇmai vāyntatum oḷimikkatumāṉa vairam mikuntu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.2
irattiṉak kaṟkaḷiṉ (maṇikaḷiṉ) amaippu muṟai palavakai yākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. oru irattiṉak kallukkum maṟṟoru irattiṉak kallukkum atikamāṉa vēṟupāṭu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. vairattiṉ irācayaṉa amaippō cātāraṇamāṉatu. cuttamāṉa veṟuṅkarip poruḷ oṉṟaik koṇṭē vairam amaintiruk kiṟatu. māṇikkattil iraṇṭu mūlaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. cila irattiṉak kaṟkaḷ pala mūlaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉavāy amaintirukkiṉṟaṉa.
vairattiṉ taram
vairam uyarntatā, kuṟṟamaṟṟatā? eṉṟu pārkkac cila cōtaṉaikaḷ uṇṭu. vairattiṉmītu puḷi, uppu ivaikaḷai araittup pūci, pātaracattil pōṭṭu eṭuttāl nalla vairam niṟam māṟātu irukkum. ceyaṟkai vairam niṟam māṟiviṭum.
vairak kaṟkaḷiṉ ciṟappu ataṉ oḷiyiṉaik kōṭac ceyyum caktiyēyākum. atāvatu vairattiṉvaḻi ūṭuruvic cellum oḷi irēkaiyāṉatu ēṉaiya poruḷkaḷiṉ mūlam ūṭuruvic cellum poḻutu cātāraṇamākak kōṭuvataik kāṭṭilum atikamākak kōṭukiṟatu. atāvatu oḷik kiraṇam vairattiṉ mēṟ paṭuvataṉ muṉ entap pakkattai nōkkic ceṉṟu koṇṭiruntatō antap pakkattai viṭṭu atikam vilaki atu, vairattiṉiṉṟu veḷiyē varum poḻutu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ivvāṟu amainta oḷikkōṭum caktiyāṉatu ovvoru poruḷilum ataṉ tiṇmaik kēṟpak kuṟaintum niṟaintum kāṇappaṭum. tiṇmai niṟainta vairattil itu niṟaivākat tikaḻkiṟatu. inta oḷikōṭum tiṟamaiyiṉ niṟaiviṉāltāṉ vairattiṟku oppaṟṟa oḷiyum ariya cōpaiyum uyariya kāntiyum uṇṭākiṟatu. itaṉāl vairam taṉiyāka oḷirntu ciṟantu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. kuṟṟamaṟṟa vairattiṉ mītu atilum naṉṟākap paṭṭan tīrttup paḷapaḷaveṉṟu miṉṉum vairattiṉ mītu oḷik kiraṇam oṉṟupaṭṭuk kōṭumpoḻutu vairam takatakaveṉṟu kaṇṇaip paṟippatupōl oḷi vīcukiṟatu.
cōtiṭa muṟaippaṭi pārttāl, vairam cukkiraṉ, ātikkat tiṟku uṭpaṭṭatu. iṭapa lakkaṉattil allatu tulālakkaṉattil piṟantavarkaḷukku vairam atirṣṭam tarum maṇiyākum. cātakattil cukkiraṉ palam uḷḷavaṉāka iruntālum vairattai aṇiyalām. kaurava uyarvu, akaṉṟa aṟivu, taṉatu tēvai niṟaivu ākiyavai vairam aḷikkum teyvīkat taṉmai, nalla vairattiṟku uṇṭu. atu āyuḷai nīṭikkat tuṇai ceyyum. paittiyam tīravum pāmpu kaṭi mutaliyavaikaḷukku āḷākāmal kākkum taṉmaiyum vairat tiṟku uṇṭu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
kuṟṟamaṟṟa vairamaṇi kiṭaippatu aritu. ulakap pukaḻpeṟṟa vairaṅkaḷilum kuṟṟaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. pukaḻ peṟṟa vairamākiya kōkiṉūrilum kūṭa kuṟṟaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. veṇmai, mañcaḷ, poṭi niṟa nīlam, veḷiṟiya civappu niṟa vairaṅkaḷ kiṭaippatu aritu.
intiya vairamaṇikaḷ 
pārata nāṭṭiluḷḷa kōlkoṇṭā, paṉṉā, vacrakuram pōṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil vairaṅkaḷ muṟkālattil kiṭaittaṉa. vintiya malaiccāralil vairappaṭikaṅkaḷ vaḷam peṟṟiruntaṉa. 3 taṭcaṇa pīṭa pūmiyiṉ kiḻakkup pakkattilum vairac curaṅkaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. kiruṭṉā āṟṟup pirāntiyaṅkaḷil varalāṟṟup pukaḻ vāynta vairak kaṟkaḷ kiṭaittaṉa. paḻaiya kālattil kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭaic cārnta kōlār eṉṉum iṭattil uyarnta vairaṅkaḷ kiṭaittu vantaṉa. ippoḻutu intiyāvil atikamāṉa vairaṅkaḷ kiṭaikka villai. pūmikkaṭiyiluḷḷa vairac curaṅkaṅkaḷai kaṇṭupiṭikka intiya aracāṅkam cōviyat nipuṇarkaḷiṉ tuṇai koṇṭu perum muyaṟci ceytu varukiṟatu.
veḷi nāṭukaḷil vairam
pirēcil nāṭṭil miṉācukiraicuṭai meṇṭiṉāriyō apēṭ eṉṟa pakutikaḷil vairac curaṅkaṅkaḷ perum aḷavil kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pākiyā eṉṟa pakutiyil 54 āyiram kāraṭ vairam kiṭaittatu.
iṉṟu āppirikkā vaira uṟpattiyil pirēcil nāṭṭait tāṇṭi viṭṭatu. 1906ām āṇṭil ulaka vaira uṟpatti 90 catavikitam teṉṉāppirikkāvil iruntatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭatu. kimparli, ṭīpircu, rōṭēciyā, lūṭaricu virikuṭā pōṉṟa pakuti kaḷil nalla vairac curaṅkaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. aṅku ērāḷamāṉa vairaṅkaḷ tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟu amerikkā vilum cōviyat yūṉiyaṉilum vairaṅkaḷ kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa.
ulakap pukaḻ vāynta vairaṅkaḷ
oru kālattil intiya vairaṅkaḷ ulakap pukaḻ vāyntavai kaḷāy, varalāṟṟil iṭam peṟṟavaikaḷāy miḷirntaṉa. 1726-ām āṇṭuvarai ulakil uḷḷa vairac curaṅkaṅkaḷil kōlkoṇṭā curaṅkaṅkaḷē cīrum ciṟappum peṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉa. intiya vairaṅkaḷ veḷuṟiya nīla niṟattuṭaṉ uṟuti mikkaṉavāy oḷirntaṉa. intiya vairaṅkaḷ vilai matikkap peṟṟavaikaḷāy viḷaṅkiṉa.
1. kōkiṉūr vairamaṇi
intiya mokal aracar, cājakāṉiṭam ērāḷamāṉa vaira maṇikaḷ iruntaṉa. turuva naṭcattirattaiviṭa oḷitarum oppaṟṟa vairaṅkaḷ avariṭam uḷḷaṉa. atai avar poṉṉāl ceyta oru kuttu viḷakkil patittu anta viḷakkai makkamā nakariluḷḷa puṉitap paḷḷikkuk kāṇikkaiyāka aḷittār. akkālattil itaṉ vilai oru kōṭi rūpāykku matippiṭap paṭṭatu. ivvaracariṭamirunta vairam, māṇikkam pōṉṟa maṇikaḷ patitta taṅkattiṉālāṉa mayilācaṉam aṉṟu 53 kōṭi rūpāykku matippiṭap peṟṟatu. ivvaracaruṭaiya karuvūlat til 1663ām āṇṭil makā mokal eṉṟa vairamaṇi oṉṟu iruntatām. atu 787½ kāraṭ eṭaiyuḷḷatu. ki.pi. 1526ām āṇṭil pēpar eṉṟa mokalāya maṉṉariṭam anta maṇi iruntatu. inta ariya periya vairamaṇi 1739ām āṇṭu varai tilli mokalāya cultāṉkaḷiṉ karuvūlattil iṭam peṟṟiruntatu. appāl itai pāracīka vīraṉāṉanātar eṉṟa maṉṉaṉ kavarntu koṇṭu pōṉāṉ. appāl atu mīṇṭum intiyāvil irañcit ciṅ eṉṟa maṉṉariṭam vantatu. avariṭamiruntu kiḻakkintiya kampeṉiyār paṟittuk koṇṭārkaḷ. piṉ piriṭṭīcu araci vikṭōriyāviṭam oppaṭaikkappaṭṭu, piriṭṭīcu maṉṉarkaḷiṉ maṇimuṭiyil iṭam peṟṟu iṉṟaḷavum iruntu varukiṟatu. aṉṟu 186 meṭrik kāraṭ eṭaiyāka irunta immāmaṇi palvēṟu kāraṇaṅkaḷāl kuṟaikkappaṭṭu 1862ām āṇṭu 108-9 meṭrik kāraṭ eṭaiyuḷḷatāy paṭṭan tīṭṭappeṟṟatu. iṉṟu piriṭṭīcu araci elicapet ammaiyār avarkaḷiṉ maṇimuṭiyil ilaṅki varukiṟatu6  itaṉ vilai pattāṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ 60,00,000 rūpāyāka matippiṭappaṭṭatu. inta vairam āṇkaḷ aṇintāl turatirṣṭa mum peṇkaḷ aṇintāl nalla  viḷaivum uṇṭākum eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. inta vairam piriṭṭicu makkaḷ intiyāvai aṭimaippaṭutti atai nīṇṭa kālamākac curaṇṭi vantatiṉ niṉaivākap piriṭṭicu maṇimakuṭattil iṭam peṟṟirukkiṟatu. itu intiyāviṉ cottu. iṉṟalla eṉṟākilum nām allatu namatu varuṅkāla makkaḷ mīṇṭum ataippeṟa vēṇṭum eṉpatu eṉatu ācai. intiya mātāviṉ viruppamum atutāṉ. kōkiṉūr eṉṟāl malaiyiṉ oḷi eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟappaṭum.
2. mokal vairam 
intiyāvil āṭci purinta mokalāya maṉṉar cājakāṉ kōkiṉūr eṉṉum vairamaṇiyuṭaṉ vaittup pōṟṟi vanta ciṟanta vaira maṇikaḷil kuṟippiṭattakka perumai vāynta perum maṇi kaḷil mokal vairamaṇiyum oṉṟākum. cājakāṉ  kālattiṟkup piṉ immā maṇi ālaṅkīr eṉṉum auraṅkacīp aracariṭam pōyviṭṭatu. itu uyaramākavum vaṭṭa vaṭivamākavum nuṉi ciṟuttum aṭi akaṉṟum aḻakiya vimāṉam pōl viḷaṅkiyatu. itu kōkiṉūr vairattiṉiṉṟu veṭṭi eṭukkappaṭṭatu eṉṟu eṇṇuvārum uṇṭu. itaṉ eṭai 280 - kāraṭ iṟuti yil ivvaira maṇi uruciya nāṭṭiṟkup pōyviṭṭatu. uruciyāviṉ muṉṉāḷ āṭci purinta jār cakkaravarttiyiṉāl mika arumaiyāṉa maṇi eṉṟu pōṟṟappaṭṭu vantatu. immaṇi ippoḻutu iṉṟuḷḷa cōviyat aracāṅkat tiṭam iruppatākavum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
3. ārlāp vairamaṇi
ārlāp allatu ṭāriyā nūr maṇi (Derya Noor) āṟṟiṉ oḷi eṉṟu pukaḻpeṟṟu viḷaṅkiyatu. inta aḻakiya vairamaṇi mokalāya maṉṉar pēpariṭam iruntatu. appāl atu maicūril uḷḷa cirireṅka paṭṭiṉattil uḷḷa teyva uruviṉ kaṇṇil patippikkappaṭṭatām. piṉṉar atai 18ām nūṟṟāṇṭil evarō tiruṭik koṇṭu pōyviṭṭaṉar. piṟaku atu eppaṭiyō oru āṅkilak kappalil uḷḷa kappittāṉ kaikkup pōyiṟṟu. avaṉ atai ārlāḵp eṉṉum uruciya iḷavaracaṉukku 90,000m  pavuṇukku viṟṟu viṭṭāṉ. avviḷavaracaṉ uruciya araci kātaraiṉukkup paricāka aḷittu akamakiḻntāṉ. anta vairamaṇi ip poḻutu cōviyat uruciyāvil irukkiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ataṉ eṭai 199.6 meṭrik kāraṭ inta maṇi mañcaḷ niṟattuṭaṉ cōviyat uruciya makuṭak kaṟkaḷiṉ naṭuvē oḷi vīcik koṇṭiruntatu. itaṉ vilai matippu rū. 50,00,000-kku mēl irukkum. itaṟku āmcuṭar ṭēm, lācurev eṉṟa peyarkaḷum uṇṭu. ikkal māṇpuṟa oḷivīcap paṭṭantīrkkappeṟṟuḷḷatu.
4. piṭvairamaṇi
piṭ allatu rīcaṇṭ eṉṟu aḻaikkap peṟum aḻakiya vairamaṇi 1701ām āṇṭu āntira nāṭṭil kiruṭṇā āṟṟaṅ karaiyil kiṭaittatu. aṉṟu atu 410 meṭrik kāraṭ eṭai yuḷḷatāy iruntatu. appoḻutu ceṉṉaik kavarṉarāka irunta villiyam piṭ eṉpavar atai vilaikku vāṅkiṉār. appoḻutu ataṉ matippu rū. 13,00,000m ākum. appāl avar pireñcu nāṭṭu araca kuṭumpattaic cārnta ārliyaṉ ṭiyūk eṉpavarukku rū. 15,00,000-kku viṟṟu viṭṭār. ataṉpiṉ am maṇi pirañcu nāṭṭu maṉṉar kaḷiṉ maṇimuṭiyil iṭam peṟṟu atai alaṅkarittu vantatu. ippoḻutu atu 140.5 meṭrik kāraṭ eṭai irukkum. itaṉ peṟumati 60,00,000 ākum. itu iṉṟum pirañcu nāṭṭiluḷḷa lōvaril tēcīya cēkarippu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭu varukiṟatu.
5. nācak vairakkal
nācak eṉṟa nalamikka vairamaṇi maicūr nāṭṭiṉaic cērnta kōlāril kiṭaittākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. itu eṭai kuṟainta tāyiṉum eḻilmikkatu; oḷi ciṟantatu; kuṟṟamaṟṟa naṉmaṇiyākap pōṟṟap peṟukiṟatu. itu mukkōṇa vaṭivil kaviṉuṟap paṭṭan tīṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu. itaṉai intiyāvil āṅkila aracāṅkattiṉ piratiniti yāka irunta mārk vicu, ēṭiṅcu eṉpavar tamatu takkāṇap paṭai eṭuppiṉ pōtu kaippaṟṟik koṇṭār. itaṉ eṭai 80.3 meṭrik kāraṭ ākum; vilai matippu rū. 40,00,000 ākum.
6. cāṉci vairamaṇi
pārata nāṭṭil uḷḷa pukaḻpeṟṟa vairamaṇikaḷil cāṉci vairakkallum oṉṟāka matikkappaṭukiṟatu. itu kuṟippiṭattakka eḻilum oḷiyum vāyntatu. 1489-ām āṇṭil inta māmaṇi cārlacu-ṭi cāṉi eṉpavar kaikkup pōṉatu. appāl atu elicapet araci, eṇṭiri eṭṭāmēriyā, kārṭiṉal pecāriṉ, patiṉāṉkām lūyi ākiyavarkaḷiṭam māṟi māṟic ceṉṟatu. piṉṉar pirañcup puraṭcik kālattil kāṇāmal pōyviṭṭatu. ataṉ piṉ peyaṉ aracar ṭemi ṭōv iṭamum intiya iḷavaracar oruvariṭamum kaimāṟiyatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. itaṉ eṭai 55 meṭrik kāraṭ. itai oru intiyar 60,00,000 rūpāy vilai koṭuttu vāṅkiṉārām. iṉṟu itu irukkumiṭattait tiṭṭavaṭṭamākak kūṟamuṭiyavillai.
7. nampikkai nīla vairamaṇi
vaṇṇam vāynta kiṇṇam pōṉṟa maṇikaḷil mikac ciṟantu oḷirvatu nampikkai nīla vaira maṇiyākum. itaṉai āṅkilattil hōp puḷū eṉṟu kūṟuvar. itu, vaṇṇa muḷḷa vairamaṇikaḷil mika mikap periya tum uṟuti vāyntatumākum. eṉṟālum inta arumperum nīlaniṟa vairamaṇi ippoḻutu kāṇappaṭum amaippai 1839- ām āṇṭilē peṟṟuviṭṭatu eṉṟu ataṉ varalāṟṟai naṉkuṇarnta aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. ki.pi. 1642-ām āṇṭil ṭēvarṉiyar eṉpavar ārampak kālattil intiyāviṉiṉṟu tirumpi vantapoḻutu itaṉ eṭai 67 meṭrik kāraṭ eṭaiyuḷḷa inta vaṭivāṉa vaira maṇiyait tammōṭu koṇṭu vantār eṉṟu cila mēṉāṭ ṭaṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. immaṇi 1668-ām āṇṭil 14ām lūyi maṉṉarukku viṟkappaṭṭatu. appāl atai 1830ām āṇṭil 44 meṭrik kāraṭ eṭaiyākak kuṟaittu ilaṇṭaṉil uḷḷa tāma hōppukku 90,000 pavuṉukku viṟkappaṭṭatu. 1911il ēlam viṭappaṭṭu vāciṅṭaṉiluḷḷa eṭvarṭ mekḷiṉ 30,00,000 
pavuṇukku vāṅkiṉār.
8. cantira vairamaṇi
cantira vairamaṇi eṉṉum ivvaḻakiya maṇi pāracīka vīraṉ nātarcā eṉpavarāl intiyāviṉiṉṟu cūṟaiyāṭappaṭṭa koḷḷaip poruḷkaḷil oṉṟu eṉṟu iṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. itaṉai maṟutturaip pārum uṇṭu. itu nātarcā pakaivarkaḷāl kolaiyuṇṭu muṭintapiṉ oru āpkāṉiyap pōr vīraṉāl avariṭamiruntu kaippaṟṟap paṭṭu caprā eṉpavarukku viṟkappaṭṭatu. appāl ammaṇi uruciyāviṟku viṟkappaṭṭatu. ataṉ eṭai appoḻutu 120 meṭṭirik kāraṭ āka iruntatu; vilai aṉṟu 7,00,000 rūpāyāka matikkap peṟṟatu. ivvaḻakiya vairamaṇi iṉṟu aikkiya cōviyat cōcaliṭ kuṭi araciṉ karuvūlattil iṭam peṟṟu oḷirkiṉṟatu.
9. cūpsi vairamaṇi
cūpsi vairamaṇi eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭum cōkara vairakkal teṉ āppirikkāvil uḷḷa vairac curaṅkattil 1895ām 
āṇṭil tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭṭatu. itu 239 meṭrik kāraṭ eṭaiyuḷḷatāy iruntatu. itaip paṭṭan tīṭṭi 60,00,000 rūpāykku viṟkappaṭṭatu. teṉ āppirikkāvaic cērnta oru nīkki rōvartāṉ inta vairamaṇiyaik kaṇṭeṭuttār. avariṭa miruntu oru intiya nāṭṭu camatāṉa iḷavaraci 10,00,000 rūpāykku vilaikku vāṅkik koṇṭār.
10. naicām vairamaṇi
naicām vairakkal kōlkoṇṭāvil vilaikku kiṭaitta tām. atai aitarāpāt naicām aracar vilaikku vāṅkiṉār. atai avariṭamē iruntu varukiṟatu. anta vairamaṇiyai alaṅkārap poruḷāka avar tam mējai mītu vaittiruntār. ataṉ eṭai 340 meṭṭirikkāraṭ; vilai 25,00,000 rūpāykku mēl matikkap paṭṭatu.
11. akparcā vairakkal
akparcā eṉṉum mokal aracar varalāṟṟil ciṟappāṉa iṭattaip peṟṟiruppatupōl avaratu peyaraip peṟṟa inta vairamaṇiyum intiya varalāṟṟil oru ciṟanta iṭattaip peṟ ṟuḷḷatu. itu mutaṉ mutalāka 119 meṭṭirik kāraṭ eṭaiyuḷḷa tāka iruntatu. itil cila arāpi eḻuttukkaḷ cetukkappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. immaṇi 1886 āṇṭil oru tuṉi vaṭivil (Drop  - Shope) paṭṭai veṭṭiyatil 75 meṭṭirik kāraṭṭāka eṭai kuṟaintu viṭṭatu. inta maṇiyai parōṭā maṉṉar keykvār 3,50,000 pavuṇukku vilaikku vāṅkiṉār. iṉṟu itaṉ vilai 26,000 pavuṇ eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.8
12. kalliṉaṉ vaira māmaṇi
kalliṉaṉ eṉṟa vaira māmaṇik kal teṉ āppirikkāvil 1905ām āṇṭil peraṭarikvelcu eṉpavarāl kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭatu. itu ulakil op paṟṟa periya māmaṇiyāka matikkappaṭṭatu. inta māmaṇiyai kaṇṭeṭuttatum 3106 meṭrik kāraṭ eṭaiyuḷḷatāy irun tatu. itaṉai teṉ āppirikka ara cāṅkam vilaikku vāṅki 1907ām āṇṭil iṅkilāntiṉ aracar ēḻām eṭvarṭ eṉpavarukkup paricāka aḷittatu. itaṉ vilai aṉṟu 70,10,00,000 āka matikkap peṟṟatu. 1908ām āṇṭil intap periya vairakkallai 9 peruntuṇṭukaḷākavum 87 ciṟu tuṇṭu kaḷākavum veṭṭiṉārkaḷ. veṭṭappaṭṭa ammāmaṇiyiṉ ciṟu pakuti piriṭṭi maṉṉariṉ ceṅkōlil iṭam peṟṟatu eṉṟu kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. inta māmaṇiyiṉ peruntuṇṭiṉ eṭai 517 meṭrik kāraṭ ākum. itaṉ perum pakutikaḷ kirēṭ piriṭṭaṉi luḷḷa māmaṉṉariṉ makuṭattil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. aintām jiyārj aracar paṭṭattiṟku vantatum kalliṉaṉ eṇ 1-eṉṟa vairamaṇikku āppirikkāviṉ viṇmīṉ eṉṟu peyar cūṭṭap peṟṟatu. āppirikkā piriṭṭi aracāṅkattiṉ uṟuppāka iruppatai eṇṇi anta āppirikkāviṉ viṇmīṉ eṉṟa periya vairamaṇikkup piriṭṭaṉiṉ maṇimuṭiyil iṭam aḷikkap paṭṭatu. itaṉait tuṇṭākkumuṉ itaṉ vilai matippu veḷḷi (ṭālar) 45,23,400 eṉṟu matippiṭappaṭṭatu.
(II) muttu (Pearl)
paṇṭaiyat tamiḻar kaṇṭa oṉpatu maṇikaḷil talaiyāṉatu vairam. aṭutta nilaiyil vaittu matikkappaṭuvatu muttu. muṟkāla mutumakkaḷ muttukkaḷai peritum virumpi, kātaṇiyākavum mālaikaḷākavum, palvēṟu vitamākavum aṇintu vantaṉar. aracikaḷ muttukkaḷai, cēlaikaḷiṉ viḷimpil iṇaittu aṇintu vantaṉar. tamiḻarkaḷaṉṟi urōmar, kirēkkar, arāpiyar, ekiptiyar, cumēriyar, ittāliyar pōṉṟavarkaḷum ērāḷamāka muttukkaḷai vāṅki aṇintu vantaṉar. ulakilēyuḷḷa muttuk kaḷil pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiluḷḷa koṟkai muttē uyarntatu. immuttiṟku ulakam muḻuvatilum kirākki uṇṭu. yavaṉavarkaḷ kappalkaḷil vantu pāṇṭiya nāṭṭut tuṟaimukappaṭṭiṉamākiya koṟkaiyil naṅkūram pāyccip paṭṭiṉattiṟkuḷ vantu poṟ kācukaḷaiyum, poṟkaṭṭikaḷaiyum koṭṭik koṟkai muttuk kaḷai koḷ mutal ceytu koṇṭu pōṉatai avarkaḷiṉ varalāṟukaḷ eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. cintu veḷip paṇpāṭṭil koṟkai muttukkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. atai āynta varkaḷ 5000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ vaṇika vaḷarcciyaik kaṇṭu viyappuṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. mikat toṉmaiyāṉa kālattilē tamiḻarkaḷ taṅkaḷiṉ iṉattavarkaḷākiya cintuveḷi makkaḷōṭu - nilavaḻiyāy 2000 kal tolaiviṟku mēṟceṉṟu vaṇikattoṭarpu koṇṭiruntatu iṉṟu kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu.
muttu vakaikaḷ
muṟkālattilē muttukkaḷ palavakaippaṭṭavaikaḷāy iruntaṉa. (1) vaṭṭamuttu (2) oppumuttu (3) kuṟumuttu 
(4) nimpōḷam (5) payiṭṭam (6) ampu (7) karaṭu (8) iraṭṭai 
(9) cappātti (10) cakkattu (11) kuḷirnta nīr (12) civanta nīr eṉṟu pala vakaippaṭṭavaikaḷāy iruntaṉa eṉat teṉ intiya cācaṉaṅ kaḷil viḷakkamākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
ivaikaḷil vaṭṭamuttu eṉpatu, uruṇṭaiyāṉa muttu. aṉuvaṭṭam eṉpatu kiṭṭattaṭṭa vaṭṭavaṭivamāṉatu. oppumuttu oḷiviṭum uyarmuttu. kuṟumuttu ciṟumuttākum. karaṭu eṉpatu muraṭāṉa muttu. iraṭṭai eṉpatu irumuttukkaḷ iyaṟkai yākavē iṇaintu kāṇappaṭuvatu. ēṉaiya muttukkaḷukku nalla viḷakkam teriyavillai.
tōṟṟam
muttukkaḷ kaṭaṟcippikaḷiṉ uṭaliṉiṉṟu eṭukkappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē ivaikaḷaik kaṭal muttu, cippi muttu eṉṟu kūṟuvar. ēṉeṉil kuṉṟimuttu, puḷiya muttu, āmaṇakku muttu, ākiyavaikaḷukkum itaṟkum vēṟṟumai terivataṟkēyām. āṉāl muttu eṉṟu ciṟappāka collappaṭuvatu intak kaṭal muttutāṉ. cippi muttukkaḷ kaṭalil viḷaintu kiṭantāl mīṉavarkaḷ muttu eṭukka muṉaivar. itu mikak kaṣṭamāṉa toḻil. kaṭaliṉ aṭiyil pāṟaikaḷil muttuc cippikaḷ kuviyal kuviyalāka viḷaintu kiṭakkum. avaṟṟai muttukkuḷikkum mīṉavarkaḷ tōṇiyiṟ ceṉṟu kaṭalil mūḻkit taṅkaḷōṭu koṇṭu pōyirukkum kūṭaiyil vāri vaippārkaḷ. kūṭai niṟaintatum, taṅkaḷaip piṇaittirukkum kayiṟṟai acaippārkaḷ. tōṇiyil iruppavarkaḷ kayiṟṟai iḻuttuk kūṭaiyai veḷiyē koṇṭu vantu atiluḷḷa muttuc cippikaḷait tōṇiyil koṭṭu vārkaḷ. muttu eṭuttatum muttuk kuḷippavarkaḷ mēlē varu vārkaḷ. kaṭaliṉ aṭittaḷattiliruntu cippikaḷai vārum mīṉa varkaḷ kaṭalil vāḻum payaṅkaramāṉa cuṟā mīṉkaḷukkuc cila camayaṅ kaḷil āḷāvatum uṇṭu. cuṟā mīṉkaḷiṉ apāyaṅkaḷukkut tappa mīṉavarkaḷ pala mantiraṅkaḷum tantiraṅkaḷum kaiyāṇṭu varuki ṟārkaḷ.
muttukkaḷiṉ piṟappu
muttukkaḷil aṭaṅki irukkum mūlapporuḷ cuṇṇāmpuc cattutāṉ eṉṟu viññāṉikaḷ muṭipu kaṭṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ. muttuc cippikaḷ taṅkaḷ mītuḷḷa iru ōṭukaḷaiyum tiṟantu koṇṭu veḷiyē cañcarikkum poḻutu ataṉuḷ ciṟumaṇ, kal pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷ cila camayaṅkaḷil ceṉṟuviṭum. melliya tacai kaḷuḷḷa cippikku itu oru perum vētaṉaiyāy irukkum. eṉavē atait taṇikka virumpit taṉ uṭalil iruntu oruvakait tirāvakattai veḷippaṭuttum. cippiyiṉ uṭampil uḷḷa kal allatu maṇ mītu attirāvakam oṭṭik koḷḷum. ataṉāl ataṉ vētaṉai nīṅkukiṟatu. appāl ataic cuṟṟik kaṭiṉamāṉa tacai vaḷarum. nāḷaṭaivil uḷḷirukkum kal allatumaṇ muttāka māṟukiṟatu.
muttuk kiṭaikkumiṭaṅkaḷ
muttukkaḷ intiyā, cīṉā, cappāṉ, amerikkā, ilaṅkai pōṉṟa nāṭukaḷil kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa. paṇṭu toṭṭu intiyāvil ciṟappākat teṉ pāṇṭi nāṭṭil muttuk kuḷikkun toḻil naṭaipeṟṟu varukiṟatu. maṉṉār kuṭākkaṭalil kuṟippāka nellai māvaṭṭattai aṭuttuḷḷa kaṭalil nalla muttukkaḷ viḷaikiṉṟaṉa. inta muttukkaḷukku ulakameṅkum matippu iruntu varukiṉṟatu. koṟkai muttu kuvalayam aṉaittilumuḷḷa ellā maṇic cantaikaḷaiyum eṭṭippārttavai. pāracīka vaḷaikuṭāvil viḷaiyum muttukkaḷaiviṭa maṉṉār vaḷaikuṭāvil viḷaiyum muttukkaḷ matippuṭaiyaṉavākum. koṟkai muttukkaḷ veṇmai yum tiṇmaiyum oḷiyum aḻakum vāyntaṉavākum. vāṉmīki irāmāyaṇattil pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷiṉ talainakarattai muttūr eṉṉum poruḷtarum kavāṭapuram eṉak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
naṉ muttu
peritākavum uruṇṭaiyākavum teḷinta nīr pōṉṟum tikaḻum muttukkaḷē naṉmuttukkaḷ eṉṟu navilappaṭum. tūyatāyum vaḻavaḻappāyum viṇ pōṉṟa oḷiyuḷḷatāyum nīroḷiyuḷḷa tāyum irukkum. muttukkaḷ ciṟanta muttukkaḷ eṉṟum, avai kaḷai aṇintāl ellā nalaṉkaḷum perukum eṉṟum kūṟappaṭu kiṟatu.
kuṟṟaṅkaḷ
muttukkaḷ vikāramākavum aḻukkup paṭintum karaṭu muraṭākavum uppup pōlavum muṭiccu viḻuntum āmai uru piṟai vaṭivam, tēṉmeḻuku pōṉṟa puḷḷi, nīla niṟam, kaṟuppum mañcaḷum kalanta vaṇṇam vāyntum vēṟṟiṭat tuḷaiyuḷḷavaiyāyum iruntāl kuṟṟam uḷḷaṉavāka matikkappaṭum.
maruttuvattuṟai viḷakkam
muttu kuḷircciyum tittippum uḷḷatu. kaṇ nōy, vāta nōy, iratta pittam, kāyccal, mayakkam mutaliya nōykaḷai akaṟṟum. nīṇṭa āyuḷaiyum, mēṉi miṉu miṉuppaiyum nalkum. muttup papam pittam kācam kapam mutaliya ellā, pollāp piṇikaḷaiyum pōkkum. vīriya viruttiyai aḷikkum. muttuc cuṇṇam kapatōcattaik karuvaṟukkum. iḵtaṉṟi vikkal, akkiṉi mantam pōṉṟa aṉēka nōy akalum.
cōtaṉai
uppum eṇṇeyum kalanta vennīril, ōriravu muḻuvatum muttai ūṟavaittu appāl atai nellōṭu cērttut tēyttup pārkkavum nalla oḷiyōṭu iruntāl naṉ muttu eṉṟum oḷi kuṉṟi niṉṟāl ceyaṟkai muttu eṉṟum teḷika.
ippoḻutu ulakil ērāḷamāṉa ceyaṟkai muttukkaḷum, ceyaṟkai muṟaiyil kaṭalil viḷaivikkum muttukkaḷum malivāṉa vilaiyil eṅkum viṟkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cappāṉ pōṉṟa nāṭukaḷil mēṟkaṇṭa iruvakai muttukaḷum kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa.
muttu mālaikaḷ
muttu aṇikalaṉkaḷai muṟkālat tamiḻ makkaḷ virumpi aṇintu vantaṉar. mēṉāṭṭu mātarkaḷ kūṭa malaṭṭut taṉmai nīṅkavum, kaṇpārvai teḷivuṟavum muttuk kolucaiyum muttu maṇivaṭaṅkaḷaiyum aṇintu vantaṉar. tamiḻ nāṭṭu makaḷir kaḷukku muttumālaikaḷil mōkam atikam. avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ makkaḷukku muttu mālai, muttu maṇi, muttaiyā, muttam perumāḷ, muttuk kiruṣṇaṉ eṉṟellām peyar cūṭṭi vantaṉar. paṇṭaiyat tamiḻakattil muttumālai 5 vakaippaṭṭatāy iruntatu. (1) talaimaṇiyuṭaiyatu (2) tuṇait talai maṇiyuṭaiyatu (3) kaṇu nilaiyuṭaiyatu (4) taraḷak kaṭṭuṭaiyatu (5) ceṟi nilaiyuṭaiyatu.
1. talaimaṇivaṭam
vaṭattiṉ naṭuvē periya maṇiyai iṭam peṟa ceytu, kōttu iru pakkaṅkaḷilum otta aḷavuḷḷa ciṟiya muttumaṇi kaḷai eṭuttuk kōttavaṭam talaimaṇi vaṭam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum.
2. tuṇait talaimaṇivaṭam
naṭuvil oru paru muttum iru puṟaṅkaḷilum otta aḷaviṉai yuṭaiya ciṟumuttu maṇiyum appāl perum maṇiyum iṭam peṟumāṟu māṟi māṟik kōtta muttu mālai, tuṇait talaimaṇi vaṭam eṉappaṭum.
3. kaṇu nilaimaṇivaṭam
naṭuvil oru perum muttum irupuṟamum otta parumaṉuḷḷa iraṇṭiraṇṭu ciṟu muttukaḷumāka māṟi māṟik kōtta vaṭam kaṇu nilaimaṇivaṭam eṉappaṭum.
4. taraḷak kaṭṭuvaṭam
otta aḷavuṭaiya nittilaṅkaḷ (muttukkaḷ) kōtta vaṭam taraḷak kaṭṭu vaṭam eṉappaṭum. muttukkut taraḷam, nittilam eṉṟa peyarkaḷ uṇṭu.
5. ceṟi nilai vaṭam 
naṭuvil periya muttu maṇikaḷaik kōttu irupuṟamum muṟaiyē ciṟuttu varumāṟu kōttavaṭam ceṟi nilai vaṭam eṉappaṭum.
mēṟkūṟiya aintu vakaiyāṉa taraḷa vaṭaṅkaḷum avaṟṟil kōkkappaṭṭa maṇivakaikaḷaip poṟuttē vevvēṟu peyar kaḷaip peṟukiṟatu. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka talaimaṇivaṭam mutal aintu vaṭaṅkaḷum intirac cantam eṉṉum peyarōṭu iṇaintu talaimaṇi intirac cantam tuṇait talaimaṇi intirac cantam eṉpaṉa pōl vevvēṟu peyarkaḷaip peṟum. ivvāṟu aimpatu peyarkaḷaiyuṭaiya mālaikaḷ uḷa. (1) intirac cantam 1008, (2) viyac cantam  500, (3) tēvaccantam 100, (4) arutta mālai 64, (5) katirk kalāpam 54, (6) kuccam 32,  (7) naṭcattiramālai 27, (8) artta kuccam 24, (9) māṇavakam 20, (10) artta māṇavakam 10 - muttuk kaḷaiyum uṭaiyaṉa.
iṅkilāntil caut keṉciṅṭaṉ poruṭkāṭci nilaiyat til, ulakattil uḷḷa ellā muttukkaḷaiyum viṭa periya āṇi muttu uḷatu. atu 3 avuṇcuk kaṉamum 4 aṅkulac cuṟṟaḷavum uḷḷa oru perum muttu ākum. āṉāl ataiviṭap periya māmuttu cōviyat yūṉiyaṉil uḷḷa cocimā (Zosima) eṉṟa kāṭci cālaiyil irukkiṟatu. itaṉ peyar vāpel vikariṉā eṉappaṭum. itaṉ eṭai 28 kāraṭ. itu aḻakum oḷiyum vāyntatu. itutāṉ ulakilē periya oppumai illāta oḷi muttākum. itu koṟkai muttu eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.
cōtiṭa nūṟkaḷ
cōtiṭa nūṟkaḷil cemmai, veṇmai, poṉmai, karumai ākiya niṟaṅkaḷ vāynta naṉmuttukkaḷ tiṅkaḷukkuk kaḷippai uṇṭākkuvaṉavākum. eṉavē tiṅkaḷukku uriya vīṭākiya naṇṭu ōraiyil (kaṭaka lakkaṉattil) piṟantavarkaḷ kaṭal muttukkaḷai aṇivatu nalam tarum eṉṟu tamiḻ nāṭṭuc cōtiṭa nūṟkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āṅkilēyarkaḷ kūṭap piṟanta tiṅkaḷai vaittu ataṟkuriya maṇikaḷ ivaiyeṉṟu aṟintu anta maṇikaḷai aṇintu varukiṟārkaḷ. itu āṅkilamaṇi āyvu nūṟkaḷil naṉku kāṇappaṭum.
marakatam (Emerald)
oṉpatu maṇikaḷuḷ paccai niṟam vāyntatu marakata maṇi yākum. talaimaṇikaḷil mūṉṟāvatu iṭattaip peṟṟuḷḷa kaṇ kavar maṇiyākum. paccaip pacumai vāyntatu eṉṟu paṇṭu mutal tamiḻarkaḷ pōṟṟi varum vaṇṇamākum. paccai parācattiyiṉ niṟam eṉṟu pōṟṟappaṭuvatākum. paccai niṟamāṉa poruḷkaḷiṉiṉṟu uyirkaḷukku uyirūṭṭum uṇavup poruḷ kiṭaikkiṉṟatu. aṉṉai parācakti taṉ makkaḷākiya uyirkaḷukku pāl curantu ūṭṭi avarkaḷaik kaḷippil āḻttit tāṉum kaḷippuṟukiṉṟāḷ eṉpatu muṟkālat tamiḻarkaḷiṉ eṇṇam.
marakatattiṉ taṉmai
marakatam vairam pōṉṟu kaṭiṉamāṉatu. eṉṟālum pacumai yāṉa oḷikkatirkaḷuṭaṉ meṉmaiyāṉatu pōl kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ñāyiṟṟiṉ oḷi marakatak kalliṉ mītu paṭṭāl atiṉ paccai vaṇṇa oḷi ataṉ cuṟṟuppuṟamellām paravi arukiluḷḷa ellāp poruḷkaḷaiyum paccai niṟamāka oḷirac ceykiṉṟatu. eṉavē aṇikalaṉkaḷil marakata maṇiyai naṭunāyamāka amaittu ēṉaiya maṇiyaic cuṟṟilum vaittu aṇikaḷ camaikkappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. marakatattiṉ pacumaiyāṉa oḷi ēṉaiya maṇikaḷil pirati palippatu oru arum perum aḻakākum.
kiṭaikkum iṭam 
marakatam kōmētakattiṉ iyalpukaḷai uṭaiyatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ikkaṟkaḷ ceyappūr, kācumīr pakutikaḷilum parmā, ilaṅkai, cōviyat yūṉiyaṉ, ekiptu, kalipōrṉiyā, maṭakācukkar, ātirēliyā pōṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷilum pūmiyiṉiṉṟu tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. intiyāvil kiṭaikkum marakata maṇikaḷ mikap pacumai vāynta uyariya maṇikaḷāka matikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟu, intiya maṇi vaṇikarkaḷ ceyappūril kiṭaikkum marakatamaṇi kaḷaiyē peritum virumpi avaṟṟukku atika vilai koṭuttu vāṅkukiṟārkaḷ. marakatak kaṟkaḷ aṇikaḷ amaippataṟku maṭṭu maṉṟi iliṅkaṅkaḷ ceyvataṟkum vāṅkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. marakata maṇikaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa iliṅkaṅkaḷ ciṟappuṭaiyaṉavākum. namatu nāṭṭil cila kōyilkaḷil marakata iliṅkaṅkaḷ iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
maṇiccōtaṉai (irattiṉap parīṭcai) eṉṟa nūlil uyarnta paccaik kaṟkaḷ, marakatam kārut matam acma karppam, ariṉmaṇi eṉṟellām aḻaikkappaṭum eṉak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
nikaṇṭukaḷil marakatam, kāṭam, ullacitam, pēcalam, pittakam, muktam, pirutukam; eṉum aṟuvakaik kuṇaṅkaḷ uṭaiyatu eṉak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
kuṇaṅkaḷum kuṟṟaṅkaḷum
marakatattiṟkuk karukal, veḷḷai, kal, maṇal kīṟṟu, kuḻi yuṭaimai, tār ōṭi iruttal pōṉṟavai kuṟṟaṅkaḷ ākum. aṟu vakaik kuṇaṅkaḷ : atāvatu kārṭam appoḻututāṉ muḷaitta aṟukam pulliṉ niṟam, ullaḷitam - vaḻavaḻappu, pēcalam - neṟpayir pōṉṟa niṟam, pittam - pacuṅkiḷiyiṉ iṟaku pōṉṟa vaṇṇam. muktam - tuḷici ilai pōṉṟa niṟam. pirutukam - tāmarai  ilai pōṉṟa taṉmaikaḷ immaṇiyiṉ ciṟanta kuṇaṅkaḷ ākum. im maṇiyai aṇipavarkaḷukku ellā nalamum perukum. iḵtaṉṟi kuṟṟamuḷḷa maṇikaḷai aṇintāl kēṭukaḷ viḷaiyum eṉṟu maṇi nūṟkaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
	ēkaiyum mālaiyum iruḷoṭu tuṟanta
	 pācār mēṉip pacuṅkatir oḷiyum	  (cilap. ūr. vari. 184 - 185)
eṉṟa cilappatikārac ceyyuḷukku uraikaṇṭa āciriyar aṭiyārkku nallār mēṟkaṇṭa karuttukkaḷai araṇ ceyyum vaḻiyil viḷakka urai kūṟiyuḷḷār.
maruttuva nūṟkaḷil marakatamaṇi
marakata maṇi vayiṟṟuk kaṭuppai nīkkum; mēka nōyaip pōkkum; eḷitākap makappēṟu peṟap peritum tuṇaiceyyum. marakata papam, marakatamaṇi pōṉṟavai, tiritōcaṅkaḷaip pōkkum; uṭaṟ palamum nīṇṭa āyuḷum tarum eṉṟu maruttuva nūṟkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
cōtaṉai
nalla cāti marakatattaik kaṇṭaṟiya vēṇṭum eṉṟāl, oru kiṇṇattil pālai vaittu atil marakata maṇiyaip pōṭṭāl ataṉ oḷi, pāl muḻuvatum paravi pāl, paccai niṟam pōṉṟu tōṉṟum. ceyaṟkai marakatam avvāṟu oḷi viṭātu. iyaṟkai yāṉa marakata maṇiyaik kallil tēyttāl maṇi tēyātu. ceyaṟkai marakatam tēyum, uṭaiyum.
marakata māmaṇi
ituvarai mānilattil kiṭaitta marakata maṇikaḷil tivāṉcayar iḷavaracariṭam irukkum 1350 kāraṭ eṭaiyuḷḷa marakata maṇiyē mikap periyatākum. iḵtaṉṟip piriṭṭīcut tol poruḷ kāṭcic cālaiyilum oru periya marakata māmaṇi irukkiṟatu. ivai kuṟṟam aṟṟavai; vilaiyuyarntavai.
pirivum kirakaṅkaḷum
marakatam vēḷāḷar iṉam. cātvīkakuṇam uṭaiyatu. mayil, kāṭai pōṉṟa paṟavai iṟaku niṟam vāynta marakatam putaṉukku makiḻcci tarum. putaṉukkuriya irāciyil piṟantavarkaḷ kuṟṟamaṟṟa marakatattai aṇivatāl aṟivu vaḷarum; kirakatōcam akalum; aḻakum āyuḷum perukum.
māṇikkam (Ruby)
māṇikkam, civappu, patumarākam eṉṟu kūṟip pōṟṟap peṟum maṇiyākum; vairamaṇikku aṭutta paṭiyāka vaittup pōṟṟakkūṭiya uyarnta kallākum. māṇikkam nalla aḻaku vāynta cenniṟattaiyuṭaiyatu. itu mutalil veḷuppākat tōṉṟip piṉ ñāyiṟṟiṉ veppattāl cenniṟam peṟukiṟatu. nalla cenniṟam vāynta māṇikkattiṟkuc ciṟappu atikam; māṇikkam maṇikaḷiṉ maṉṉaṉ eṉṟu maṇi nipuṇarkaḷ kūṟuvārkaḷ.
vakaikaḷ 
maṇikaḷil māṇpu vāyntatāka matikkappaṭum māṇikkam  4 vakaippaṭum. (1) cavuntikam (2) nīlakanti (3) patmarākam (4) kuruvintam.
(1) cavuntikam : itu āntira nāṭṭil atikamākak kiṭaik kiṟatu. acōkat taḷir pōṉṟa vaṇṇam vāyntatu.
(2) nīlakanti : itu tumpurattil atikam kiṭaikkiṟatu; nīla niṟam vāyntatu; kavarccimikkatu.
(3) patmarākam : immaṇi irattiṉa tīpam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum; īḻa nāṭṭil (ilaṅkaiyil) ērāḷamākak kiṭaikkiṟatu; nalla civappu niṟam vāyntatu.
(4) kuruvintam : kalāpurattil atikam akappaṭukiṟatu; mañcaḷ niṟam vāyntatu.
ivaikaḷil, ilaṅkaiyil centāmarai malar vaṇṇattil kiṭaikkum patmarākam eṉṉum maṇi ciṟantatu.
kiṭaikkum iṭaṅkaḷ : iṉṟu pala māṇikkac curaṅkaṅkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. parmā, cayām, intōcīṉam, roṭai ciyā, cācumīr (intiyā) āpkāṉicuttāṉ, ilaṅkai pōṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil  ellām māṇikka maṇikaḷ kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl parmāvil mōkāk eṉṟum iṭattilum kāttē māvaṭṭattilum uyarnta māṇikka maṇikaḷ atikam kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa.
iṉappirivukaḷ
māṇikkattil patumarākam antaṇar iṉam eṉṟum cavun tikam vaṇikacāti eṉṟum nīlakanti vēḷāḷar iṉam eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
kuṇaṅkaḷ
kaṉamāka iruttal, aḻakāṉa vaṇṇamāka iruttal, mācillātatāy viḷaṅkal, nalla centāmarai malar pōṉṟa civappu niṟamākap polital, nalla māṇikka maṇiyiṉ kuṇaṅkaḷākum. itai aṇipavarkaḷukku nīṇṭa vāṇāḷ, nōyaṟṟa vāḻkkai, uṭaṟpalam mutaliyavai vaḷarum; maṉanōykaḷ iruntāl māṟum; eḻil perukum.
kuṟṟaṅkaḷ : civappu niṟamaṟṟamaṇi, vēṟum vaṇṇam vāyn tatu, puḷḷi, tarācam (oḷi calittal), kīṟṟu, kākapatam, ākiyavai iruttal  māṇikkak kaṟkaḷiṉ māperum kuṟṟaṅkaḷākum. ivai kaḷai aṇintālum vaittiruntālum keṭuti uṇṭākum. pakaivarkaḷāl iṭaiyūṟu uṇṭākum. vilaṅkukaḷālum nōy kaḷālum tuṉpamum tuyaramum viḷaiyum eṉpar.
uyarnta maṇi : ulakil uyarnta māṇikka māmaṇi 167 kāraṭ eṭaiyuḷḷatu. itu paṭṭai tīrkkappaṭātatu eṉṟu kūṟap paṭukiṟatu. āṉāl intiyāvai āṇṭa mokalāya maṉṉar cācakāṉ avarkaḷiṉ mayilācaṉattil patippikkappeṟṟa timūr eṉṉum māṇikkak kal nalla paṭṭai tīrkkappeṟṟa kallākum. itu 361 kāraṭ  eṭaiyuḷḷatu. uruciyāvil irunta kātaraiṉ araciyiṭam 50 kāraṭ eṭaiyuḷḷa māṇikkam iruntatu. atu iṉṟaiya cōviyat yūṉiyaṉil uḷḷa aracāṅkat tolporuḷ kāṭcic cālaiyil uḷḷatu.
maruttuva nūṟkaḷil māṇikkam
māṇikka papam, caḷi, curam, tākam, mēkam, kaṇṇōy pōṉṟavaikaḷai akaṟṟum; āyuḷ viruttiyai uṇṭākkum;  mēṉiyil paḷapaḷappaiyum, uṭalukkuk kuḷircciyaiyum tarum.
cōtaṉai : māṇikkak kaṟkaḷil nalla iṉakkaṟkaḷaik kaṇṭupiṭikka, kallil tēyttup pārkkalām; nīril pōṭṭuk kotikka vaittup pārkkalām. nalla māṇikkamaṇi tēyātu; niṟam māṟātu. ceyaṟkai māṇikkam uṭaiyum tēyum, niṟam māṟum.
cōtiṭa nūlil māṇikkam : cōtiṭa nūṟkaḷil ñāyiṟṟukku nalla civappu niṟam vāynta māṇikkak kaṟkaḷil atikappiriyam uṇṭeṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. māṇikkam ñāyiṟṟiṉ vīṭākiya ciṅka ilakkaṉattil piṟantavarkaḷukku naṉmaiyai nalkum. ñāyiṟṟiṉ tōcaṅkaḷait tuṭaikkum. maṇikaḷil māṇikkam maṉṉar marapaic cērntatu. irajata kuṇam uṭaiyatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
irattiṉa tīpam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum ilaṅkaiyil irattiṉapuri eṉṉum ūril iṉṟum iṇaiyilāta māṇikkak kaṟkaḷ kiṭaittu varukiṉṟaṉa. iṅkuḷḷa maṇikaḷai - ciṟappāka, kāyal paṭṭiṉam, kīḻakkarai mutaliya ūriluḷḷa iculāmiya vaṇikarkaḷ vāṅki ilaṅkaiyilum piṟaviṭaṅkaḷilum periya maṇi vaṇikar kaḷāy viḷaṅkukiṉṟārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ ittoḻilil nalla nipuṇattuvam peṟṟirukkiṉṟārkaḷ.

iṭai maṇikaḷ
I
nīlam (Sapphire)
nīlamaṇikku ataṉ niṟattai vaittē nīlam eṉṟa peyar vaikkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. potuvāka vairam-veḷḷai, marakatam - paccai, māṇikkam - civappu, puṭparākam - mañcaḷ, kōmētakam - mañcaḷum civappum kalanta civappu, vaiṭūriyam - veḷuṟiya nīlaniṟam, nīlam - nalla nīla niṟamum uṭaiyaṉa. ivaikaḷil māṇikkam, marakatam, puṭparākam, vairam, nīlam ākiya maṇikaḷai aṟiñarkaḷ aintu maṇikaḷ (pañcarattiṉam) eṉpārkaḷ. marakatam, māṇikkam, intira nīlam, muttu, vairam, ākiyavaikaḷai māmaṇikaḷ (mahā rattiṉaṅkaḷ) eṉpārkaḷ.
iṉam
potuvāka vairamum muttum antaṇar iṉam eṉṟum māṇikkamum pavaḷamum aracar iṉam eṉṟum puṭparākam, vaiṭūriyam, kōmētakam ākiyavai vaṇikar iṉam eṉṟum, nīlam, marakatam ākiyavai vēḷāḷar iṉameṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. iḵtaṉṟi veḷuṟiya nīlam antaṇar cāti eṉṟum, civappu nīlam aracar cāti eṉṟum, mañcaḷ nīlam vaṇikar cāti eṉṟum, kaṟuppu nīlam uḻavar cāti eṉṟum kūṟuvar. nīlattil nāṉku iṉam pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atāvatu vēḷāḷar iṉamākiya nīlattilē nāṉku iṉappirivukaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
mēlum nīlamaṇiyai intira nīlam, makā nīlam eṉṟu iruvakaiyāyp pirippatum uṇṭu. intira nīlam karumaiyāṉatu; kaṉamāṉatu; ciṟantatu. calanīlam ilēcāṉatu, veḷuṟiyatu. maṇiyiṉ naṭuvil vāṉavil pōṉṟa oḷi iruntāl atu cuttamāṉa intira nīlam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. itu kiṭaippatu aritu. vilaiyum uyarntatu. inta nīlattaip pālil pōṭṭāl pāl muḻuvatum nīlamākak kāṇappaṭum. itaṉai makā nīlam eṉpar. inta makā nīlattaiyum ataṉ niṟattaiyum tarattaiyum koṇṭu aṟiñarkaḷ itaṉuḷ nāṉku iṉappirivukaḷ kāṇpar.
kuṇam
maṇiccōtaṉai eṉṟa vaṭamoḻi nūlil kaṉam, kavarcciyāṉa oḷi, nalla pūrippu, pakkaṅkaḷil pūrippu, pullaippiṭikkun taṉmai eṉṟa aintu kuṇaṅkaḷum nīlamaṇikku irukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
kuṟṟam
aprakam, cacarkaram, tirācam, piṉṉam, miruttikā karpam, acma karpam  ākiyavai nīlamaṇiyiṉ kuṟṟaṅkaḷākum: 1. aprakattaip pōṉṟa vaṇṇam nīlattiṉ mītu paṭarntiruntāl atu aprakam eṉappaṭum. itai aṇintāl celvam aḻiyum; āyuḷ kuṉṟum. (2) maṇiyil maṇal cērntiruntāl cacarkaram eṉappaṭum. itaip pūṇṭāl vaṟumai vāṭṭum; ciṟumaiyūṭṭum; nāṭṭaiviṭṭu ōṭṭum. (3) porical iruntāl tirācam eṉpārkaḷ. itaip pūṇṭāl naccuḷḷa uyiriṉaṅkaḷāl tīmai viḷaiyum. (4) uṭainta maṇiyaip piṉṉappaṭṭa maṇi eṉpārkaḷ. itai aṇintāl maṉaiviyai iḻakka nēriṭum eṉpar. (5) nīlakkallil naṭuvē uṭpuṟa  māka maṇ iruppiṉ atai miruttikākarpam eṉpārkaḷ. itai aṇintāl avamatippu, uṭampil tōlaippaṟṟiya nōykaḷ tōṉṟum eṉpar. (6) nīlamaṇiyil uḷḷē kal kāṇappaṭṭāl atai acmakarpam eṉpar. atai aṇipavarkaḷ avamatippukku āḷāvārkaḷ.
nīlam kiṭaikkum iṭam
nīlamaṇikaḷ, intiyāvil kācumīrp pakutiyilum ilaṅkai yil irattiṉapuriyilum cayāmil pāṭṭam pāṅkilum parmāvil mōkākkilum kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa; aṉṟiyum kalipōrṉiyā maṭa kācukkar, kuviṉculāntu pōṉṟa kāṭukaḷilum kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa.
maruttuva nūṟkaḷil nīlam :
nīla maṇiyaip poṭi (papam) ceytu uṇṭāl cītaḷam, caṭam, ciṉikkam, pittam mutaliya piṇikaḷ akalum. pāṇṭu, mati mayakkam pōṉṟa pala nōykaḷum akalum. itai aṇintāl aṟivu oḷirum; iṉpam perukum.
	kutivalimē kampittam kūṟariya pāṇṭu
	 matimayakka mellā maruḷum - matinutalē
	 puttiyoṭu mēṉmēlum pōka cukamuṇṭām
	 cuttanaṟu nīlattāl col
eṉṟu patārttakuṇa cintāmaṇi eṉṟa maruttuva nūl nīla paṭpattiṉ kuṇattaik kūṟukiṟatu.12
ulakil uyarnta maṇi 
ulakil uyarnta nīlamaṇi 916 kāraṭ eṭaiyuḷḷatu. itu 3½ aṅkula nīḷamum 2 aṅkula akalamum 1 aṅkula kaṉamum uḷḷatu. immaṇi aitarāpāttil uḷḷatu. itaṉ vilai matippu 5,00,000 rūpāy ākum. muṉṉar itutāṉ ulakiṟ periya nīlamaṇiyāy iruntatu. ippoḻutu parmāvil 1029 kāraṭ eṭaiyuḷḷa nīlamaṇi oṉṟu kiṭaittiruppatākat terikiṟatu.
cōtiṭa nūṟkaḷil nīlam
cōtiṭa nūṟkaḷil maṇikaḷaip paṟṟik kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. intira nīlakkal caṉikku uriyatu. makaram, kumpam ākiya caṉikkuriya irācikaḷil piṟantavarkaḷ kuṟṟamaṟṟa intira nīlakkallai aṇin tāl caṉi tōcam nīṅkum. mēlum caṉi pakavāṉiṉ karuṇai perukum. āyuḷum, celvamum uṭal nalaṉum perukum eṉṟu kūṟappaṭu kiṉṟatu.
II
puṭparākam (Topaz)
puṭparākam vairam pōṉṟu mañcaḷ kalanta veṇmaiyāṉa maṇi. mēl pākam vaṭṭamākavum maḻamaḻappākavum iruntāl ciṟanta maṇiyāka matikkappaṭum. itu potuvākap pāṟaikaḷiṉ naṭuvilē uṇṭākiṟatu. puṭparākam pūcanaṭcattirampōl poli yum. poṉṉait teḷiya vaittāṟpōṉṟu ilaṅkum. itaṉaic cilappatikāram pūca uruviṟ polanteḷittaṉaiya 13 eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu. immaṇiyai aṇipavarkaḷukku pala nalam viḷaiyum. vēntarāyiṉ veṟṟimālai cūṭuvar eṉṟu tiruviḷaiyāṭaṟ purāṇam kūṟukiṟatu.
kiṭaikkum iṭaṅkaḷ
puṭparākam pirēcil nāṭṭil ūṭā, kolarāṭā, orō piriṭṭā, villārīkā, miṉācu nōvācu, miṉācu cerāyacu pōṉṟa pakutikaḷil kiṭaikkiṉṟatu. ōrō piriṭṭāvil ciṟanta ārañcu vaṇṇap puṭparākaṅkaḷ kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa. ilaṅkaiyil lēcāṉa mañcaḷ niṟa maṇikaḷ kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa. cōviyat yūṉiyaṉil uḷḷa iyūral malaiyilum caipīriyāvilum lēcāṉa paccai veḷuṟiya nīlam vāynta puṭparākaṅkaḷ kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa. vaṭakku naicīriyāvil uḷḷa takarak kaṉikaḷaiyuṭaiya vaṇṭal piratēcaṅkaḷil itaṉai otta, nīrāl aṟukkappaṭṭa paṭikaṅkaḷum kūḻāṅkaṟkaḷum kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
kuṇaṅkaḷ
aḻakiya tōṟṟam, parumaṉ, teḷivu, kaṉam, meṉmai kōṅkilava malar pōṉṟa niṟam, nalla veṇmai ākiya ēḻu vitamāṉa kuṇaṅkaḷ nalla puṭparākattiṉ kuṇaṅkaḷāka vākaṭam eṉṟa nūl kūṟukiṟatu.
kuṟṟaṅkaḷ 
oḷiyiṉmai, nācaṉai (māṇikkattil uṇṭākum veṇṇīr) karumai, mañcaḷ, mēṭupaḷḷam, kalaṅkal, karu veṇmai, veṇmai ākiya  taṉmaikaḷ puṭparākattiṉ kuṟṟaṅkaḷākum.
puṭparākam uruvil periyatāyum vaḻavaḻappāyum tāmarai yiṉ karṇikai pōṉṟa niṟam uṭaiyatāyum iruntāl atai aṇiyalām. atai aṇintārkku ellāc celvaṅkaḷum nalamum poṅkum.
uyarnta maṇi
piriṭṭiṣ tolporuḷ kāṭciccālaiyil nārvēyi liruntu koṇṭuvanta 137 - pavuṇṭu niṟaiyuḷḷa puṭparākam oṉṟuṇṭu. intiya mokalāya maṉṉaṉ avuraṅkacīppiṭam 157- kāraṭ eṭaiyuḷḷa 8 paṭṭaiyāṉa puṭparāka maṇi oṉṟu iruntatu.
maruttuva nūlil puṭparākam
puṭparāka pacuppam kapattai nīkkum; uṭal vaḷattaip perukkum; vīriya viruttiyum puttik kūrmaiyum uṇṭākkum eṉpar.
cōtiṭa nūlil puṭparākam
puṭparākam viyāḻaṉukkuriyatu. mīṉa lakkiṉattil piṟanta varkaḷ itai aṇintāl viyāḻatōcam nīṅkum; nōy akalum; palam uṇṭākum eṉpar.
III
vaiṭūriyam (cat - eyes)
vaiṭūriyam paḻuppu niṟa oḷiyum veṇmaiyāṉa oḷiyum koṇṭu oḷirum. itaic cilappatikāra uraiyāciriyar katiravaṉ oḷiyaip pōṉṟu ilaṅkum; tēṉ tuḷiyiṉ uruvam pōṉṟu tikaḻum eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
kuṇam
mūṅkil ilai, pūṉaikkaṇ, mayiṟkaḻuttu ivai pōṉṟa oḷiyuḷḷa vaiṭūriya maṇikaḷē uyarntaṉa. immaṇi eṅku iruppiṉum  ataṉuḷ nūl iḻai pōṉṟa oḷi vīcum. itaṉai nūlōṭṭam eṉpar.
kuṟṟam
kaṟuppum cama uruvamum uḷḷatu; karaṭu muraṭāṉatu; nīr niṟam uḷḷatu. ilēcāṉatu, kuṟṟamuḷḷa maṇiyākum. itu aṇiyattakka taṉṟu; vilakkattakkatu. itai aṇintāl kēṭu cūḻum. vaṟumai mikum. nōy perukum.
cōtaṉai
vaiṭūriyattai uraikallil uraittāl  oḷi perukum. ituvē uyarnta vaiṭūriyattiṉ taṉmaiyākum.
kiṭaikkum iṭam
vaiṭūriyam intiyāvilum ilaṅkaiyilum kiṭaikkiṟatu. iḵtaṉṟi pirēcil, urukuvē, iṅkilāntu, kāṭlāntu, vaṭa amerikkā, teṉ āppirikkā, ācuttirēliyā pāracīkam pōṉṟa nāṭukaḷilum kiṭaikkum.
maruttuva nūlil vaiṭūriyam
vaiṭūriya pacuppam cīvatātam, cilēttumam, puḷiyēppam, kātu cūlai, kuṉmam, pittam pōṉṟa nōykaḷai akaṟṟum.
cōtiṭa nūṟkaḷil vaiṭūriyam
vaiṭūriyam kētuviṟku uriyatu. kētu tōcam uḷḷavarkaḷ itai aṇintāl attōcam nīṅkum eṉpar.
iṉam
kōmētakam, vaiṭūriyam, puṭparākam pōṉṟa maṇikaḷum cantirakāntam, cūriyakāntam pōṉṟa kaṟkaḷum paṭika iṉattaic cērntavai eṉṟu maṇiccōtaṉaiyil tērnta nipuṇarkaḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ.

kaṭai maṇikaḷ
I
kōmēkatam (zircon)
kōmētakam pacuviṉ ciṟunīr niṟattai otta vaṇṇamāka iruppatāl kōmētakam eṉṟu peyar vantatu eṉṟu cilar kūṟuvar. airōppiyar kōmētakam kutiraiyiṉ mūttira vaṇṇattil irukkum eṉpar. immaṇi mañcaḷum civappum kalantatu. cilap patikāra āciriyar immaṇiyai iru vēṟu ruva eṉṟu kūṟi yuḷḷār. itu kaṟpāṟaikaḷiliruntu eṭukkappaṭukiṟatu.
kuṇaṅkaḷ
(1) nalla, teḷinta pacuviṉ ciṟunīr niṟamuṭaiyatu. (2) mācil lātatu, cuttamāṉatu. (3) paḷapaḷappuṭaiyatu. (4) aḻuttam uḷḷatu. (5) kaṉam uṭaiyatu (6) mēṭupaḷḷam illātatu, (7) koḻuppāṉatu, (8) oḷi niṟaintatu ākiya eṭṭuk kuṇaṅkaḷ koṇṭatu, nalla kōmētaka maṇiyāka matikkappeṟum.
kuṟṟaṅkaḷ
(1) maṅkalāṉatu, (2) mācuḷḷatu (pāci paṭarntiruppatu) (3) lēcāṉatu (4) mēṭupaḷḷam uḷḷatu (5) uppuk kacivuḷḷatu (6) takaṭu vaṭivam vāyntatu (7) oḷiyaṟṟiruppatu, (8) urukkuk kal pōliruppatu ākiya eṭṭum kuṟṟaṅkaḷ ākum.
iyalpu : kōmētakattait taṇalil kāṭṭiṉāl āccariyap paṭattakka māṟutal eḻum. mikac cūṭākkiṉāl niṟaṅkuṉṟum; paḷapaḷappu mikum.
 maruttuva nūlil kōmētakam : kōmētakap pacuppam cāppiṭṭāl kapam nīṅkum; vātapitta tōcampōm, cīraṇacakti perukum; malakkaṭṭai akaṟṟum, valimaitarum; uṭampiṟku nalla oḷi ūṭṭum.
cōtiṭa nūlil kōmētakam
irākukku ukanta maṇi eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itai aṇintāl irākutōcam akalum. immaṇi aṇivatāl nalla uṭal valimai tarum. uṭal nalam perukum. uṭalil nalla miṉumiṉuppu uṇṭākum eṉṟu cukkira nīti eṉṟa ciṟpanūl kūṟukiṟatu
pavaḷam (Coral)
pavaḷap pūccikaḷ kaṭalil uḷḷa piṟa uyiriṉaṅkaḷāl taṅkaḷukku nēriṭum āpattai akaṟṟa oṉṟu kūṭi uṇṭākkum oru pātukāppu araṇē pavaḷamākum. āḻ kaṭalkaḷil pavaḷaṅkaḷ ceṭikaḷ pōl kāṇappaṭum. pavaḷak kāṭukaḷ kaṭalil - ciṟappāka intumāk kaṭalil uṇṭu. iḵtaṉṟi intu mattiya taraik kaṭalilum cenniṟap pavaḷak kāṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ācuttirēliyāviṟku vaṭakiḻakkilum mattiyataraik kaṭaṟpakutikaḷākiya morōkkō, alcīriyā, tūṉiciyā, cappāṉ, ayarlāntu, ittāli mutaliya nāṭukaḷilum pavaḷaṅkaḷ kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa.
niṟaṅkaḷ
pavaḷaṅkaḷ palvēṟu vaṇṇaṅkaḷ uḷḷavai. civappu, veṇmai kalanta civappu, veṇmai, kaṟuppu, nīlam pōṉṟa niṟaṅkaḷil pavaḷaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. tamiḻarkaḷ civappu niṟam vāynta pavaḷattai virumpuvar.
kuṇam
kōvaik kaṉi pōṉṟa niṟam, viruttam (vaṭṭam) nīḷam, mēṭu paḷḷamiṉmai, maḻamaḻappu, vaṭuviṉmai, periyavai - pavaḷattiṉ ciṟanta kuṇaṅkaḷākum.
kuṟṟam
uḷḷē tuḷai, vaḷaivu, kal uṭaiyavai kuṟṟam uṭaiya pavaḷamākum. veṇmai, cāmpal niṟam, kurūrat tōṟṟam, citaivu paṭṭiruttal, tuḷaiyāy iruttal, kaṉamiṉmai, tūya veṇmai, cora corappu, veḷuppu kōṇal ivai kuṟṟamākum.
maruttuva nūlil pavaḷam 
pavaḷa pacuppam curatōcam, kācam, iratta pittam, iratta cūlai, iratta picāram, pirataranōy, malapantam mutaliya piṇikaḷaip pōkkum. itaip pala aṉupāṉa māṟṟattōṭu cāppiṭṭāl eṇṇiṟanta nōykaḷ nīṅkum.
cōtiṭa nūlil pavaḷam
cevvāykkup pavaḷam uriyatu. cevvāy uccamāṉavarkaḷum cevvāy tōcam uḷḷavarkaḷum pavaḷam aṇintāl nalamuṟu vārkaḷ eṉpar cōtiṭa vallunar.
kuṟippukaḷ
vairamaṇi vaṇikattil nalla vallunar eṉap pukaḻ peṟṟa ciṅkappūr nipuṇar keṉṉat pullarṭṭaṉ vairaṅkaḷiṉ taraṅkaḷ paṟṟik kūṟumpoḻutu, potuvāka vairam ataṉ niṟam, teḷivu (tūymai), veṭṭu ākiyavaikaḷē ataṉ tarattaik kāṭṭum eṉṟu kūṟuvar.
uyarnta vairam 
nērttiyāṉa iḷañcivappu niṟam uḷḷatu vilai uyarntatu. atu kiṭaippatu aritiṉum aritu. peritum iyalpāṉa nīla veḷḷai niṟa vairaṅkaḷum vilai uyarntavaitām. tūya veḷḷai vairaṅkaḷ aṭuttavai.
maṭṭa vairam
ciṟitu mañcaḷ vaṇṇam vāynta vairamaṇikaḷ taraṅ kuṟaintaṉavākum. āṉāl cila uṇmaiyāṉa mañcaḷ vaṇṇam vāynta vairaṅkaḷ vilai mikuntaṉa.
tūymaiyaik kaṇṭupiṭippatu
pūtak kaṇṇāṭi mūlam vairattil uḷḷa veṭippukaḷum miṉukkamum kāṇamuṭiyum. vairattiṉ veṭṭu mūlam miṉukkattai aḷaviṭa muṭiyum.
paṭṭaṅkaḷ (paṭṭaikaḷ)
potuvāka oru vairamaṇiyil 58 paṭṭaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭ ṭirukkum. mēlē 33 kīḻē 25. antap paṭṭaikaḷ cariyāṉa kōṇaṅ kaḷil illāviṭṭāl oḷi cariyāka virintu paravātu. ataṉāl oḷi kuṉṟum - atāvatu paḷapaḷappu illāmaṟ pōkum.
vairattil tūymaiyaiviṭa niṟantāṉ mukkiyam, tūya mañcaḷ vaṇṇam vāynta vairamaṇiyaiviṭa ilēcāṉa kōṭukaḷ uḷḷa oru veḷḷai vairattai nāṉ peritum virumpuvēṉ eṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṉṟār.1

	“Like almost every other activity this ministers to the comfort of man and gratifies his taste for the beautiful; the search for and retrieving of diamonds from the earth has had a history in this an ancient interesting and romantic one - As far as we knew diamonds were first found in central India, thus carrying out the tradition that very many of the precious and semiprecious stones Originate in the Orient.”

	“The ancient diamond workings or India were scattered over quite a wide area but the distributing centre or mart was the town of Golconda, a name which has always been highly suggestive of opulence. As far back as that ancient epic, the Mahabharata, diamonds have figured largely in the life and history of the Hindoos and it was from India that they were introduced into Europe.”

8. pēraṇikalaṉkaḷ
talai, kātu, kaḻuttu aṇikaḷ
tamiḻ makkaḷ, ulōkak kālattai aṭainta poḻutu tāṉ, avarkaḷ poṉ, veḷḷi, cempu, irumpu, īyam pōṉṟa tātup poruḷkaḷaik kaṇṭaṉar. avarkaḷ tātup poruḷkaḷāl uṇavup poruḷkaḷaip perukkuvataṟkuriya koḻu, arivāḷ, maṇveṭṭi, kōṭāri, kūntālam, uḷi, vāḷ pōṉṟa karuvikaḷai mutalil uruvākkiṉar. appāl piṟa toḻil ceyvataṟkuriya cuttiyal, kuṟaṭu, paṟṟukkuṟaṭu, cāmaṇam, poṭi veṭṭi aram, kaivāḷ, kuḻal, āṭutamar, nūl caṭṭam mutaliyavaikaḷai uruvāk kiṉar. ataṉ piṉṉartāṉ poṉ, veḷḷi mutaliya tātup poruḷkaḷil aṇikalaṉkaḷaic ceyya muṟpaṭṭaṉar.
makkaḷ, vayiṟāra uṇṭu kaḷitta piṉṉartāṉ, taṅkaḷai aḻaku ceytu koḷḷa nāṭip poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷaic ceytu aṇiya muṟpaṭṭaṉar. antap poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷum tamiḻ makkaḷ ātiyil kaṇṭa malar aṇiyākavum arumpu aṇiyākavum kāy aṇiyākavumē pariṇamittaṉa.
itaippaṟṟi āḻntu ārāyntu aṟinta aṟiñarkaḷ, tamiḻarkaḷ kuṟiñci nila vāḻkkaiyil malar aṇikaḷaiyum arumpu aṇikaḷaiyum kaṇṭaṉar. mullai nila vāḻkkaiyilē ilai, taḻai, ōlai, koṭi mutaliyavaikaḷai aṇiyāka aṇintuvantaṉar. maruta nila vāḻkkaiyilē malar, arumpu, ilai, ōlai, koṭi, piñcu, kāy, paḻam, koṭṭai pōṉṟavaikaḷai aṇikaḷāka aṇintu vantaṉar. piṉ, maruta nilamākkaḷē nel mālai, miḷaku mālai, kottamalli mālai, kuṉṟimaṇi mālai, marutaṅkāy mālai, nellikkāy mālai, māmpiñcuk kolucu, picci arumpu mālai, kaṉṉap pū, cuṇṭaikkāy mālai, uruttirākka mālai, tāmaraik kāy mālai pōṉṟavaikaḷai aṇintu vantaṉar. neytal nilavāḻkkaiyilē cippi cōvi, caṅku kōvañci, muttu, pavaḷam mutaliyavaikaḷaik kātaṇiyākavum, kaiyaṇi yākavum kaḻuttaṇiyākavum talaiyaṇiyākavum aṇintu vantaṉar.
makkaḷ maṇṇālum, marattālum, kallālum aṇikalaṉkaḷ ceyyamuṟpaṭṭaṉar. maruntup poruḷkaḷāl kūṭa uruṇṭai yāṉa maṇikaḷ ceytu mālaikaḷākak kōttut kaḻuttil aṇin taṉar. elumpukaḷālum kaṇṇāṭi pōṉṟa kallukaḷālum mālaikaḷ ceytu aṇintu vantaṉar. cemmaṇṇālum, kallā lum, maruntālum, paḷiṅku pōṉṟa kallālum ceyyappaṭṭa aṇikaḷ cila eṉṉiṭam uḷḷaṉa. avai cumār 3000 āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷaṉavāka matikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
āṉāl, tamiḻakattil ulōka kālattilētāṉ uyarnta nākarikam untiyatu; ariya kalaikaḷ arumpiṉa. ōlai, ilai, kuḻai, kompu, koṭi, arumpu, pū, kāy, paḻam, koṭṭai, caṅku, cippi vaṭivākavē ulōkaṅkaḷil aṇikalaṉkaḷ ceytu aṇintu vantaṉar. avaikaḷiṉ vaṇṇaṅkaḷ taṅkaḷiṉ poṉ aṇikaḷil tikaḻumāṟu marakatam (paccai) nīlam (nīlam) vairam (veḷḷai) māṇikkam (veḷuriya civappu) puṭparākam (mañcaḷ) pavaḷam (karuñcivappu) mutaliya maṇikaḷaip patittu ilai, malar, kāy, paḻam, koṭṭai mutaliyavaikaḷiṉ vaṇṇam oḷirumpaṭi aṇi kalaṉkaḷ ceytu aṇintaṉar. ellāvaṟṟaiyumviṭac ciṟappu anta aṇikaḷukku ilai, pū, kāy eṉṟa anta ātippeyarai maṟavātu cūṭṭi viṭṭatākum. itu tamiḻarkaḷiṉ kalaivaḷarcciyaiyum kalaippaṇpaiyum kāṭṭuvatākum.
iṉṟu tamiḻakattil ettaṉaiyō nākarikaṅkaḷ vantu muṭṭi mōti vantuḷḷaṉa. eṉṟālum tamiḻaka makaḷir aṇintu vanta naṉiciṟanta aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ peyarai acaikka muṭiyavillai. paṇṭait tamiḻar kaṇṭa aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ peyarum uruvum tiruvum māṟā vaṇṇam iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa.
tamiḻarkaḷ aṇintu vanta ātikāla aṇikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ, aṭiyil varumāṟākum :
1. talaiyaṇi
tāḻampū, tāmaraippū, corukuppū, cāmantip pū, aṭukku mallikaippū, ilai, aracilai, patumam, caram pūrappāḷai, kōtai, valampuri.
2. kātaṇi
tōṭu, koppu, ōlai, kuḻai, ilai, kuvaḷai, kontiḷa vōlai, kaṉṉappū, muruku, viciṟi muruku, ciṉṉappū, vallikai, cevippū, maṭal.
3. kaḻuttaṇikaḷ
kottu, koṭi, tālikkoṭi, kottamalli mālai, miḷaku mālai, picciyarumpu mālai, nellikkāy mālai, marutaṅkāy mālai, cuṇṭakkāy mālai, kaṭumaṇi mālai, tāḻampū aṭṭikai, māṅkāy mālai, mātuḷaṅkāy mālai, kāraippū aṭṭikai, arumpuccaram, malarccaram, kaṇṭa caram, kaṇṭamālai, kōtai mālai, kōvai pōṉṟaṉa.
4. puya aṇikalaṉkaḷ
kontikkāy
5.kai aṇikalaṉkaḷ.
kāppū, kontikkāyk kolucu
6. kaiviral aṇikalaṉkaḷ
civantippū mōtiram, arumpu, vaṭṭappū.
7. kāl aṇikalaṉkaḷ
māmpiñcuk kolucu, attikkāyk kolucu, ālaṅkāyk kolucu pōṉṟaṉa.
ivaṟṟil navamaṇikaḷum patikkappeṟṟaṉa. nākarikam vaḷara, vaḷara ovvoru uṟuppilum palvēṟu aṇikaḷ palvēṟu muṟai yil apiviruttiyuṟṟum vaḷarccipeṟṟum vantuḷḷaṉa. iḵtaṉṟi caṅkuvaḷai, pulippaltāli, pulinakattāli pōṉṟavaikaḷum uṇṭu.
iṉṟaiyat tamiḻaka makaḷiraiviṭa muṟkāla makaḷir aḻakiya aṇikalaṉkaḷai maṭṭumaṉṟi viḷaiyuyarnta maṇikaḷ patitta, ariya vēlaippāṭukaḷ niṟainta aṇikalaṉkaḷaiyellām aṇintu vantaṉar. eṉavē paṇṭaikkālat tamiḻnāṭṭup pūvaiyar namatu paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil āyiḻaiyār eṉṟu kūṟap peṟ ṟuḷḷaṉar. itaṉāl peṇkaḷ aṇikalaṉ peyarāl aḻaikkap paṭṭaṉar eṉṟu terikiṟatu. tamiḻ makaḷir muṟṟuppeṟṟa vēlaip pāṭukaḷaiyuṭaiya aṇikalaṉkaḷai aṇintu vantaṉar. ataṉāl avarkaḷ aṇinta aṇikalaṉkaḷ muṟṟiḻai eṉṟu aḻaikkap peṟṟaṉa.
kōttu aṇintu koḷvatai kōvai eṉṟaṉar (maṇik kōvai). toṭuttu aṇivatait toṭai eṉṟu moḻintaṉar. acaintu āṭattakkatai mālai (āram) eṉṟaṉar (nellikkāy mālai). oru taramāy oḻuṅkuṟa amaintu koṇṭatai caram eṉṟaṉar (muttuccaram, vaṉṉacaram). koṭikaḷai muṟukki aṇivataik koṭi eṉṟaṉar (pūṅkoṭip poṟkalam). vaṭivam paṟṟi ilai, kuḻai eṉṟaṉar (aracilai, poṟkuḻai). ōlai pōṉṟavaṟṟai ōlai eṉṟaṉar (poṉṉōlai). kāppāka aṇi vataik kāppu eṉṟaṉar. (poṟkāppu). taṭaiyāka aṇivatait taṭaiyam eṉṟum kūṟiṉar. kiṇkiṇ eṉṟu olippataik kiṇkiṇi eṉṟaṉar. kaṇṇiyākat toṭuttu aṇivataik kaṇṇi eṉṟaṉar (poṟkaṇṇi).
ātikālattil caṅkukaḷāl mōtiraṅkaḷum vaḷaikaḷum (kāppukaḷum) kuḻaikaḷum (kātaṇikaḷum) aṇintu vantaṉar. piṟkālattil ulōkaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiyatum ulōka aṇikaḷukkum avvaṭivam paṟṟi valampuriccaṅku, poṉvaḷai, poṟkuḻai, poṟṟoṭi eṉap peyar vaittuḷḷaṉar.
talai aṇikalaṉkaḷ
1. makuṭaṅkaḷ
1. kirīṭa makuṭam 2. karaṇṭa makuṭam 3. maṇi makuṭam (irattiṉa makuṭam) 4. caṭā makuṭam
talaimītu aṇiyappaṭum aṇikalaṉkaḷ talaiyaṇi eṉap paṭum. makkaḷ uṭampil, talaimika mukkiya uṟuppākaiyiṉāl talaimītu aṇiyappaṭum aṇikalaṉkaḷum ciṟappuṭaiyaṉavāka matikkappeṟṟaṉa. talaiyil vilaiyuyarnta aṇikaḷaiyē aṇivar. poṉṉum maṇiyum iṭam peṟuvataṟkuriya ciṟappāṉa iṭam - mutal iṭam talaiyēyākum.
tamiḻarkaḷil, āṇ makkaḷ talaiyiṉ makuṭattait tavira vēṟu talaiyaṇi etuvum aṇintatākat teriyavillai. makuṭam maṉṉarkaḷtām  aṇivārkaḷēyaṉṟi piṟar aṇiyamāṭṭārkaḷ. paṇṭaik kālat tamiḻ āṇ makkaḷ talaiyil caṭāmakuṭattaiyum talaippākai pōṉṟavaikaḷaiyum tavira vēṟu poṉ aṇikaḷ etuvum aṇintatāka namakkut teriyavillai. āṉāl paṇṭaik kālattil āṇ makkaḷ peṇ makkaḷaippōl talaiyil muṭi vaittiruntārkaḷ eṉṟu tiṭṭavaṭṭamākat terikiṟatu.
āṉāl, āṇ makkaḷ talaiyaṇi maṭṭuntāṉ aṇiya villaiyēyoḻiya kaḻuttil mālaikaḷum kātil kuṇṭalaṅkaḷum puyam, kai, iṭuppu, toṭai, kāl pātam mutaliya uṟuppukaḷil pala aṇikalaṉkaḷum aṇintu vantuḷḷārkaḷ eṉṟu naṉku teri kiṟatu.
āṉāl āṇ makkaḷ talaiyaṇi maṭṭuntāṉ aṇiya villaiyēyoḻiya kaḻuttil mālaikaḷum kātil kuṇṭalaṅkaḷum puyam, kai, iṭuppu, toṭai, kāl pātam mutaliya uṟuppukaḷil pala aṇikalaṉkaḷum aṇintu vantuḷḷārkaḷ eṉṟu naṉku teri kiṟatu.
makuṭam
varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṉṉiruntu tamiḻarkaḷ talaiyil ētāvatu aṇintu varuvatu marapāka iruntu varukiṟatu. ātiyil taṭpaveṭpa nilai, mūḷaiyaip pātikkāmal iruppataṟkākat talaiyai, ilai, tuṇi mutaliyavaṟṟāl maṟaittirukkalām. mūḷai irukkum pakutiyākiya talai pātukāppāka irukkumpaṭi ataic cuṟṟi valuvāṉa maṇṭaiyōṭu iyaṟkaiyilē amaintirukkiṟatu. anta maṇṭai ōṭum eḷitil tākkuṟāmal irukkavē mēl tōlum ataṉ mēl aṭarttiyāṉa mayirkaḷum irukkiṉṟaṉa. makkaḷ ataṟku iṉṉum pātukāppu aḷippataṟkākak koṇṭai, caṭāmakuṭam, caṭāpāram, caṭā maṇṭalam mutaliyavaikaḷai amaittaṉar. avaikaḷum ñāyiṟu veppattai īrppatai eṇṇi talaippākai (ciratarakam) kaṇṭaṉar. ēṉ? aḷakacūṭam, tammillam mutaliyavaikaḷaiyum kaṇṭaṉar. makkaḷ nākarikam eytiya piṉ, maṉṉarkaḷ makuṭam amaittaṉar. anta makuṭaṅkaḷai, kalaippulamai vāynta ciṟpikaḷ poṉṉālum maṇikaḷālum ceytu nāluvakaiyāka amaittaṉar. avaikaḷāvaṉa :
(1) kirīṭa makuṭam (2) karaṇṭa makuṭam (3) irattiṉa makuṭam (4) caṭāmakuṭam eṉappaṭum.
makuṭam ātiyil talaiyaip pātukākkum kavacamāka eḻuntu, appāl aḻaku ceyyum aṇikalaṉkaḷil oṉṟāka malarntuḷḷatu. piṉ atu teyvaṅkaḷukkum maṉṉarkaḷukkum uriya ciṟappāṉa ciṉṉaṅkaḷil oṉṟākat tikaḻntatu. namatu tamiḻ nikaṇṭil iṟaivaṉukkuriya iṉṟiyamaiyāta poruḷkaḷ palavaṟṟil, makuṭam (muṭiyum) oṉṟākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.2
muṭi - atāvatu makuṭam talaiyaṇiyāyt tōṉṟi appāl teyvaṅkaḷukkum pēraracarkaḷukkum uriya poruḷāyp pariṇamit tuḷḷatu. muṭiyaip peritum ciṟṟaracarkaḷ aṇiyārkaḷ. pēraracar kaḷē aṇivārkaḷ. aracarkaḷiṉ perumaiyaik kuṟippiṭum poḻutu muṭiyuṭaiyamaṉṉarkaḷ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭum. muṭi - atāvatu makuṭam ovvoru maṉṉarum ovvoru vitamāka aṇintu vantaṉar. itu nāṭṭiṟku nāṭu, kālattiṟkuk kālam vēṟupaṭṭu viḷaṅkiyatu. itu kirīṭam, maṇi makuṭam, muṭi, mauli, cirōṉmaṇi eṉṟellām kūṟappaṭum.
nālvakai makuṭaṅkaḷil kirīṭa makuṭamum irattiṉa makuṭamum tirumāl mutaliya pōka mūrttikaḷukkum, karaṇṭa makuṭam piḷḷaiyār mutaliya kaṇaṅkaḷiṉ talaimait teyvaṅkaḷukkum caṭāmakuṭam civaṉ, nāṉmukaṉ mutaliya teyvaṅkaḷukkum uriyaṉa.
vīravallavar eḻutiya parata cāttiram mūṉṟuvita makuṭaṅ kaḷaip paṟṟik kūṟukiṟatu. avai (1) pārcavākatam (pārcava makuṭam) (2) mataki (3) kiritti eṉappaṭum.
potuvākat teyvaṅkaḷum kantarvarkaḷ, yākarkaḷ, nākarkaḷ (paṉṉakarkaḷ) arakkarkaḷ ākiyavarkaḷum pārcavamakuṭam tarippārkaḷ. eṉavē nāṭakattil avvēṭam pūṇṭu naṭippavar kaḷum pārcava makuṭattai aṇiya vēṇṭum.
kiruttik eṉṉum kirīṭattai uyarnta teyvaṅkaḷum, mataki eṉṉum makuṭattai naṭuttarat teyvaṅkaḷum aṇiyum. eṉavē inta teyvaṅkaḷiṉ vēṭampuṉaipavarkaḷ ivviraṇṭu makuṭat taiyum aṇiya vēṇṭum. cātāraṇat teyva vēṭam puṉaivarkaḷ pārcava makuṭattai aṇiyalām.
maṉṉarkaḷ mataki mātiriyāṉa makuṭaṅkaḷai aṇital vēṇṭum. vittiyātararkaḷ cittarkaḷ, kāraṇarkaḷ talaimayirāl kaṭṭappaṭṭa kēca makuṭam aṇiyalām.
amaiccar, taḷapati, vaṇikak kuḻuviṉ talaivarkaḷ kurukkaḷ pōṉṟōr talaippākai aṇivatāl avvēṭam puṉaipavar kaḷum talaippākai aṇiyalām.  taḷapatikaḷum iḷavaracarkaḷum ciṟumakuṭam (artta makuṭam) aṇivar; muṉivarkaḷ caṭā makuṭam aṇivar. ivvēṭam puṉaipavarkaḷum immakuṭaṅkaḷ aṇiya vēṇṭum.
maṇimakuṭaṅkaḷum, māmakuṭaṅkaḷum maṉṉarkaḷālum teyvaṅkaḷālum kiṟittava ūḻikku muṉpē tamiḻakattil aṇiyap paṭṭiruppatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. teyvaṅkaḷukkuriya aṇiyil makuṭam iṉṟiyamaiyātatu. inta makuṭam ovvoru nāṭṭilum ovvoru vitamāy avarkaḷiṉ aṟivu, āṟṟal, celvam, kalai mutaliyavaikaḷukkēṟpa amaintirukkiṟatu. tamiḻakattil cērarkaḷ, cōḻarkaḷ pallavarkaḷ mutaliya maṉṉarkaḷ vevvēṟu vitamāṉa muṭikaḷaip puṉaintuḷḷārkaḷ. avarkaḷ nāṭṭil eḻunta teyvaṅ kaḷum vevvēṟu vitamāṉa aḻakiya makuṭaṅkaḷōṭu viḷaṅku kiṉṟaṉa. ciṟṟaṇṇal vāyil, acantā, cikiriyā pārkūt mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa ōviyaṅkaḷilum maturai, tañcai, kāñci, māmalla puram, amarāpati, kāñci mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa ciṟpaṅkaḷi lum palvēṟu vakaiyāṉa makuṭaṅkaḷaiyum talaiyaṇikaḷaiyum kāṇkiṉṟōm.
tirumāl intiraṉ mutaliya teyvaṅkaḷum vēṟu pala ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷum aṭiparuttu nuṉiciṟutta aḻakiya vēlaippāṭuḷḷa muṭikaḷaip puṉaintuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu aṟikiṉṟōm. piṟkālattil ellāt teyvaṅkaḷum oru niyatikkuṭpaṭṭuc ciṟcila vitamāṉa makuṭaṅkaḷai aṇintiruppatāka aṟikiṉṟōm.
tamiḻakattiṉ makuṭaṅkaḷ pala ariyavēlaippāṭamaintaṉa vāy nalla vaḷarcciyaip peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa. nām pallavarkaḷ kālattiliruntu iṉṟuvarai, atāvatu ki.pi. 6vatu nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal 20ām nūṟṟāṇṭu varai makuṭaṅkaḷiṉ apiviruttiyai ārāyntāl pala vicayaṅkaḷai aṟiyalām. ovvoru kālamum ataṉ apiviruttiyaic cuṭṭik kāṭṭuvatāy amaintuḷḷatu. ataṉuṭaiya aḻakiya vēlaippāṭukaḷiṉ eḷimai ataṉuṭaiya paḻaiya paṇpāṭṭiṟku oru cōtaṉaiyākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ivvitat tōṟṟam ataṉ kālattai nirṇayikkattakka karuviyāy viḷaṅkukiṟatu. makkaḷiṉ nākarika vaḷarcciyiṉ amcaṅkaḷ aṉaittum ammakuṭaṅkaḷil naṉku piratipalikkiṉṟaṉa.
1. ki)rīṭa makuṭam
ik kirīṭa makuṭam mūṅkil ilaipōṉṟu tikaḻum. iḵtiṉṟi ciṟparattiṉam eṉṉum nūliṉ ilakkaṇa vitiyai aṉucarittu muṭṭai vaṭivamākavum irukkum. tāmarai mukai, kuṭai, āmai, vaṭivamākac ceyyappaṭuvatum uṇṭu. immakuṭam poṉṉāl ceyyappeṟṟu vilaiyuyarnta maṇikaḷ patikkappeṟṟirukkum. nālu pakkaṅkaḷilum makara uruvattōṭu koṭikaḷum ilaikaḷum taḷir kaḷum pūkkaḷum arumpukaḷum aṇi ceyyum. muttuk kōvai kaḷum vilaiyuyarnta maṇikaḷum iṭam peṟṟilaṅkum. neṟṟikku mēl uṭaṉaṭiyāy makuṭattaic cuṟṟi akaṉṟa oru paṭṭapantam irukkum. makuṭattiṉ maṟṟap pakutikaḷ mauli pantaṅkaḷālum ceṭi koṭikaḷālum eḻil peṟṟilaṅkum. makuṭattiṉ aṭip pākam iḷam piṟaiyaip pōṉṟu vaḷaintirukkum mutal vakai tirumāliṉ paṭimaṅkaḷilum cakkiravarttikaḷiṭattilum kāṇap paṭum.
immakuṭam 16, 18, 24 aṅkulam  eṉṟu mūṉṟu vakaiyil amaiyum. ivvāṟāka amaikkappaṭum aḻakiya kirīṭa makuṭattil ucci (cikaram) mika aḻakāka ilaṅkum. ivai 3, 5, 7, eṇṇikkai yuḷḷa kumiḻkaḷ pōṉṟa uṟuppukaḷuṭaṉ tikaḻum. paṭimaṅkaḷiṉ amaippukku ēṟṟavāṟu eṇṇikkaikaḷ kuṟaintum kūṭiyum irukkum. inta kirīṭa makuṭam, muṭṭai (aṇṭam) vaṭivamākavō, tāmarai malar, kuṭai, āmai pōṉṟa vaṭivamākavō amaiya vēṇṭum eṉṟu ciṟpa vitikaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
inta makuṭaṅkaḷiṉ ciṟappai vaittu atai aṇintirukkum teyvam allatu aracaṉiṉ āṟṟal, perumai mutaliyaṉa teriyak kūṭum. intak kirīṭa makuṭattil ulakil uyarnta vairam, māṇikkam, muttu marakatam pōṉṟa maṇikaḷ iṭam peṟṟirukkum. maṉṉaṉiṉ mariyātai, māṉam, kauravam ellām inta makuṭattilē aṭaṅki irukkum.
2. karaṇṭa makuṭam
karaṇṭa makuṭam aṭiyiṉiṉṟu muṭivarai paṭippaṭiyāka cuṟṟaḷavu kuṟaintu koṇṭē pōy makuṭattiṉ nuṉi, mokku (arumpu) pōṉṟu kuvintu kāṇappaṭum itu 3, 4, 5 karaṇṭaṅkaḷ uḷḷatāka irukkum. maṟṟavai kirīṭamakuṭam pōṉṟu irukkum.
teḷivākak kūṟuvatāṉāl karaṇṭa makuṭam oru karaṇṭaka vaṭivākac ceyyappaṭṭa oru muṭi eṉṟu kūṟalām. itu oru kiṇṇam pōṉṟa vaṭivuḷḷatāka irukkum. itu neṭṭai yākavō allatu kuṭṭaiyākavō allatu oru pakkam kuvin tirukkāmalō aḻakāka irukkum. itu tōṟṟattil mikac ciṟi tākavē kāṇappaṭum. pala karaṇṭaṅkaḷ aṭiyum nuṉiyum oṉṟākak kāṇappaṭum. pāttira aṭukkukaḷaip pōl oṉṟiṉ mītu oṉṟāka 3,5,7 varicaikaḷākap poṟittiruppatu ataṉ uruvattai ciṟitāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṟatu. intak karaṇṭa makuṭaṅkaḷ perumpālum tēvikaḷ, piḷḷaiyār, iḷamurukaṉ, aṉumāṉ mutaliya teyvaṅ kaḷiṭattilum pēraracarkaḷukkuk kīḻpaṭṭa kuṟu nila maṉṉarkaḷiṭat tilum kāṇappaṭum. intak karaṇṭa makuṭa vakaikaḷai vēṟu eṅkum kāṇamuṭiyātu. itu tamiḻakattiṟkē oru ciṟappāka amainta makuṭamāka eṇṇappaṭṭu varukiṟatu.
intak karaṇṭa makuṭamum poṉṉāl ceyyappaṭṭatē yākum. itilum vilaiyuyarnta vairam marakatam māṇikkam marakata nīlam pōṉṟa palvēṟu vaṇṇak kaṟkaḷ patikkappaṭṭirukkum. itu pārvaikkuc ciṟitākak kāṇappaṭṭālum eḻiluṭaṉ tikaḻum. itaṉ ciṟpa vēlaippāṭukaḷ piṟamakuṭaṅkaḷaiviṭa eḷḷaḷavum kuṟaintaṉavalla.
3. irattiṉa makuṭam
irattiṉa makuṭam eṉṟāl maṇikaḷ patitta aṇi makuṭam eṉṟē poruḷpaṭum. itu karaṇṭa makuṭattai viṭa uyaramākavum aḻakākavum irukkum. immakuṭam poṉṉāl ceyyappaṭṭu oṉpatu vitamāṉa maṇikaḷ aḻuttappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. itu atika māṉa maṇikaḷ iḻaittuc ceyyappaṭṭatāl irattiṉa makuṭam eṉṉum peyar peṟṟatu. irattiṉa makuṭam civaperumāṉ pōṉṟa yōka muṟaiyait taḻuvi niṟkum teyvattiṟkuriyataṉṟu. pōka muṟaiyai nāṭum tirumāl, tēvikaḷ pōṉṟa teyvaṅkaḷ aṇiya lām. maṉṉarkaḷukku irattiṉa makuṭam ēṟṟatu.
makuṭattiṉ naṭuvil muṉpakkam periya vairam allatu māṇikkam allatu marakatam vaittu irukkalām. atu ellā vaṟṟaiyumviṭa periyatāy ñāyiṟu pōṉṟu tikaḻum. itaic cuṟṟic ceṭi koṭikaḷum malarkaḷum kāykaṉikaḷum poṉṉāṟ ceyyappeṟṟu iṭai iṭaiyē maṇikaḷ patippikkappeṟ ṟirukkum.
inta makuṭaṅkaḷ maṉṉarkaḷiṉ celvattaiyum valimaiyai yum perumaiyaiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭuvaṉavāka irukkum. makuṭaṅ kaḷukkākap pala pōrkaḷ nikaḻntatuṇṭu. eṇṇaṟṟa uyirkaḷai oru makuṭattiṟkākap paliyiṭṭatumuṇṭu.
tamiḻakattiṟku urittāṉa cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyarkaḷ maṭṭu maṉṟip pallavarkaḷiṉ maṇimakuṭaṅkaḷum eṅku ceṉṟaṉa eṉṟu nammāl kaṇṭupiṭikkavē muṭiyavillai. avaikaḷil etuvum nammai iṟutiyāka āṇṭa āṅkilēyarkaḷiṉ tol poruṭ kāṭci cālaiyilō allatu pirañcu, ulāntākkārarkaḷiṉ tol poruḷ kāṭcicālaiyilō iruppatākat teriyavillai. avai kaḷaik kāṇavēṇṭum eṉṟu akkarai nammiṭam illai. āṉāl namatu varuṅkāla cantatikaḷukkāvatu avvācai varavēṇṭum eṉpatu ematu eṇṇam.
4. caṭāmakuṭam
caṭā makuṭam ulōka kālattiṟku muntiya makkaḷiṉ nākarikattil eḻunta oru talaiyaṇiyākum. intac caṭā makuṭam  caṭaimuṭiyāl toppi allatu oru muṭiccaip pōṉṟu koṇṭai piṉṉappaṭṭuk kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum. caṭai muṟkālat til kuṭimallam iliṅka uruvil kāṇappaṭṭatu pōl muṟukkap paṭṭa muṭiyāka allāmal piṉṉappaṭṭa muṭiyākak kāṇappaṭum; itu piṟkālattil muṟukkiya muṭiyākiviṭṭatu. ākamaṅkaḷ aṉait tilum  kuṟippāka uttara kāmikākamattilum itaṉ viḷakkam kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu. ivvitamāṉa makuṭaṅkaḷ pala cakkaraṅkaḷ pōṉṟa uruvaṅkaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭa vēṇṭum. avvaṇikaḷ makara kuṭam, pattirakuṭam, irattiṉakuṭam, puritaṅkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. makarakuṭam eṉpatu muṉpakkattil aṇi ceyyappaṭavēṇṭum. eñciya ēṉaiya pakutikaḷil allatu nāṉku pakkaṅkaḷilum puritaṅkaḷ maṭṭumē iṭam peṟalām, puritaṅkaḷum allatu makara vaṭivaṅkaḷum ceṭi, koṭi, ilai, pū, kāy, uruvaṅkaḷum iṭatu pakkattil allatu valatu pakkattil maṭṭum irukkalām; mēlē irukkakkūṭātu. makarakuṭam muṉ pakkattilum pattirakuṭam allatu irattiṉakuṭam piṉ pakkattilum iṭam peṟṟirukkalām.5
caṭāmakuṭam civaperumāṉukkē uriyatu. caṭā makuṭattiṉ valatu pakkattil veḷ erukkam pū, pāmpu, kaṅkātēvi uruvaṅ kaḷum iṭatu pakkattil iḷam piṟai uruvamum kāṇappaṭa vēṇṭum. itaṉ mūlam civaperumāṉ ātikālattil atāvatu ulōka kālam arumpummuṉ atāvatu kaṟkālattil eḻunta paḻam perum teyvam eṉpatai uṟutippaṭuttukiṟatu. arappā nākarika mum civaperumāṉ 5000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē - āriyarallātār kaṇṭa perunteyvam eṉpatai uṟutippaṭuttu kiṟatu.
talai aṇikalaṅkaḷ (ā)
