11. ciṟpa nūṟ cāṉṟukaḷ
namatu  muṟkāla aṇikalaṉkaḷil mikat toṉmaiyāṉavai kaḷai ippoḻutu kāṇpatē aritu. makkaḷ iṉṟu paḻaṅkālap pāṇikaḷil ceyyappaṭṭa aṇikalaṉkaḷai aṇiya virumpuvatē illai. aṇiya virumpāviṭṭālum, avaikaḷaip pātukāttu vaittirukkiṟārkaḷ eṉṟum kūṭac colvataṟkillai. āṉāl airōppiyarkaḷ ulakil atikamākap putumaiyai virumpupavarkaḷ. avarkaḷ āṭaikaḷum aṇikalaṉkaḷum illaṅkaḷum taṭṭu muṭṭuc cāmāṉkaḷum ovvoru nūṟṟāṇṭilum putup putu māṟṟam aṭaintu vantuḷḷaṉa. nām āṇṭāṇṭu tōṟum peṟṟu vanta airōppiya nākarikam ippoḻutu nāḷukku nāḷ māṟṟam aṭaintu varukiṉṟatu. eṉṟālum mēṉāṭṭār paḻamai yāṉa paṇṭaṅkaḷaiyō, āṭaikaḷaiyō aṇikalaṉkaḷaiyō, kōppaikaḷaiyō, taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷaiyō aḻittu viṭuvatillai; pattiramākap pātukāttu varukiṟārkaḷ. ammakkaḷ taṅkaḷ illattiṟku varum viruntiṉarkaḷukku, itu eṅkaḷ koḷḷup pēraṉār upayōkitta nāṟkāli, itu eṅkaḷ pāṭṭi aṇinta aṇikalaṉ, atu eṅkaḷ aṉṉai eḻutiya peṉcil, itu eṉatu māmiyār eḻutiya ūṟṟuppēṉā eṉṟellām eṭuttuk kāṭṭi makiḻkiṉṟārkaḷ. tamiḻarkaḷ vīṭṭil attakaiya paḻam poruḷkaḷaip pātukāttu vaippatē kiṭaiyātu. eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka eṉatu vīṭṭil eṉ maṉaivi eṉatu tāyār upayōkitta aṇikalaṉkaḷaiyō, āṭaikaḷaiyō, veṇkalap pāttiraṅ kaḷaiyō pātukāttu vaikkavillai, vaikka virumpuvatumillai. avaikaḷai viṟṟuviṭṭup putiya paṇṭaṅkaḷai vāṅkuvatē vaḻakkamāyviṭṭatu. ataṉāl namatunāṭṭil paḻaiya pāttiraṅ kaḷaiyō aṇikalaṉkaḷaiyō paḻaiya icaikkaruvikaḷaiyō paḻaiya viḷakkukaḷaiyō allatu paḻaiya paṇṭaṅkaḷaiyō kāṇamuṭivatillai. namatu varalāṟṟup paḻam perumaiyaik kūṟa muṉvarum eḻuttāḷarkaḷ evvita ātāramum iṉṟi allaṟpaṭu kiṉṟārkaḷ. pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷ upayōkitta poṟkācukaḷil oṉṟu kūṭa iṉṟu evarum pattirappaṭutti vaikka villai. ēṉ, maturaiyil āṇṭa muculim maṉṉar kācukaḷaiyum, nāyakkamaṉṉar kācukaḷaiyum, āṅkilak kampeṉi āṭci veḷi yiṭṭa poṟkācukaḷaiyum nām pātukāttu vaikkat tavaṟi viṭṭōm. namatu aṭimaic cakāptattil, camūkam peṟṟa iḻi nilaiyum, poruḷātāra vīḻcciyumē itaṟku mukkiya kāraṇaṅ kaḷākum. iṉṟu namatu nāṭṭil oru cila paḻamaiyāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷ kiṭaippatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. āṉāl avaikaḷum paḻamaiyāṉa kālattil ceyyappaṭṭa aṇikalaṉkaḷākat teriyavillai. avai paḻaiya peyarkaḷaip peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa; āṉāl uruvattil perum māṟutal peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē paḻaiya peyarkaḷuṭaṉ ippoḻutu putitākac ceyyappaṭṭa aṇikalaṉkaḷ eṉṟu tiṭṭamāka colla muṭiyum.
namatu ilakkiyaṅkaḷil pala paḻaiya aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avai nām kaṇṭum kēṭṭum aṟiyāta putup peyarkaḷāyum, uruvaṅkaḷāyum uḷḷaṉa. in nilaiyil namatu paḻaiya aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ uṇmaiyāṉa uruvattai eppaṭip pārppatu?
āṉāl namatu ciṟpaṅkaḷil paḻamperum aṇikalaṉkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avai uṟutiyākat tamiḻ nāṭṭup paḻaiya aṇi kalaṉkaḷiṉ uruvamēyākum. āṉāl avaikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷai aṟivatu mikavum aritu. ciṟpa nūṟkaḷil vaṭamoḻiyil peyarkaḷ poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. itaṉāl ivai tamiḻaka aṇikaḷ alla eṉṟu kūṟuvōrum uṇṭu. namatu kāḷiyai vaṭamoḻiyil kauri tēvi eṉṟum, murukaṉai kantaṉ eṉṟum tirumālai mahā viṣṇu eṉṟum civaperumāṉai caṅkaraṉ eṉṟum kūṟiyiruppataik koṇṭu ivai āriyarkaḷiṉ teyvamāki viṭumā? namatu kaik kāppai ata kaṭakam eṉṟu kūṟiviṭṭāl atu āriyarkaḷ aṇi kalaṉākiviṭa muṭiyumā? namatu nāṭṭu aṇikalaṉkaḷukku vaṭa moḻip peyar cūṭṭiyatāl avai tamiḻaka aṇikalaṉkaḷ alla veṉṟu kūṟiviṭamuṭiyātu. namatu teyva uruvaṅkaḷil kāṇap paṭum aṇikalaṉkaḷ aṉaittum tamiḻaka aṇikalaṉkaḷēyākum. oṉṟiraṇṭu ayalaka aṇikalaṉkaḷ kāṇappaṭiṉum avaikaḷai nām eḷitil aṟiya muṭiyum. namatu ilakkiya aṇikalaṉkaḷaip pōlaṉṟi namatu ciṟpa nūṟkaḷil kāṇum aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil iruppatai aṟintu cilar aiyam uṟukiṉṟaṉar, uṇmaiyil ciṟpa nūṟkaḷil kāṇum teyvaṅkaḷ tamiḻar kaṇṭa teyvaṅkaḷākum. anta ciṟpa nūṟkaḷil kāṇum muṟaikaḷum tamiḻar muṟaikaḷēyākum. intap paṭimaṅkaḷai uruvākkum ciṟpi kaḷum tamiḻarkaḷēyāvar. āṉāl nam muṉṉōrkaḷ ekkāraṇatti ṉālō tamiḻ nāṭṭuc ciṟpak kalaiyai vaṭamoḻiyil eḻuti vaittuḷḷārkaḷ. atai vaittu intac ciṟpa nūṟkaḷ nam nūlkaḷ allaveṉṟō aṅkuk kāṇum aṇikalaṉkaḷ nam aṇikalaṉkaḷ allaveṉṟō karutuvatu tavaṟu.
nam ciṟpa nūṟkaḷil palavitamāṉa mālaikaḷum pala vitamāṉa vaḷaikaḷum kāppukaḷum iṭaiyaṇikaḷum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka kaṭakam, kāppu, kolucu, āṭakam, pariyakam, pācittāmaṇi, pavaḷam, palavaḷai, cūṭakam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. ivaikaḷellām oruvar orē camayattil kaiyil aṇiyum aṇikalaṉkaḷā? paṭimaṅkaḷil pala aṇikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaikaḷil kaṭakam etu? āṭakam etu? cūṭakam etu? pariyakam etu? pācittāmaṇi etu eṉṟu aṟivatu? eṉa viṉavukiṉṟārkaḷ. itu nalla kēḷviyēyākum. ciṟpa nūṟkaḷil kāṇum peyarkaḷaip paṭittu vaittuk koṇṭu cilaikaḷiṉ mītuḷḷa aṇikalaṉkaḷaik kaṇṭupiṭippatu ciramantāṉ. itai virumpupavarkaḷ tapatikaḷiṉ tuṇai koṇṭu eḷitil aṟiya muṭiyum. nāṉ cila tapatikaḷiṭam cila aṇikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷaik kūṟi anta aṇikalaṉ eppaṭi irukkum eṉṟu viṉaviṉēṉ. uṭaṉē avarkaḷ cuvarkaḷilum tāḷ kaḷilum peṉcilaik koṇṭu aḻakāka varaintu kāṭṭiviṭ ṭārkaḷ. aṇikalaṉkaḷai aṟiya aṇikalaṉkaḷaip paṟṟi ārāynta  aṟiñarkaḷiṉ ottuḻaippum ciṟpikaḷiṉ ātaravum iruntāl eḷitil ellā aṇikalaṉkaḷaiyum aṟintu viṭa muṭiyum. ārāya muṉvantuḷḷa aṟiñarkaḷiṉ aṟivuk kuṟaivāl cila tavaṟukaḷum eḻum eṉpatai nāṉ iṅkuk kuṟippiṭāmal irukka muṭiyavillai. in nilaiyil namatu tamiḻakattiṉ karuvūlamākak karutap peṟum ciṟpa nūṟkaḷil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭirukkum aṇikalaṉ viḷakkam namatu ārāyccikku mika iṉṟiyamaiyātatākum. avai aṭiyil varumāṟu:
cilparattiṉam eṉṉum cīriya nūliṉ patiṉāṟām atti yāyattil aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇam kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atu varumāṟu :
talaimītu mēl nōkki kūrmaiyākak kāṇappaṭum aṇikalaṉē kirīṭam (makuṭam) ākum. namatu nāṭṭup paṭimaṅkaḷil ellām carva cātāraṇamāka itaip pārkkalām. makuṭaṅkaḷil pala vitam uṇṭu. kirīṭa makuṭam eṉpatu oru vakai. tirumāl uruvaṅkaḷil talaiyil kēcāntattil uṣṇīca paṭṭam oṉṟu talaiyaic cuṟṟiṉavāṟāka oru aṅkula aḷavil amaikkappaṭa vēṇṭum. kirīṭattil aṭippuṟattil talaiyaic cuṟṟit talaip pākai pōl aṅkula akalam uḷḷatāy paṭṭaiyāka irukkum pākamē uṣṇīca paṭṭam eṉappaṭum. itaṉ mēl makuṭattuṭaṉ oḷi vīcum kōṭīrattai amaikka vēṇṭum. kōṭīram, makuṭam eṉṉum coṟkaḷ muṭiyiṉ (kirīṭattiṉ) uṭ pakutiyaik kuṟippaṉa vām. makuṭam eṉpatu kirīṭattiṉ aṭippīṭattaiyum kōṭīram eṉpatu makuṭattiṉ mēṟ pakutiyaiyum uṇarttuvaṉavākum.1 intak kōṭīrattiṉ aḷavu ellā pakkaṅkaḷilum eṭṭaṅkulamē amaiyum.
tirumālukku amaikkappaṭum kirīṭam uṣṇīcattiṉ mēl viḷaṅkum makuṭattuṭaṉ kūṭiyatāka irukka vēṇṭum. ataṉ aḷavu patiṉāṟaṅkulam, patiṉeṭṭaṅkulam, irupattu nāṉku aṅkulam eṉṟu mūṉṟu vakaiyil amaiyum.
ivvāṟu amaikkappaṭum kirīṭattiṉ cikaraṅkaḷai (ucci nuṉi) aḻakuṟa amaikka vēṇṭum. mūṉṟu, aintu allatu ēḻu eṇṇikkaiyuṭaiyatāka irukka vēṇṭum. uruvaṅkaḷiṉ amaip pukku ēṟṟavāṟu ivveṇṇikkai iṭam peṟum. intak kīriṭam aṇṭam (muṭṭai) pōṉṟatākavō tāmarai malar pōṉṟa tākavō kuṭai pōṉṟatākavō āmai pōṉṟatākavō avaravar viruppampōl amaikkappaṭalām.
kēcāntattiliruntu makuṭāntam varai neṟṟiyil paṭṭam amaikkappaṭa vēṇṭum. mukattiṉ akalam evvaḷavō, avvaḷavē makuṭattiṉ akalamum irukka vēṇṭum. makuṭattiṉ aṭippuṟattiṉ akalattil ēḻu eṭṭu allatu oṉpatil oru paṅku kuṟaintatāka ataṉ nuṉiyiṉ akalam irukkum. atil nāṉku pūrimaṅkaḷ irukka vēṇṭum. pūrimaṅkaḷiṉ uyaram paṉṉiraṇṭu aṅkulam. kirīṭa makuṭam allatu karaṇṭa makuṭam camaikkalām. karaṇṭa makuṭattil aṭiyiṉiṉṟu muṭivarai paṭippaṭiyāka akalam kuṟaintu koṇṭē pōkum. makuṭattiṉ muṭi muṇṭaka mokkup pōṉṟatāka amaikka vēṇṭum. karaṇṭa makuṭam mūṉṟu, nāṉku allatu aintu karaṇṭaṅkaḷ uḷḷatāka irukka vēṇṭum.
pattira kuṇṭalaṅkaḷ 3, 4 allatu 5 aṅkulam irukkalām. ivaṟṟiṉ curuḷkaḷiṉ kaṉam oru yavam irukka vēṇṭum. avai veṇmai allatu cenniṟamāka irukkalām.
makarak kuḻai - makaramīṉ vaṭivam pōl uḷḷatu. ciṅka karṉam eṉum kuḻai ciṅkamukam pōṉṟa vaṭivamuḷḷatu. yāṉaik kuḻai yāṉai muka vaṭivil uḷḷatu. kuḻaikaḷ pala vakaiyāy iruppatu pōl kuṇṭalattilum palavakaikaḷuṇṭu. kuṇṭalam, vaṭṭam, tāmaraip pū, tāmarai arumpu pōṉṟa vaṭivilum irukkalām. upakrīvam eṉṉum uḷkaḻuttaṇiyum aṭṭikaiyum mēṟkaḻuttai aṇiceyyum palavitamāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷum aṇivikkalām. uruttirākkamaṇimālai, tuḷaci maṇimālai, tāmaraikkāy maṇi mālai poṉmaṇimālai pala aḻakiya cittira vēlaippāṭu kaḷuṭaṉ viḷaṅkum. uruttirākkamaṇi, tuḷaci maṇimālai poṟ ṟakaṭukaḷāl  potiyap peṟṟu veḷḷik kampiyil kōkkap peṟ ṟirukkum. itōṭu poṉ aṭṭikaiyum takkavāṟu aṇivikka vēṇṭum.
muṉ kaiyil kaṭakam valayam ivaṟṟai aṇivikka vēṇṭum. (muṉ kaiyil aṇiyum vaḷaikaḷ kaṭayam  valayam eṉa iruvakaip paṭum. itaṉ cuṟṟaḷavu ciṟu viraliṉ cuṟṟaḷavu irukkalām. ivvaḷai uruṇṭaiyākavō taṭṭaiyākavō irukkalām. vaḷai pala vakai maṇikaḷ patittuc cittira vēlaippāṭukaḷ uḷḷatāka irukka vēṇṭum.
kaiyiṉ mēṟpuṟattil - atāvatu puyattil kēyūram (vaṅki) eṉum tōḷvaḷaiyai aṇivikka vēṇṭum. eṭṭitaḻt tāmaraip pūvai itil eḻiluṟa amaikkalām. palavakai maṇikaḷai oḷi tikaḻumāṟu itil porutta vēṇṭum. tāmarai malarukkup patil pācik kottup pōṉṟa amaippaiyum uṇṭākkalām. eḻiloḻukum ilaikaḷ pōṉṟa uruvaṅkaḷ itil iṭam peṟa vēṇṭum. itaip pattira pūritam eṉpar. ivvāṟāṉa kēyūrattai puyattiṉ mattiyil aṇivikka vēṇṭum. ippattira pūritattiṉ kaṉam kēyūrattiṉ kaṉam pōṉṟatām. ituvē pākuvalayam (puyattil aṇiyum vaḷai) eṉappaṭum.
kaiviralkaḷiṉ naṭuviralait tavira ēṉaiya nāṉku viral kaḷilum mōtiram aṇivikka vēṇṭum. mōtiram vaṭṭa vaṭiva mākap palavakaiyāṉa maṇikaḷ aḻutti vicittiramāṉa vēlaip pāṭukaḷ ceytu aḻaku niṟaintatāy tiru uruvaṅkaḷukkut takkatāy amaikka vēṇṭum.
mārpil koṅkaikaḷukku eṭṭu aṅkulat tolaivil uracu cūttirattiṉiṉṟu toṅkiyavāṟāka caṉṉavīram eṉṉum aṇi tikaḻa vēṇṭum. itu pūṇūl pōl amaiyum. āṉāl iru tōḷkaḷi ṉiṉṟum toṅkum. (itu kaikaḷiṉ mēl cellāmal uṭampukku maṭṭum aḷavuṭaṉ nirumikkappaṭṭu aṇiyum poṉmayamāṉa caṭṭaiyākum. uṭampiṉ mēṟpakutiyil aṇiyappaṭum aṇikaḷ yāvaṟṟaiyum oṉṟu cērttuk kaṭṭiṉāṟ pōlat tōṉṟuvatum valatu puṟam pūṇūl toṅkukiṟa vakaiyil vayiṟṟil iṭatu puṟam toṅkum muttuccaram pōṉṟatumāṉa oru aṇiyē caṉṉa vīramākum.)
kaḻuttiṉiṉṟu koṅkaikaḷiṉ naṭuvil toṅkumāṟu kiraivēyakāram eṉṟa aṭṭikai vaṭivamāṉa mālaiyai aṇi vikka vēṇṭum. itu palavakai maṇikaḷ aḻuttip poṉṉāl paḷapaḷaveṉa oḷivīcumāṟu ceyyappaṭa vēṇṭum. kaḻuttiliruntu utarapantam varai toṅkumāṟu akkamālai (uruttirākkamālai) aṇivikkappaṭavēṇṭum. allatu tāmarai maṇimālai allatu tuḷacimaṇimālai mutaliyavaikaḷai aṇivikkalām. itai hruṉmālai eṉṟum kūṟuvar. iṉi kantamālai eṉṟa maṟṟoru vakaiyāṉa aṇiyaiyum aṇivikka vēṇṭum. palavakai malarkaḷālāṉa immālai tōḷiṉ mēṟpuṟam toṭaṅkip puyattiṉ mēlēyē irupuṟamum toṅkiyavāṟu irukka vēṇṭum.)
araiyil (kaṭicūttiram) araiñāṇ mūṉṟu nūlkaḷ uḷḷatāy irukka vēṇṭum.
kirutrumāciyam eṉṉum aṇi kaṭicantikku mēṟpuṟattil mēṭarattiṉ kīḻ aṇivikkappaṭavēṇṭum. muṉpuṟam mattiyil ciṅkam mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ mukam pōṉṟa paṭṭam iṭaiyil cuṟṟiya vāṟu amaikkappaṭum mattiyil mukam pōṉṟa pakutitāṉ kirutru māciyam. itiliruntu toṭaiyil muttucaraṅkaḷ toṅkaviṭa vēṇṭum.
pātaṅkaḷ iraṇṭilum kaṇukkālukkuk kīḻē cālakam (paṭṭaicaram) aṇivikkavēṇṭum. itu pātacaram eṉṟum kūṟap paṭum cila iṭaṅkaḷil paṭṭaik kolucu eṉṟu kūṟuvar. itil cālakaṅkaḷai oru cūttirattil kōttuk kaṭṭa vēṇṭum. muttukkaḷ pōl taṉit taṉiyākap paṇṇappeṟṟu cālakacūttirattil kōkkappaṭṭu valaipōl iṇaintu niṟkum ciṟu paṭṭaikaḷ kūṭiyatu pātacaram.
cakaḷātikāram : cakaḷātikāram eṉṉum ciṟpa nūl āṇ kaḷiṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷ eṉṟa talaippil kūṟuvatāvatu :- cōma kanta cukācaṉa mūrttiyiṉ iṭatu kaiyil kaṭakam aṇivikkappaṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. iṭatu kātil caṅkapattiram (caṅku vaḷaiyum) valatu kātil makarak kuṇṭalam allatu cimmak kuṇṭalam aṇiya vēṇṭum. iṭuppil mukam pōṉṟa vēlaip pāṭuḷḷa araiñāṇum kaiyil mūṉṟu kaṅkaṇaṅkaḷum koṭip pū vēlai ceyta kēyūramum tōḷ vaḷaiyum aṅkatam (vāku valayam) utara pantaṉam (vayiṟṟup pakkam aṇiyum aṇikalaṉum) aṇintirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ūrutāmam eṉṉum aṇikalaṉum mālai pōṉṟa pātacaramum tuṭai varai yil toṅkum. kaṉṉa pūram eṉṉum kātaṇiyum āram, āraciri vatcam (mālaiyil toṅkum māṇikkamaṇi) maṇi aṭṭikai nitalāñcalamum aṇintirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu.
āṭa vallāṉukkuk kaḻuttai oṭṭi irukkum upakkirīvam eṉṉum aṭṭikai aṇintirukka vēṇṭum.
kaṅkātara mūrttikku uḷḷaṅ kaiyil aṭippākattil kaṅkaṇam (vīra caṅkiliyum) neṟṟiyil paṭṭamum aṇivikkappaṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu.2
māṉacāram : māṉacāram eṉṉum ciṟpa nūlil, talaiyil kirīṭamakuṭam, maṇi makuṭam, karaṇṭa makuṭam, cirattāra makuṭam mutaliyavaikaḷum cirac cūḷam eṉṉum uccan talaiyil aṇiyum akaḷa cūṭamum aṇiya vēṇṭum eṉak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
āparaṇaṅkaḷ pattira kalpam, cittira kalpam, irattiṉa kalpam, miccira kalpam eṉa nāṉku vakaiyākap pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tēvarkaḷukku ellāk kalpamum uriyaṉa. maṉṉarkaḷukku cittira kalpam, irattiṉa kalpam, miccira kalpam ākiya mūṉṟum uriyaṉa. atirācaṉ, narēntiraṉ ākiya aracarkaḷukku irattiṉa kalpam, miccira kalpam ākiya iraṇṭum ēṉaiya maṉṉarkaḷukku miccira kalpamum uriyaṉa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
pattira kalpam : ilaikaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya koṭikaḷaip pōl alaṅkarittuc ceyyappaṭṭa aṇikalaṉ.
cittira kalpam : vicittiramāṉa ellā maṇikaḷālum tāmarai malarkaḷālum alaṅkarikkap peṟṟa aṇikalaṉākum.
irattiṉa kalpam : malarkaḷālum maṇikaḷālum eḻil peṟa aḻaku ceyyap peṟṟa aṇikalaṉ.
miccira kalpam : ilaikaḷālum taḷirkaḷālum maṇikaḷā lum aḻaku ceyyap peṟṟu oḷirum aṇikalaṉ.
mālaikaḷ, upakkirīvam, kēyūra kaṭakaṅkaḷ, cupūrimam, miccira kalpam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum.
vāku valayam (tōḷ valayam), tāmam (mālai) eṉṟa aṇikaḷum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pirakōṣṭattil (maṇikkaṭṭukkum muḻaṅ kaikkum naṭuvil) valayam eṉṟa aṇiyum maṇi pantattil (maṇik kaṭṭil) kalāpam eṉṟa aṇiyum, utarapantam, taṉa cūttirakam (itu utara pantattiṉ mēliruntu kuṟukku neṭukkāka mēlē ceṉṟu koṅkaikaḷai maṟaippatu), kēyūra kaṭakattōṭu iṇainta pāku mālā valampam (kanta mālā valampam) eṉṟa tōḷiṉiṉṟu toṅkum aṇiyum, pūrimamum (taḻai, kuḻai pōṉṟa aṇiyum) kātil makarak kuṇṭalam (mīṉ vaṭivamāṉa kātaṇiyum), tirumālukku vaṉa mālaiyum (kaḻuttiṉiṉṟu nuṉikkāl varai toṅkum aṇikalaṉum) iṭuppil paṭṭikai, kaḻuttil mālaikaḷ, maṇikaḷ patitta kaṅkaṇaṅkaḷ, talaiyaṇikaḷ, paṭṭikaiyil uḷḷa kiṇkiṇi vaḷaiyaṅkaḷ, cuvaraṇa tāṭakam eṉṟa kātaṇikaḷ, talaiyiṉ ucciyil uḷḷa cūṭāmaṇi, neṟṟiyil aṇiyum pāla paṭṭam, irupattēḻu maṇikaḷ koṇṭa upakkirīvamāṉa naṭcattira mālai, pāti aḷavu toṅkum artta āram, upakaṇṭam eṉum kaḻuttai yoṭṭiya mālai, kēyūram (tōḷvaḷai), maṇi pantaṉam (maṇikaṭṭiṉ aṇi) araiñāṇ, nīvi (iṭaiyil aṇiyum calaṉam eṉṟa acaintāṭum mukappuṭaṉ kūṭiya iṭaip paṭṭikaiyum) mōtiraṅkaḷ, pātacaraṅkaḷ mutaliya aṇikalaṉkaḷum iṭam peṟa vēṇṭum.3
ciricāracuvatīya cittirakarmacāttiram : innūlil cōma kanta cukācaṉa mūrttiyiṉ ilakkaṇam kūṟum poḻutu avaratu iṭatu kātil karṇi (tāmarai naṭuvil irukkum kāy pōṉṟa vaṭivamuḷḷa tōṭupōṉṟa nakai) allatu caṅkapattiram (caṅkiṉā lāṉa kātaṇi) valatu kātil makarak kuṇṭalam (cuṟāmīṉ vaṭivamuḷḷa kuṇṭalam) kēcari kuṇṭalam (ciṅkak kuṇṭalam) viyāḻak kuṇṭalam (pāmpu pōṉṟa kuṇṭalam) kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
iṭuppil araiñāṇ allatu kaṭṭuvār irukka vēṇṭum eṉṟum araiñāṇ mukappil ciṅkam allatu yāḷi pōṉṟu amaikkappaṭavēṇṭum eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
iḵtaṉṟi kēyūram (tōḷvaḷai), aṅkatam, vāku valayam (tōḷaṉi), kaṭakattirayam (mūṉṟu vaḷaiyal), muntirikai (mōtiram), utarapantam (iṭuppil kaṭṭum vār) kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
kēyūram tōḷ mūlattil aṇiyum aṇikalaṉ; aṅkatam tōḷ mattiyil pūṇum aṇikalaṉ. kaṭakattirayam, tōḷvaḷai, pirakōṣṭavaḷaiyam, maṇipanta kaṭakam ākiyavaikaḷākum. utara pantam vayiṟṟil iḻuttukkaṭṭum vār. pattiram ilaikaḷiṉ vaṭiva muḷḷa aṇi; itu vāril toṅkaviṭappaṭum. ūrutāmam - tuṭai yil toṅkaviṭappaṭum kayiṟu, allatu kuñcam; pātacālam - pātacaram ākum. kiraivēyām - kaḻuttiliruntu mārpuvarai toṅkum kācumālai, kārai, aṭṭikai pōṉṟa uḷkaḻuttaṇikaḷ. irattiṉa panta mupakīrivam - maṇikaḷ iḻaitta uḷkaḻuttaṇi mutaliyavai iṭam peṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.4
cakaḷātikāram : cakaḷātikāram eṉṉum nūlil peṇkaḷiṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷaip paṟṟik kūṟiyiruppatāvatu :
tēviyiṉ kātil makarakkuṇṭalamum tōḷil caṉṉa vīramum (kuṟukkumālaiyum) kaḻuttil palavitamāṉa mālaikaḷum, puyattil tōḷvaḷaiyum tikaḻavēṇṭum. tēvikkup pāmpu mukattōṭu kūṭiya araiñāṇum iṭampeṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. 5
ciri cāracuvatīya cittirakarma cāttiram : ciri cāracu vatīya cittira karma cāttiram eṉṉum nūl cōmakanta mūrtta vaṭivil irukkum umātēvikkuk kal iḻaitta ōlai allatu poṉ ōlaiyum callari (olikkum vaḷai), atavalayam (kaivaḷai), pūrimam (tōḷiṟku mēlē aṇiyum aṇi), aṅkuliyakam (mōtiram) mutaliya aṇikaḷ aṇiyap peṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu.
koṟṟavaikku (makicācuramarttaṉikku) kaṉṉatāmā valampaṉam - kātil kayiṟu pōlat toṅkum māṭṭi, cimikki, ṭōlak pōṉṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷ aṇivikkappaṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.6
kuṟippu
1.	cilparattiṉam - kat. 11 pak. 245-271.	
cirikāciyapacilpacāttiram 56. pak. 212-215.

ā)  Indian Images Part I B. C. Battacharya M.A., F.R.S.S.G.S.

12. ilakkiyac cāṉṟukaḷ
tirāviṭarkaḷ iṉṟaikku aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē aṇikalaṉkaḷ palavaṟṟaic ceytu aṇiyat toṭaṅkiviṭṭār kaḷ. arappā nākarikattil arumpiya poṉ aṇikaḷum, maṇikaḷ patitta kal aṇikaḷum namakku nalla eṭuttukkāṭṭāyt tikaḻ kiṉṟaṉa.
tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ mūtta - mutuperum nākarikattaik kaṇṭa mutuperuṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ nākarikattai eṭuttuk kāṭṭa āticca nallūril akaḻntu eṭutta aṇikaḷum, aṅkuk kaṇṭa pommai kaḷil kāṇappaṭum aṇikalaṉkaḷum ariya eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷāy aṟiñarkaḷāl matikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
āticcanallūr  nākarikattai viḷakkik kūṟum eṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷ pōl, namatu ilakkiyaṅkaḷil aṇikalaṉkaḷukkuriya eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ ērāḷamāka uḷḷaṉa.
1. tolkāppiyam
tolkāppiyam iṉṟu kāṇakkiṭakkum tamiḻ nūṟkaḷil paḻamaiyāṉatu. atil tamiḻarkaḷ cumār mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar aṇintirunta aṇilaṉkaḷaip paṟṟic cila kuṟippukaḷ, uḷḷaṉa. atikamāṉa kuṟippukaḷ illāviṭiṉum mukkiyamāṉa oṉṟiraṇṭu kuṟippukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. atāvatu,
kātoṉṟu kaḷaital (tol. poruḷ 1205) eṉṟu oru cūttirattil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṟkuk kātil aṇinta kuḻaiyai nīkkutal eṉpatē poruḷākum. eṉavē tolkāppiyar kālattiṟku muṉpē tamiḻakattil kuḻai arumpiviṭṭatu. civaperu māṉ kuḻai aṇipavarātaliṉ avarukku kuḻaikkātar eṉṟa peyar eḻuntatu eṉṟu nām ellōrum naṉkaṟivōm. maṟṟōr cūttirattil
aṇintavai tiruttal - (tol - poruḷ - 1206) eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṟkup poruḷ kaṇṭa uraiyāciriyar kaḷ aṇintirukkum aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ alaṅkōlattaic carip paṭuttik koḷḷutal eṉṟē poruḷ kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. iḵtaṉṟi iṉṉum, iḻaiyaṇintu, iḻaiyē - (tol. poruḷ. 1096, 1038) eṉṟu maṟṟīriṭattil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṟku aḻakiya nalla vēlaippāṭukaḷuṭaṉ ceyyappaṭṭa aṇikalaṉ eṉṟu urai kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷatu.
ilakkaṇa nūlātaliṉ tolkāppiyattil mika viḷakkamākap pala aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kāṇappaṭavillai.
2. cilappatikāram
cilappatikāram aimperuṅ kāppiyaṅkaḷil oṉṟu. innūl cēranāṭṭu iḷavaracar oruvarāl iyaṟṟap peṟṟatu. inta iḷavaracar iḷaṅkō aṭikaḷ eṉṉum tuṟavi; eṉṟālum aracar, vaṇikar mutaliyavarkaḷiṉ vāḻkkaic ciṟappai naṉkaṟintavar. itu oru nāṭakak kāppiyam eṉṟu matikkappaṭukiṟatu. itu camaṇac cārpuṭaiya nūlākum.
aṇikalaṉkaḷ
cilampu eṉpatu peṇkaḷ kālil aṇiyum oru aṇikala ṉākum. tamiḻ aṉṉaikku āciriyar camarppikkum oru ciṟanta kāl aṇikalaṉ pōṉṟu ipperum kāppiyattai  eḻutiyuḷḷār. tamiḻ nūlil atikamāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷai eṭuttukkāṭṭuvatu in nūl oṉṟēyākum. mēlum āciriyar aracakumārarāy iruntamai yāl araca kuṭumpattār aṇiyum aṇikalaṉkaḷaiyellām naṉkaṟintirukkiṟār eṉṟu naṉkaṟikiṟōm. kaṇṇakiyiṉ kāṟcilam piṉuḷ vilaiyuyarnta māṇikkak kaṟkaḷum, pāṇṭiyaṉ maṉaivi yiṉ kāṟcilampiṉuḷ vilai kuṟainta muttukkaḷum pōṭappaṭ ṭiruntatē nalla eṭuttuk kāṭṭākum.
kālam
ipperuṅkāppiyam tiṭṭavaṭṭamāka ki.pi. 2ām nūṟṟāṇ ṭil arumpiyatu eṉṟu aṟiñar vi.ār. ār. tīṭcitar kūṟukiṉṟār.
nūl
innūlil cōḻanāṭu, pāṇṭiyanāṭu, cēra nāṭu eṉṟa mūṉṟu tamiḻnāṭukaḷum piṇaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cōḻanāṭṭiṉ vaṇika vaḷarcciyum vaṇikarkaḷiṉ vāḻkkai muṟaiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil niyāyam vaḻaṅkum muṟaiyum, pāṇṭiyarkaḷ nīti pēṇum tiṟaṉum naṉku eṭuttukkāṭṭappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. cēra maṉṉaṉ tuṟaviyāy iruntum annāṭṭil pattiṉit teyvam vaittu vaḻipaṭa vēṇṭiya avaciyamum naṉku eṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭukiṟatu. ēṉ? ciṅkaḷa nāṭṭil kūṭa pattiṉit teyva vaḻipāṭu eḻum avaciyam eṭuttukkāṭṭappaṭukiṟatu. mūṉṟu tamiḻnāṭukaḷilum, caivam, camaṇam, pauttam eṉṟa mūṉṟu camayaṅ kaḷum iruntanilai eṭuttukkāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
cōḻa nāṭṭiṉ celvac cerukkum ataṉāl eḻunta parattaiyar vāḻvu, āṭaṟkalai, icaikkalai, aṇikalakkalai mutaliyavai ellām ciṟappākac cittarikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
innūl camaṇa kāppiyam eṉṟālum itil vaṇikar vāḻvu, parattaiyar vāḻvu nāṭṭiyam, icai, aṇikalaṉ mutaliyavai kaḷaip pōṟṟi varuvatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. makaḷir uṭalai aḻakupaṭuttum kalai, nāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ naṉi ciṟanta vāḻkkainilai ellām tiṟampaṭa eṭuttukkāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
caṅkam maruviya nūṟkaḷil perum nūlō, paḻam perum kāppiyamō namakkuk kiṭaikkavillai. eṉavē caṅkak kālattiṟku muntiya aṇikalaṉkaḷaip paṟṟi atikamāka aṟiya muṭiyavillai. eṉṟālum kaṭaic caṅkak kālattil pāṇṭiyanāṭu poṉṉum maṇiyum peṟṟuc ciṟantu viḷaṅkiyatākat terikiṟatu. irāmāyaṇak kālattilē - pāṇṭiyarkaḷ tam talainakarākiya kapāṭapuratti liruntu āṭci celutti vantaṉar. talainakariṉ kōṭṭaivāyil poṉṉum maṇiyum muttum patikkap peṟṟiruntatu eṉṟu vāṉmīki vaṭamoḻi irāmāyaṇattil kuṟippiṭuvatu namatu caṅkak kāla vāḻviṟku oru eṭuttukkāṭṭākum.
cilappatikāramum aṇikalaṉkaḷum
cilappatikārattil nam aṇikalaṉkaḷaip paṟṟi mikac ciṟappāka eṭuttukkāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu.
matimuka maṅkai nallāḷ māmalarkkaṇṇi mātavi, intira viḻāviṉ iṟutiyil taṉ illattai eytiṉāḷ. uṭaṉē taṉ māntaḷir mēṉiyai merukiṭṭa poṟpāvai eṉṟu eṇṇumpaṭi oppaṉai ceytu koṇṭu ūṭal koṇṭa kōvalaṉ muṉ vantāḷ. avaḷ aḻakiṉaik kaṇṭu avaṉ makiḻntāṉ. avaḷ evvāṟu taṉṉai aṇi ceyvittu eḻilōviyamāka - aṟputamāṉa poṟpāvaiyākak kāṭci aḷittāḷ eṉpatai iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ ciṟappāka eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār. atu aṭiyil varumāṟu :
mātavi maṇantarum neyyai talaimayirukku ūṭṭi pattut tuvariṉālum aintu viraiyiṉālum muppattiraṇṭu vakai ōmālikaiyiṉālum ūṟik kāyccappeṟṟa naṉṉīrilē kārmēkam pōṉṟu kaṟuttuk kaviṉpeṟa nīṇṭu vaḷarntirukkum kūntalaik kaḻuviṉāḷ. naṟumaṇam nalkum pukaiyūṭṭi īram ularttiṉāḷ. kūntalai aḻaku peṟa vārip piṉṉik kaṭṭiṉāḷ. vācaṉaik kuḻampai meykku ūṭṭiṉāḷ; koṅkaiyil ceṅkaiyiṉāl toyyil eḻutiṉāḷ. iṭaiyil poṟcarikai puṉaiyappaṭṭa paṭṭāṭaiyaip puṉaintāḷ.
cempañcuk kuḻampu ūṭṭiya civanta pātaṅkaḷiṉ melliya viralkaḷukku makaravāy mōtiram, pīli, kāl viral mōtiram mutaliya kālviral aṇikalaṉkaḷaip pūṭṭiṉāḷ. piṉ taṉ kālkaḷil pātacālam, pāṭakam, cilampu (kuṭaiccūl) cataṅkai, kiṇkiṇi, kāṟacari ākiyavaikaḷaip pūṭṭiṉāḷ. melliya iṭai yil aṇinta nīlavaṇṇappaṭṭāṭai mītu muppattu iraṇṭu parumuttuk kōvaikaḷālāṉa viricikai eṉṉum nucuppaṇiyai māṭṭiṉāḷ. māṇikkavaḷaiyuṭaṉ nīṅkāmal poṟṟoṭarāl piṇaitta muttuvaḷaiyait tōḷil pūṇṭu mukappil kaṭṭiya māṇikkattōṭu pattikaḷil vairaṅkaḷ aḻuttappeṟṟa ōviya vēlaippāṭukaḷ niṟainta cūṭakam, cempoṉvaḷai navarattiṉa vaḷai caṅkuvaḷai, pavaḷavaḷai eṉṉum muṉ kaivaḷaikaḷai poruttamuṟap pūṭṭiṉāḷ. vāḷaip pakuvāy vaṇakkuṟu mōtiram (neḷivu), ceṅkēḻ kiḷarmaṇi mōtiram (māṇikka maṇimōtiram) vairamarakat tāḷceṟi mōtiram (vairam cūḻnta marakatam patikka mōtiram ākiya kaiviral aṇikaḷai kāntaḷ malar pōl nīṇṭu civanta aḻakiya viralkaḷ maṟaiyumpaṭi pūṇṭāḷ. vīraccaṅkili, nērñcaṅkili, ñāṇ, carappaḷi muttumālai, piṉṟāli mutaliya kaḻuttaṇikaḷaik kaviṉuṟak kaḻuttil aṇintu koṇ ṭāḷ. intira nīlattiṭaiyē eḻiluṟap patitta vairattālāṉa nīlak kutampai, cantirapāṇi mutaliya kātaṇikaḷai, kātil aṇintāḷ. cītēvi valampuri, toyyakam, pullakam, cūḷāmaṇi, poṉṉari mālai kōtai mutaliya talaiyaṇikaḷai aḻakiya kūntalil poruttiṉāḷ. toṭaiyil kuṟaṅkucceṟi eṉum aṇikalaṉaip pūṭṭiṉāḷ. ivvāṟu mātavi tāṉ kaṟṟa eḻiṟkalaiyiṉ ilakkaṇam eḷḷaḷavum vaḻuvātu aṇikalaṉ aṉaittaiyum aṇintu āṭaiyaip puṉaintu aḻaku ceytu koṇṭāḷ. ūṭal koṇṭa kōvalaṉ uḷḷattil uvakai ūṭṭi iṉpa mūṭṭip paḷḷiyaṟai yil pāṅkuṭaṉ iruntāḷ.2
iḵtaṉṟi maṟṟōr iṭattum mātavi eṉum pūṅkoṭi mullaiyum mallikaiyum oḻinta tāḷik kuvaḷai mutaliya pala pūkkaḷ viritta taṉ paḷḷiyaṟaiyil aḻakiya alkul taṉṉiṭattil tuyil ceyyappaṭṭu ataṉmītu pavaḷak kōvaikaḷāl aṇi ceyyum mēkalaiyai aṇintāḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.3
vēṉiṟ kātaiyil kōvalaṉ kaṭaṟkaraiyiṉiṉṟu kōpat tōṭu pōyviṭṭatāl mātavi taṉ vīṭu pōntu ēḻāvatu māṭi yiṉ nilāmuṟṟattil cantaṉamum iḷavēṉiṟ kālattiṟkup poruttamāṉa veṇmuttārattaiyum pūṇṭāḷ eṉṟu kūṟap paṭukiṟatu.4
cilappatikārattiṉ vaḻakkurai kātaiyil kaṇṇakiyiṉ cilampum pāṇṭimātēviyiṉ cilampum eṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. kaṇṇakiyiṉ cilampiṉuḷ māṇikka maṇikaḷum (māṇikkapparalkaḷum) kōpperuntēviyiṉ kālcilampiṉuḷ muttukkaḷum pōṭappaṭṭiruntatākat terikiṟatu. cilampiṉuḷ muttup paralkaḷō allatu māṇikkap paralkaḷō pōṭuvatu naṭakkumpoḻutu avai kaṇīr ! kaṇīr! eṉṟa iṉṉoliyaik kiḷampuvataṟkēyām. māṇikka maṇi ōrvitamāṉa iṉṉoliyai yum muttukkaḷ vēṟuvitamāṉa iṉṉoliyaiyum kiḷappum.5 kaḻuttil tāliyum6 maṅkala aṇiyum (tāli) iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu.7
3. maṇimēkalai
itu aimperuṅkāppiyaṅkaḷil cilappatikārattiṟku aṭutta paṭiyāka matikkappaṭum ariya nūl. maturaik kūlavaṇikaṉ cāttaṉār eḻutiyatu. pautta camayac cārpuṭaiyatu. itu ki.pi. 2ām nuṟ ṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil eḻutappeṟṟatu eṉpar. itil aṇi kalaṉkaḷ cilappatikāram pōl atikam kāṇappaṭāviṭiṉum orucila mukkiya aṇikalaṉkaḷ kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
innūlil cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭamuraitta kātaiyil kaiyil kaṭakam8 kāppu mutaliyavaikaḷum, ciṟaikkōṭṭam aṟak kōṭṭa mākkiya kātaiyil kaiyil koṭiyum aṇintatu kuṟippiṭap paṭṭuḷḷatu.9 kaccinakar pukkakātaiyil urukkut taṭṭārum, poṟ paṇikaḷ ceyvōrum piṟarum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉar.10 malar vaṉam pukunta kātaiyil caṅkiṉāṟ ceyta kātaṇikaḷ kāṭṭappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉṉum poṟṟōṭum,11 viḻāvaṟai kātaiyil muttu mālaiyum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.12 aṉṟi aimpaṭait tāliyum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
cilappatikārattil mātavi aṇinta aṇikalaṉkaḷ aṉaittum kāṭṭappaṭṭiruppatu pōl maṇimēkalaiyil kataippōkku avaḷ āṭamparamāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷai aṇintāḷ eṉpataik kāṭṭuvataṟku ēṟṟatāka illai. mēlum āciriyar kūlavaṇikaṉ cāttaṉārum tamatu kāppiyam tamiḻ aṉṉaikku oru aṇikalaṉāka viḷaṅka vēṇṭum eṉṟa ārvattil itaṉai mēkalai eṉṉum peyaril iyaṟṟiyatu tamiḻ makkaḷukkuk kalaiyiṉ mītuḷḷa ārvattiṟku nalla eṭuttukkāṭṭāka irukkiṟatu.
4. cīvakacintāmaṇi
cīvakacintāmaṇi aimperuṅ kāppiyaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. kāppiya nūṟkaḷil mutalil cilappatikāramum, iraṇṭāvatu maṇimēkalai yum, mūṉṟāvatu cīvakacintāmaṇiyum piṟantaṉa. itu nantāp perumai vāynta cintāmaṇi eṉṟu pulavarkaḷāl pōṟṟappeṟum nūl. itaṉai iyaṟṟiyavar tiruttakka tēvar. ivar araca marapiṉar. ki.pi. 1200ām āṇṭiṟku muṉ vāḻntavarāka matikkappaṭukiṟār. paṇṭaiya pulavarkaḷ tamiḻ aṉṉaikku āṟu uyarnta aṇikaḷai camarppittuḷḷaṉar. avaikaḷil itu mūṉṟāvatāka vaittu eṇṇappeṟum. āṟu aṇikalaṉkaḷ muṟaiyē cilampu, mēkalai, cintāmaṇi, vaḷai, kuṇṭalam, ceṅkōl eṉum āṟākum. ivvāṟu aṇikaḷaiyum pulavarkaḷ āṟu nūlāka ākkit tamiḻ aṉṉaiyiṭam camarppittuḷḷaṉar. ataṉai oru piṟkālap pulavar.
	kātoḷirum kuṇṭalamum kaikkuvaḷai
		yāpatiyum karuṇai mārpil
	 mītoḷir cintāmaṇiyum melliṭaiyil
		mēkalaiyum cilampār iṉpap
	 pōtoḷir pūntāḷiṇaiyum poṉmuṭicū
		ṭāmaṇiyum poliyaccūṭṭi
	 nītiyoḷir ceṅkōlāyt tirukkuṟaḷait
		tāṅkutamiḻ nīṭu vāḻka.
eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatu aimperuṅkāppiyattiṉ perumaiyai eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṟatu. tamiḻaṉṉaikku aṇikalaṉkaḷ mītuḷḷa ārvam pulaṉākiṟatu. tamiḻp pulavarkaḷ aṉṉaiyai makiḻvikka aṇi ceytu pūṭṭi vaṇaṅkum marapu itaṉāl veḷiyākiṟatu.
cintāmaṇiyil caccantaṉ maṉaivi vicaiyai kaṇavaṉ kāṭēṟiyatai aṟintu poṉṉaṇikaḷaik kaḻaṟṟiṉāḷ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṟku uraikaṇṭa uraiyāciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar poṉṉarimālaiyai13yaiyum neṟṟi mālaiyai yum talaikkōlamākiya muttum pulampam mutaliyavaikaḷaik kuḻali ṉiṉṟu akaṟṟiṉāḷ eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār. tōḷiṉ muttait tuṟantu vaḷaiyai uṭaittu viralāḻiyai vilakkiṉāḷ eṉṟu āciriyar maṟṟoru pāṭṭil kūṟiyuḷḷār. innūlil kēmacariyārilam pakattil āciriyar kēmacari kuṇṭalam acaintu oḷivīca iṭaiyil tukiliṉuḷḷē mēkalai oḷirntatu eṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṟār.14 kantaruvattaiyār ilampakattil kantaruvattaikku avaḷ tantai ariya maṇikaḷ patitta aḻakiya aṇikalaṉkaḷ vaippataṟku vaira maṇikaḷ iḻaittuc ceyta vaṇṇap peṭṭikaḷ ainūṟum poṉṉummaṇiyum ārnta yavaṉanāṭṭil ceyta peṭṭikaḷ iraṇṭāyiramum aḷittāṉ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.15 ilakkaṇai yārilampakattil kiṇkiṇi uṭai (mēkalai) poṟcilampu ākiya aṇikalaṉkaḷum16 maṟṟōriṭattil poṟṟōṭu kaṭippu (a) kuḻai, (ā) cuṟavukkuḻai mutaliya aṇikaḷum kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. mēlum palaviṭaṅkaḷil nutal paṭṭam17 makaramīṉ muttai umiḻvatu pōṉṟu ceyyap peṟṟa tōḷaṇi18 maṅkaiyar koṅkai mītu maṟṟoru aṇiyum ilaivaṭivākiya pūṇ eṉṉum aṇiyum muṉ kaiyil oru aṇiyum maṇikkaṭakam ilaik kaṭakam pōṉṟa aṇi kalaṉkaḷum19 talaiyaṇiyākiya muttuttāmamum20, nutal aṇi yākiya ilampakamum21, kātaṇikaḷākiya makara kuṇṭalam, tōṭu, kuḻai, poṟṟōṭu, maruppiṉālāyatōṭu, kuṇṭalam, kaṭippu mutaliyavaikaḷaip peṇkaḷum āṇkaḷum aṇintu vantaṉar.22 tōḷil calākai eṉṉum tōḷ aṇiyum23 ēṉāti pōṉṟa mōtiraṅkaḷum24 poṟṟōrai eṉṉum iṭaiyaṇiyum, kuṟaṅkuc ceṟi eṉṉum toṭaiyaṇiyum, kālil aṇiyum kaḻalkaḷ ākiyavaikaḷum eṭuttukkāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
cintāmaṇi, cilappatikāra kālattiṟku 800 āṇṭukaḷukkup piṟpaṭṭa nūl. itil cilappatikāra aṇikaḷaiyaṉṟi piṟa aṇikaḷ kāṇappaṭavillai. ataṉāl ikkālattil putiya aṇikaḷ tōṉṟavillai eṉṟu terikiṟatu. cintāmaṇi eḻum kālattil tamiḻarkaḷ yavaṉarkaḷōṭu toṭarpu koṇṭiruntaṉar. avar kaḷiṭamiruntu aṇikaḷ vaikkum maṇikaḷ patitta peṭṭikaḷ pala vāṅkiṉar eṉṟu terikiṟatu. peṭṭikaḷiṉ matippum, eṇṇikkai yum pārkkumpoḻutu akkālattil atikamāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷ iruntirukkalām eṉṟu terikiṟatu.
innūlil toyyakam (pūrappāḷai), ilampakam, paṭṭam, makarakkuṇṭalam, tōṭu (poṟṟōṭu), maruppiṉāl ceytatōṭu, kuḻai, cuṟavukkuḻai, vaḷainta kuḻai, cempoṉṉōlai, cen niṟakkuḻai, kaṭippu, calākai, kuṟaṅkuc ceṟi, araiñāṇ, ēṉāti mōtiram, caṭakam, kaṭippu, kaḻal, kāñci, cilampu, cūṭṭu, cūṭakam, cūḷāmaṇi, talaippāḷai, toṭi, tōḷvaḷai, neṟṟippaṭṭam, pariyakam, pavaḷamālai, piṟai, pūṇ, poṟkaṇṇi, poṟṟōrai, poṉñāṇ, poṉṉari mālai, poṉṉirait tāli, poṉ ṉōṭai, makarakkuṇṭalam, mañcikai, maṇi mōtiram, maṇivaṭam, muttumālai, mēkalai, mōtiram, vaḷai pōṉṟa pala aṇi kalaṉkaḷ kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
cintāmaṇi cilappatikārattiṟkup pintiya kālattil piṟanta ilakkiyam. itil piṟkāla aṇikalaṅkaḷ iṭam peṟṟirukkalām eṉṟu eṇṇa iṭamuṇṭu. maṇimēkalaiyaiviṭa atikamāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷ itil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
5. tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭai
tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭai kaṭaiccaṅka nūṟkaḷil oṉṟu. pattup pāṭṭil mutalāvatāka vaittup pōṟṟappaṭuvatu. itu murukak kaṭavuḷ mītu pāṭappeṟṟa oru ariya nūl. itaṉaip pāṭiyavar caṅkak kālap perumpulavar nakkīrar. innūl ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟ ṟāṇṭil allatu ataṟkuc ciṟitu muṉpiṉ pāṭappeṟṟirukka vēṇṭum eṉ eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.
innūlil makkaḷiṉ āṭai, aṇikalaṉ ākiyavaikaḷum murukaṉ āṭum vēlaṉ allatu paṭimattāṉ eṉṟa kuṟiñcit teyvattiṉ tōṟṟamum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kuṟiñci nilak kuṉṟak kaṭavuḷai, kuṟamakaḷir eṭutta viḻāk koṇṭāṭum muṟaiyum murukaṉai ētti vaḻipaṭum neṟiyum anneṟiyāl vaḻipāṭṭāl peṟum pēṟum vivaramāka viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
kuṟamakaḷir talaimayiraik kōti eṇṇey taṭavi ataṉ mītu veṭcippūkkaḷai, viṭupūvākat tūvi ataṟku naṭuvē kuvaḷaip pūviṉ veḷḷitaḻkaḷaik kiḷḷiviṭṭu appāl talaiyiṉmītu aṇikalaṉkaḷaic cūṭuvar. talaiyil makaravaṭivāṉa neṟṟic cuṭṭiyum, cītēvi, valampuric caṅku pōṉṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷaiyum aṇintu muṟṟa muṭinta koṇṭaiyil periya ceṇmukap pūvaic ceruki mēlē marutam pūvaiyiṭṭuk kaḻuttilē kaḻumalap pūvai mālaiyākak kaṭṭi aṇintu kātukaḷil piṇṭit taḷirkaḷai māṭṭi mārpil aḻakiya aṇikalaṉkaḷai aṇintu cantaṉam pūci ataṉ īram ularumuṉ vēṅkaip pūviṉ tātiṉaip parappi viḷā marattiṉ taḷirai oruvar mītu oruvar teṟittuk koḷvar eṉa in nūlil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.29
iḵtaṉṟi kāñci30 mēkalai cataṅkai (kiṇkiṇi)31 vīra kaṇṭai32 muṭi (makuṭam)33, poṉmakarak kuṇṭalam34, poṉṉari mālai35, neṟṟippaṭṭam, vīramaṇi, vīrakaṇṭai, toṭi (vāku valayam)36 kaḻal, kuḻai, poṉ aṇi, māṟṟuyarnta maṇikaḷ pōṉṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷum kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
6. porunar āṟṟuppaṭai
porunarāṟṟuppaṭai pattuppāṭṭil iraṇṭāvatu vaittu eṇṇappaṭum ariya nūlākum. itu kaṭaic caṅkappāṭṭu kaḷil oṉṟu. cōḻap pēraracaṉ karikāṟcōḻaṉai muṭattāmak kaṇṇiyār eṉpavar pukaḻntu pāṭiya nūl. itu 248 aṭikaḷai yuṭaiyatu.
innūlil kātaṇiyākiya kuḻai37 palvēṟu muttu vaṭaṅkaḷ, maṇi mālaikaḷ, iṭaiyil ilaṅkum nucup paṇikaḷ, kaḻuttil ilaṅkum poṉṉarimālaikaḷ38 mutaliyavai kuṟippiṭappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa.
ataṉṟiyum makkaḷ talaiyilum kātilum kaḻuttilum kaiyilum iṭuppilum kālkaḷilum aṇiyum poṉ aṇikaḷum pavaḷa mālaikaḷum muttu vaṭaṅkaḷum piṟa maṇikaḷ patittu oḷiviṭum poṉmaṇi aṇikaḷum ilaikaḷ pōṉṟum kulaikaḷ pōṉṟum kuḻaikaḷ pōṉṟum malarkaḷ pōṉṟum mīṉkaḷ pōṉṟum pāmpukaḷ pōṉṟum naṇṭukaḷ pōṉṟum ceyyap peṟṟa aḻakiya aṇikalaṉkaḷum vaḷaikaḷum eṭuttukkāṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
7. perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭai
innūl uruttiraṅ kaṇṇaṉār eṉṉum pulavar, toṇṭai māṉ iḷantiraiyaṉaip pukaḻntu pāṭiyatu. itu pattuppāṭṭil 4-vatāka iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu. 500 aṭikaḷaiyuṭaiyatu.
innūlil, uṇavu niṟainta nalla vīṭukaḷilē vaḷainta poṉ aṇikaḷaip pūṇṭa makaḷir vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ muttuvaṭam pūṇṭa tam iṭaiyilē mika nuṇmaiyāṉa tukilai aṇintirun taṉar. attukil koṉṟai malar malarnta melliya kompilē paṉittuḷi tavaḻvatu pōl acaikiṉṟatu. makaḷir malaiyilē kaḷi koṇṭu kalāpam virittāṭum poṉmayilaip pōl kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷuṭaiya kālkaḷilē poṟ cilampukaḷ olik kiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷ uyarnta māḷikaiyilē pantāṭi iḷaittaṉar. muttup pōṉṟa veṇmaṇal parappiya iṭattilē kaiyil puṉainta ciṟiya vaḷaikaḷ olikkumpaṭi kaḻaṅkukaḷai ēnti āṭukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟu paṭṭiṉattiṉ perumai eṭuttukkāṭṭap paṭukiṟatu.39 kātu kaḷil kuḻaikaḷ ūcalāṭiyatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.40
eṉavē aṉṟu tamiḻarkaḷ vāḻnta nakar pala aṭukkukaḷai yuṭaiya māṭi vīṭu niṟainta paṭṭiṉam. poṉ kaḻaṅkukaḷ vaittu āṭum aḷavil celvap perukkuṭaiyatu eṉṟellām terikiṟatu.
8. maturaikkāñci
maturaikkāñci kaṭaic caṅka nūṟkaḷil oṉṟu. pattuppāṭṭu eṉṉum nūṟṟokutiyil āṟāvatāka amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. pattuppāṭṭil ituvē kūṭutalāṉa aṭikaḷai (782) uṭaiyatu. itaṉ āciriyar māṅkuṭi marutaṉār. itaṉ pāṭṭuṭait talaivaṉ talaiyālaṅkāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉ āvaṉ.
nūliṉ aṭakkam
iṟṟaikku iraṇṭāyiram āṇkaḷukku muṉ tamiḻakap paṇpum, maturai māmaṉṉaṉ māṇpum makkaḷiṉ aṟivu, āṟṟal, ākkam, āṭai, aṇikalaṉ mutaliyavaikaḷ palavum aḻakāka eṭuttukkāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. innūl pōr purivatil oppuyarvaṟṟavaṉākat tikaḻnta pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉ āṟṟiya pōraip pukaḻntu pāṭuvatāl iṅku avaṉuṭaiya allatu araci yuṭaiya allatu makkaḷuṭaiya aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ ciṟappuk kūṟap paṭavillai. eṉṟālum ōraḷavu akkāla makaḷir aṇinta talai yaṇikaḷ41 mārpaṇikaḷ42 cilampukaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭap peṟ ṟuḷḷaṉa.
poṟṟāmaraip pūvaip pūṇṭu naṉmai nalkum nāṉāvita nakaikaḷai aḷḷik koṭukkum aracaṉē! kuṭṭa nāṭṭiṉar palaraiyum veṉṟu niṟkum kōvē eṉṟu ippāṭṭu aracaṉ aṇintuḷḷa vaḷaikaḷaiyum43 mālaikaḷaiyum ciṟappittuk kūṟukiṟatu.
iḵtaṉṟip pala pēraṇikalaṉkaḷaiyum 44, poṉṉāl ceyta tumpaip pūkkaḷaip pōrmaṟavarkaḷukkup pāṇṭiyaṉ vaḻaṅkiya peṟṟiyaiyum45 makarak kuḻaiyaiyum vīrakkaḻalkaḷaiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṟatu.46, 47.
9. neṭunal vāṭai
itu pattup pāṭṭiṉuḷ 7vatu iṭattaip peṟṟuḷḷa tākum. maturaik kaṇakkāyaṉār makaṉ nakkīraṉār talaiyālaṅ kāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉaip pukaḻntu pāṭiyatu.
innūlil nīṇṭa makarakkuḻai tāṅki niṉṟa kātukaḷum ciṟiya iṭattaiyuṭaiya tāḷuruvi aḻuttiya kātukaḷum eṭuttuk kāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.48
ōriṭattu makaḷir iṉṟum aṇintuvarum neḷivu eṉṉum muṭakku mōtiramum49, valampurivaḷai50 neṭiya tāli nāṇ, kaṭukkaṉ, kaḻuttaṇikaḷ, kālviral aṇikaḷ pōṉṟavai kuṟippiṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
maṟṟōriṭattup paṉittuḷi eṉa oḷi ciṟanta maṇikaḷ oḷirum mēkalaiyai aḻakiya iḷamaṅkaiyar tam nuṇṇiya iṭaiyil aṇintiruntaṉar eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
10. paṭṭiṉappālai
paṭṭiṉappālai pattuppāṭṭil oṉpatāvatu iṭattaip peṟṟuḷḷatu. pāṭiyavar kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉār. ivar karikāṟcōḻaṉ mītu 301 aṭikaḷ koṇṭa paṭṭiṉappālai eṉṉum ciṟanta pāmālaiyaic cūṭṭiyuḷḷār.
innūlil vīrakkaḻal 51, 52 ciṟuvarkaḷ aṇinta toṭi paṟṟi kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. iḵtaṉṟi celvamikka illaṅkaḷilē akaṉṟa muṉṟil, oḷi nutalum maṭanōkkum poruntiya arammai yaṉṉa aḻakiya aṇaṅkukaḷ aṇikalaṉkaḷ aṇintu nellait tiṉṉa varum kōḻikaḷai veruṭṭataṟku eṟinta makarakkuḻai (makaramīṉ vaṭivāyc ceyyappaṭṭa kātaṇikaḷ) kuṟippiṭappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa.53 pavaḷam, muttu (koṟkai muttu) marakatamaṇi, poṟkāl putalvar, poṟṟoṭi cāmpūnatam pōṉṟa pataṅkaḷ pala kāṇak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.
11. malaipaṭukaṭām
malaipaṭukaṭām eṉṉum kūttar āṟṟuppaṭai, pāṭṭut tokutiyil pattāvatu iṭattaip peṟṟuḷḷatu. itaṉaip pāṭiya pulavar kaucikaṉār ākum. pāṭal peṟṟavaṉ palkuṉṟak kōṭṭattuc ceṅkaṇmāttuvēḷ naṉṉaṉ makaṉ naṉṉaṉ āvāṉ. paricu vēṇṭil naṉṉaṉpāl cella, kūttarai tūṇṭiyataṉāl itaṟkuk kūttarāṟṟuppaṭai eṉṉum peyar vantatu.
innūlil atikamāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷ kuṟikkappaṭavillai. eṉiṉum pēraṇikalaṉkaḷ maṇi, poṉvaḷai poṟṟāmaraippū pōṉṟa oru cila aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum vaḷai, poṉmaṇi pōṉṟa peyarkaḷum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pāṭṭuṭait talaivaṉ malaināṭuṭaiya talaivaṉāṉatāl iṅku atikamāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷ kuṟikkappaṭavillai eṉṟu karutukiṉṟēṉ.53
12. paripāṭal
paripāṭal eṭṭut tokaikaḷil oṉṟu. itu kaṭaiccaṅka nūl. eḻupatu pāṭalkaḷil kāla veḷḷattāl aḻintatu pōka eñciyuḷḷaṉa 22 pāṭalkaḷākum. ivai nallantuvaṉār, nalvaḻuti yār nalleḻiniyār, nallaḻiciyār, nappaṇṇaṉār, nallaccutaṉār, kīrantaiyār, kaṭuvaṉ iḷaveyiṉaṉār, kuṉṟam pūtaṉār pōṉṟa pala pulavarkaḷ pāṭiya pāṭal tokuppēyākum. mēlum innūl tirumāl, murukavēḷ, kāṭukiḻāḷ, vaikai, maturai mutaliya teyvaṅkaḷiṉ peyarum āṟu, nakarp pōṉṟavaikaḷiṉ perumai yum kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
itu akaccuvaip poruḷaip paṟṟiyē peritum kūṟum nūl. eṉṟālum āṅkāṅkē aṇikalaṉkaḷaip paṟṟiya kuṟippu kaḷum kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
innūlil makaḷir tam talaiyil talaikkōlam eṉṟa aṇi kalaṉkaḷai aḻakuṟa aṇintu vantatākak kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. mēlum vaḷai, āḻi, vākuvalayam, mēkalai, kāñci, toyyakam pōṉṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum kuṟippiṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
talaikkōlattil palvēṟu aṇikaḷ kuṟippiṭappaṭ ṭiruppatuṭaṉ ciṟārkkaṇiyum muñcakam eṉṉum talaiyaṇiyum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.54 makaramīṉ vaṭivil ceyyap peṟṟa nutalaṇi55, kātil pūṇum kuḻaikaḷ, kuṇṭalaṅkaḷ, kaṭippu56, pavaḷavaḷai57, mēkalai, kāñci ākiya iṭaiyaṇikaḷ, kaivaḷai, mōtiram, toyyakam, vākuvalayam mutaliya aṇikaḷum mēlāṭaiyai iṟukap piṇikkum mēkalaiyum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
tōḷil tōḷvaḷaiyum toṭiyum pūṇṭiruntaṉar. tōḷ aṇikalaṉākat toṭi kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṟatu. iḷam maṅkaikku iṉpa uṇarcci poṅki eḻukiṟatu. avaḷatu tōḷ pūrittut toṭiyai aḻuntap paṇṇukiṟatu eṉpataip paṭam piṭittuk kāṭṭukiṟatu.
kaikaḷil aṇiyum aṇikalaṉkaḷāka viḷaṅkum palavakaik kaṭakam, vaḷaikāppu, kolucu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kaiviralkaḷil pūṇum maṇikaḷ patitta mōtiraṅkaḷum  iṭuppiṉ puṟattē aṇiyum eṭṭu vaṭaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya kāñci eṉṟa nucuppaṇi, pavaḷamaṇikaḷ kōtta ēḻu vaṭaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya mēkalai eṉṉum nucuppaṇiyum pūṇṭiruntaṉar eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. nīrāṭum poḻutu makaḷir mēkalaiyai āṭaiyiṉ mītu iṟukap piṇittuk koḷvatu uṇṭeṉṟum terikiṟatu. aḻakiya pūṅkoṭikaḷ ōṭaippoṉ ṉāl ceyyappeṟṟa cilampiṉ paralkaḷuḷ muttukkaḷ pōṭṭuc ceyyappeṟṟiruntaṉa. itu nittila variccilampu eṉappaṭum. mēlum kātilum kuvaḷai kuṇṭalam, kaṭippu, ōlai eṉṉum palavakaik kātaṇikaḷ aṇintiruntatākat terikiṟatu.
ipparipāṭal mūlam pāṇṭiya nāṭṭup pāvai nallār kiṟittava ūḻi arumpumuṉṉarē eṇṇilā vakaippaṭṭa poṉ aṇikaḷaiyum maṇikaḷ patikkap peṟṟa aṇikaḷaiyum pēraṇi kaḷaiyum ciṟṟaṇikaḷaiyum aṇintu iṉpamuṭaṉ vāḻntārkaḷ eṉṟu aṟikiṟōm.
13. kalittokai
kalittokai, kaṟṟār ēttum kali eṉṟu ciṟappittuk kūṟappaṭum tokai nūl.
kaṭaiccaṅkak kālattiṉ iṟutiyil tamiḻaka makaḷir atika māṉa poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷaip pūṇṭiruntaṉar eṉpataṟku in nūl nalla cāṉṟāka uḷḷatu. maṉṉarkaḷum vaṇikarkaḷum nilak kiḻārum vīrarum pala aṇikalaṉkaḷaip pūṇṭuvantuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu innūliṉ mūlam naṉkaṟikiṉṟōm.
kālil poṉṉāl ceyyappeṟṟa vāypiḷanta poṟ cataṅkaiyum araiyil poṉmaṇikaḷaiyuṭaiya vaṭamum ataṉ mēl pavaḷavaṭamum kaiyil naṇṭiṉkaṇ pōl arumpu vēlaikaḷ ceyta kōṟṟoḻil avirkiṉṟa toṭiyum, iṭaiyil, veṭṭāta vāḷum maḻuvum toṅkaviṭappaṭṭa iṭattaiyuṭaiya pūṇum, mārpil karumuttu maṇiyum cērnta muttuvaṭamum ataṉ mēl nīla maṇikaḷāl ceyyap peṟṟa kaṇṭār maruḷum maṇimālaikaḷum vēṟu pala aṇikalaṉkaḷum aṇipeṟa aṇintiruntārkaḷ eṉṟu terikiṟatu.
palavitap poṉṉaṇi60 talaiyaṇi61 ciṟār aṇiyum piṟai62, vayantakam63, tōḷaṇi - patakkam ciṟuvar aṇikaḷum64, vāku valayam kātaṇi - kuṇṭalam, kaiyaṇi - kaṅkaṇam piṟavaḷaikaḷum65 mōtiraṅ kaḷum66 kiṇkiṇiyum67 vīravaḷai, araiyaṇi - araiñāṇa, kāla aṇi - vīrakkaḻal vīrakkaṇṭai, kālviral aṇi mutaliya palavita aṇikalaṉkaḷum pūṇṭiruntatāka innūliṉiṉṟu aṟiyalām.
ovvoru uṟuppilum aṇikaḷ palvēṟu vitaṅkaḷāy iruntaṉa. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka kātaṇiyil makarak kuṇṭalam carppak kuṇṭalam, tōṭu, ōlai eṉṟum kaḻuttaṇiyil kārai, mālai, vaṭam pōṉṟa palavita aṇikalaṉkaḷum iruntaṉa eṉṟu kalit tokaiyil kāṇkiṉṟōm.68
14. naṟṟiṇai
naṟṟiṇai, kaṭaic caṅkak kālattu eṭṭuttokai nūl kaḷāṉa puṟanāṉūṟu, patiṟṟuppattu, aiṅkuṟunūṟu, kalit tokai, paripāṭal, akanāṉūṟu, kuṟuntokai ākiyavaṟṟōṭu cērttu eṇṇappaṭuvatu.
naṟṟiṇaiyilum tamiḻ makkaḷ aṇinta aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ cila peyarkaḷ kāṇak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. talaikkōlattil oṉṟāṉa curitam kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṟatu. itu poṉṉāl ākiya tirukuppūp pōṉṟatu. ivvaṇi curitakam eṉappaṭum.69
kātukaḷil aṇiyappaṭum palvēṟu vakaiyāṉa kuḻaikaḷil kaṉaṅkuḻaiyum iṅkuk kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.70 iḵtaṉṟi cimikki pōṉṟu kuḻaiyil ūcal koḷuvappaṭṭa oṇ kuḻaiyum kāṇap paṭukiṟatu.71 vīrattiṉ aṟikuṟiyāka vīra maṟavarkaḷ aṇiyum tōḷ toṭiyākiya kōlkoṭiyum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.72 kaikaḷil caṅku vaḷaiyalkaḷum73 piṟa vaḷaikaḷum74 makaḷir tam melliya viral kaḷil mōcai eṉṉum mōtiramum75 aṇintu vantaṉar.
makaḷir putalvarkku maṇikaḷ kōtta kurumpai pōṉṟa kiṇkiṇi eṉṉum kāl aṇiyai iṭṭu aḻaku pārttatum aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatu. 76
15. akanāṉūṟu
akaccuvaip poruḷaip paṟṟi caṅkap pulavarkaḷ pāṭiya ceyyuḷkaḷil akavaṟpākkaḷāl āṉavaikaḷaip piritteṭuttup paṉṉiraṇṭu aṭikaḷukku mēṟpaṭṭa pākkaḷai neṭuntokai eṉṟa peyarāl tokuttaṉar. ituvē akanāṉūṟu eṉappaṭum.
innūlil cila iṭaṅkaḷil  aṇikalaṉkaḷaip paṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cilampu76, vīravaḷai, vīrakkaḻal77, poṉvaḷai, caṅkuvaḷai, kiṇkiṇi (cataṅkai)78, kuṭaccūl (oru vakaic cilampu)79, ceñcūṭṭukkuḻai, kōṟṟāṭi (aḻakiya vaḷai), kōlvaḷai80, cuḻalkoṭi81, polantār (poṉṉari mālai), polaṅkalam (poṉṉaṇi), maṇiceymaṇṭai (maṇikaḷ iḻaitta poṟkalaṉ) pōṉṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷ pala kūṟap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.82.
caṅkak kālattilē tamiḻakattil poṉṉum maṇikaḷum aṇikaḷum poṟkaṭṭilum poṉ vaṭṭilum piṟavum ērāḷamāka iruntuvantaṉa eṉpataṟku innūl nalla eṭuttukkāṭṭut tarukiṉṟatu.
16. puṟanāṉūṟu
eṭṭuttokai nūlkaḷil oṉṟākum. innūl tolkāp piyattiṟku muṉtōṉṟiya ceyyuṭkaḷaiyum, piṉṉar tōṉṟiya ceyyuṭkaḷaiyum taṉṉakattē koṇṭuḷḷatu. itu tamiḻar nākarikattaiyuṇarttum perum nūlākum.
tamiḻarkaḷ mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpiruntē muṉ kaiyil vaḷaikaḷai aṇintiruntaṉar eṉṟu terikiṟatu.83 vēntarkaḷ kālil vīrakkaḻal aṇintiruntaṉar eṉṟu kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu.84 makaḷir iṭaiyil poṉṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa pala kācukaḷ kōtta mēkalaiyum85 talaiyil poṉṉāṟ ceyyap peṟṟa kaṇṇiyum pūṇṭiruntārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. vaḷainta kuḻaikaḷai maṅkaiyar kātiṟ pūṭṭiyiruntaṉar eṉpatu naṉkaṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatu.86 nāñcil malaittalaivaṉ oruvaṉ muttu vaṭaṅkaḷaip pūṇṭiruntatōṭu poṉmālaiyum, poṟpūvum cūṭiyiruntāṉ eṉṟum aṟikiṉṟōm.87, 88 iḵtaṉṟip pala viṭaṅ kaḷil poṉṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa aṇikalaṉkaḷ pūṇ mutaliyavai kaḷaip pūṇṭiruntāṉ eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. cila maṉṉarkaḷ allatu vīrarkaḷ aṇinta aṇikalaṉkaḷ, avarkaḷ paricāka aḷitta aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
17. peruṅkatai
peruṅkatai piṟkālattil eḻuntatu. eṉṟālum caṅka nūl pōl pōṟṟap peṟuvatu. itu koṅkuvēḷir eṉṟa pulavarāl iyaṟṟap peṟṟatu. ivvāciriyar camaṇa camayattavar eṉṟu kūṟappaṭu kiṟatu.
innūlil patumāvati maṇakkōlam pūṇṭāḷ eṉpatai āciriyar aḻakāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. avaḷ vairamaṇi kaḷāl ceyyap peṟṟa yavaṉa nāṭṭu aṇikalap pēḻaiyil89 aḻakiya poṉkoṭi, kaḷikai eṉṉum kaḻuttaṇi, poṉṉāṟ ceyta poṭṭu,  poṉpaṭṭam, poṉṉōlai (kātaṇi) aivakai vaṇṇam oḷirumāṟu ceyta maṇikaḷ patitta mēkalai (iṭai yaṇi)90, poṉvaḷai, poṟkaṭakam, cūṭakam, pāṭakam, nūpuram mutaliya aṇikalaṉkaḷai eḻiluṟap pūṇṭāḷ eṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭukiṟatu.91
utayaṇaṉ, kātaliyiṉ pirivāṟṟāmaiyiṉāl uyir tuṟakka ettaṉittāṉ. avaṉatu tōḻarkaḷ avaṉatu kātaliyiṉ aṇi kalaṉkaḷaik kāṭṭi avaṉ ceyalait taṭuttaṉar. āciriyar avaḷatu aṇikalaṉkaḷāṉa poṉṉarimālai, poṉpaṭṭam92, tilakam, kuḻai93, muttumālai, kaḷikai, cītēvi, tōḷaṇiyākiya makaram, kaiyaṇiyākiya kaṭakam, muppattiraṇṭu vaṭṭak kācukaḷ kōttamālaikaḷ toṅkum aḻakiya nucuppaṇiyākiya viri
cikai94, tavaḷaivāyk kiṇkiṇi mutaliyavaikaḷai eḻilpeṟa eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.95
iḵtaṉṟi toyyakam, pullakam (teṉpalli, vaṭa palli eṉṉum talaiyaṇi)  kaṭikaiyāram (nāḻikai kāṭṭum karuvikaḷ pōl ceyyappeṟṟa mālai) poṉ mālaikaḷ, mēkalai, kāñci, iratap palkāḻ, makarak kuṇṭalam, pavaḷavaṭam96, muttumālai, poṉkaḻaṅku vaṭivāyc ceyyappeṟṟa mālai, nellikkāy mālai, kāviti, ēṉāti, eṭṭi mutaliya paṭṭaṅkaḷukkuriya aṟikuṟi yāka aracaṉ aḷitta poṟpaṭṭam, ēkāvali mutaliya aṇikaḷ pala eṭuttuk kāṭṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.97
mēlum aracilaip poṉ aṭar98, itayavācaṉai99, ēka valli100, kaḻaṟcikkāymālai101, kaḷikai102, ciṟuneṟṟāli103, tilakam104, toṭi105, neṟṟikkaṭṭi106, paṉṉiraittāli107, paṇ maṇittāli108, pāṇṭil109, pūṅkoṭip poṟkalam110, pūṇ111, maṇivaṭam112, māttirai113, mārāṭṭam114, muttavaḷḷi115, muttu mālai116, mummaṇikkācu117-118 pōṉṟa pala aṇikalaṉkaḷ eṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

2. cilappatikāram
2.	cilap : pukār - kaṭalāṭu 82 - 108	
alattaka mūṭṭiya vañceñ cīṟaṭi	
nalattaku melvira ṉallaṇi ceṟīip	
pariyaka nūpuram pāṭakam cataṅkai	
ariyakaṅ kāluk kamaivuṟa aṇintu	
kuṟaṅku ceṟitiraḷ kuṟaṅkiṉiṟ ceṟittup	
piṟaṅkiya muttarai muppat tirukāḻ	
niṟaṅkiḷar pūntuki ṉīrmaiyi ṉuḻīik	
kāmar kaṇṭikai taṉṉoṭu piṉṉiya	
tūmaṇit tōḷvaḷai tōḷuk kaṇintu	
mattaka maṇiyoṭu vayiraṅ kaṭṭiya	
cittirac cūṭakañ cempoṟ kaivaḷai	
pariyakam vālvaḷai pavaḷap palvaḷai	
arimayir muṉkaik kamaivuṟa vaṇintu	
vāḷaip pakuvāy vaṇakkuṟu mōtiram	
kēḻkiḷar ceṅkēḻ kiḷarmaṇi mōtiram	
vāṅkuvil vayirattu marakatat tāḷceṟi	
kāntaṇ melviral karappa vaṇintu	
caṅkili nuṇṭoṭar pūṇñāṇ puṉaiviṉai	
aṅkaḻut takavayi ṉāramō ṭaṇintu	
kayiṟkaṭai yoḻukiya kāmar tūmaṇi	
ceyattaku kōvaiyiṟ ciṟupuṟa maṟaittāṅ	
kintira nīlat tiṭaiyiṭai tiraṇṭa	
cantira pāṇi takaipeṟu kaṭippiṇai	
aṅkā takavayi ṉaḻakuṟa vaṇintu	
teyva vuttiyoṭu ceḻunīr valampuri	
toyyakam pullakam toṭarnta talaikkaṇi	
maiyī rōtikku māṇpuṟa vaṇintu

30.	tiru.muru. 19 : 37

paṭaṅkaḷ
1. itu, tañcaip peruuṭaiyār kōyilil uḷḷa periya nāyaki ammaiyiṉ veṇkalap paṭimam. itu 11-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil ceyyappaṭṭatu. ivvuruvamum itaṉmītu kāṇappaṭum āṭaiyum aṇikalaṉkaḷum paṇṭait tamiḻnāṭṭup peṇkaḷiṉ āṭai aṇikalaṉkaḷukku ōr eṭuttukkāṭṭākum. kātaṇi, kaḻuttaṇi, tōḷaṇi, kaiyaṇi, kālaṇi pōṉṟavaikaḷ tulāmparamāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
2. inta uruvam kaṉṉiyākumari māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa cēranāṭṭup peṇkaḷiṉ vaḻiyil vanta nāyarkula naṅkaiyiṉ niḻal uruvappaṭam. ippaṭattil ivaḷ paṇṭaiya tamiḻp peṇkaḷiṉ pāṇiyil āṭai aṇintu aṇikalaṉkaḷ pūṇṭu aḻakuṭaṉ kāṭci aḷikkiṉṟāḷ.
3. inta niḻaṟpaṭam 19ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil tiru nelvēli māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa vēḷāḷar kulap peṇṇiṉ tōṟṟattai eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṟatu. ivaḷ kātil aṇintuḷḷa pāmpaṭam, taṇṭaṭṭi, muṭiccu, iṭṭaṭikkai, mēliṭu ākiya aṇi kaḷum, mūkkaṇiyum kaḻuttaṇiyum akkālattil vēḷāḷar kula peṇ makkaḷ aṇinta aṇikalaṉkaḷukku ēṟṟa eṭuttukkāṭṭāy ilaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
4. kaṉṉiyākumari māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa nāṭār kula naṅkaiyiṉ uruvappaṭam. ippeṇmaṇi 20ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattiluḷḷa tamiḻnāṭṭup peṇkaḷukkuriya pala aṇi kaḷai aṇintuḷḷāḷ. ivaḷ kātu vaṭittum iravikkai aṇintum pūṭi, karṉappū pōṉṟa pala kātaṇikaḷaiyum uṭkaṭṭu, cuttumaṇi, cuṇṭaikkāy mālai pōṉṟa pala kaḻuttaṇikaḷaiyum aṇintavaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṟāḷ. ivaḷatu tōṟṟam oru paṇakkārap peṇṇiṉ tōṟṟam pōl irukkiṟatu.
5. ippaṭam tirunelvēli māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa oru ēḻai nāṭār kula maṅkaiyiṉ paṭam. ippeṇmaṇi 19-ām nūṟṟāṇ ṭiṉ naṭuvil uḷḷavar. ivar oru cila kātaṇi, kaḻuttaṇi, tōḷaṇi, kaiyaṇikaḷuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṟār. ivaratu iṭuppil uḷḷa ciṟu kuḻantai kātu vaṭikkap peṟṟu īyakkuṇukku aṇintuḷḷatu. kaḻuttil pāci mālaiyum, kaiyil veḷḷik kāppukaḷum pūṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
6. inta niḻaṟpaṭam irāmanātapuram māvaṭṭattil 20ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ ārampattil uḷḷa nāṭār kula maṅkaiyuṭaiyatu. ivaratu kātil viciṟimurukum, pāmpaṭamum, kaḻuttil cuttumaṇi, kāṟai pōṉṟa aṇikaḷaiyum kaiyil kaiccari, kaṭakam, kāppu pōṉṟa aṇikaḷaiyum, kaiviralkaḷil mōtiraṅkaḷum aṇin tuḷḷār.
7. ippeṇ maturai māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa akampaṭiyarkula āraṇaṅku. ivaratu kātilum kaḻuttilum pala aṇikalaṉkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tōḷiṉ kīḻē vaṅkiyum, kontikkāy kolucum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
8. (1) irāmanātapuram māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa maṟavar kula maṅkai. (2) ātittirāviṭa arivai (3) kōvai māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa kavuṇṭarkulak kārikai (4) kōvai māvaṭṭattil muṉṉēṟṟam peṟāta iṉattaic cērnta iḷamaṅkai. ivaḷ kaḻuttaṇi, kātaṇikaḷuṭaṉ kāṭci aḷikkiṟāḷ.
9. intap paṭam acentā ōviyam. itu tirāviṭa nāṭṭu eḻil oḻukum oru iḷamaṅkaiyiṉ tōṟṟattai eṭuppuṭaṉ eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṟatu. talaiyaṇikaḷum kātaṇikaḷum kaḻuttaṇi kaḷum kaiyaṇikaḷum paḻaṅkālattavai eṉṟālum iṉṟaiya aṇikalaṉkaḷaiviṭa aḻakuṟap polikiṉṟaṉa. inta ōviyam ki.pi. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭil tīṭṭiyatu.
10. cintuveḷiyil kiṭaitta oru veṇkalap paṭimam. itu ki.mu. 3000 āṇṭiṟku muṉṉuḷḷatu. ippaṭimam oru nāṭṭiya māṭum naṅkaiyiṉ uruvamākak karutappaṭukiṟatu. ivaḷatu tōḷi ṉiṉṟu maṇikkaṭṭu varai, kaiyil pala vaḷaikaḷ aṭukkaṭukkāy aṇiyappaṭṭu uḷḷaṉa.
11. mokañcatārōvil kiṭaitta aṇikalaṉkaḷil iṅku kaḻuttil aṇiyum mālaikaḷ aintu uḷḷaṉa. ivai cintuveḷit tirāviṭap peruṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ aṇinta poṉ aṇikaḷ. ivaikaḷil maṇikaḷ pala patikkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivai viyattakuvēlaippāṭukaḷ koṇṭavai. ivaikaḷil oṉṟu 240 - poṉmaṇikaḷum oṉṟu 70. poṉmaṇikaḷum, oṉṟu 27- poṉmaṇikaḷum, oṉṟu 6 caraṅkaḷum, oṉṟu mūṉṟu caraṅkaḷum uṭaiyaṉa. ivaikaḷil iṭai iṭaiyē vilaiyuyarnta kaṟkaḷum, maṇikaḷum, patakkaṅkaḷum kōrkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. oṉṟu aṭṭikai pōṉṟuḷḷatu. kīḻē kāṇappaṭuvatu caṅkili pōṉṟa kaḻuttaṇi.
12. itu, arappāvil akaḻntu kaṇṭa ariya kai aṇi. itu, maṇikkaṭṭu pakkam aṇiyum oruvakaikkāppu. mēlē kūrmaiyāṉa muṭkaḷ pōl ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. uṭpuṟam irutayavaṭivilum veḷippuṟattōṟṟam cūriyaṉ taṉ oḷikkiraṇaṅkaḷuṭaṉ kāṇap paṭuvatu pōlum uḷḷa intak kaiyaṇi kōvai māvaṭṭattil uḷḷaṉa. tiruttam peṟāta paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ kaikaḷil iṉṟum uḷḷaṉa.
13. nākar.
14. (a) mokañcatārōvil kaṇṭeṭukkap peṟṟa iru kaḻuttaṇikaḷum (mālaikaḷum), ataṉ utirip pākaṅkaḷum kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
(ā) maturai mīṉāṭci - cuntarēcuvarar kōyilil uḷḷa mīṉāṭci tēvikkuriya talaiyaṇi. itu muttukkaḷālāṉa poṉ aṇi.
(i) ituvum aṅkuḷḷa maṟṟōr talaiyaṇi.
(ī) ituvum mīṉāṭciyammaṉ kōyilil uḷḷa oru kaḻuttaṇiyum (navakaṇṭikaiyum), patakkamumākum.
15. itu, maturai mīṉāṭci ammaṉiṉ talaiyaṇikaḷil oṉṟu. itu vilaiyuyarnta maṇikaḷ patitta poṉ aṇi.
16. (1) talaic cāmāṉ 1 (a) nilavu piṟai 1 (ā) cūriyap piṟai (2) māṭṭiyum tōṭum cimikkiyum (3) campum neṟṟic cuṭṭiyum (4) campum neṟṟic cuṭṭiyum. (5) campu (6) kirttā (7) neṟṟiccuṭṭi (8) cimikki
17. (1) caṭāṅkam (caṭai aṇi) (2) kuñcam (3) irākkuṭi (4) cantira villai.
18. kātaṇikaḷ 
(1) kuṇukku (nērppārvaiyum, pakkappārvaiyum (2) makara kuṇṭalam (3) kuṇukku (maturai maṟavar maṅkaiyarkaḷ aṇintu varum kātaṇi (nērppārvaiyum pakkap pārvaiyum)
19. kirttā (nērppārvaiyum, pakkap pārvaiyum (2) kuṇukku (nērppārvaiyum, pakkappārvaiyum)
20. (1) koppu (2) pūvāḷi (3) cūri (muṉ pārvai) pakkappārvai, (4) taṇṭaṭṭi (muṉpārvai) pakkappārvai (maturai maṟavar kulap peṇkaḷ aṇintu varuvatu) (5) tār kuṇukku (pakkat tōṟṟamum, nēr mukat tōṟṟamum.)
21. (1) pullākku (2) pēcari (3) nattu (palvēṟu tōṟṟam) (4) nākapātam (nērppārvaiyum, pakkappārvaiyum)
22. (1, 2, 3) makara kuṇṭalaṅkaḷ (4,5,6) carppa kuṇṭalaṅkaḷ (7) irattiṉa kuṇṭalam (8) poṉ ōlai (9) vāḷi (10) poṟ kuṇṭalam (11) tōṭu (12, 13) kuṇṭalaṅkaḷ.
23. kaḻuttaṇikaḷ : 1 mutal 8 varaiyuḷḷa uruvaṅkaḷ tamiḻakattil palvēṟu iṉattavarkaḷ aṇiyum tālikaḷ (9) cuttu maṇiyum tāliyum.
24. (1) kāy vaṭivāṉa poṉ maṇikaḷ kōtta kaḻuttaṇi 
(2) uṭkaṭṭup pōṉṟu iru aricikaḷai oṉṟāka iṇaittuk kaḻuttaic cuṟṟiyaṇiyum oru aṇi. (3) kaḻuttaic cuṟṟiyaṇiyum mūṉṟu vaṭaṅkaḷālāṉa ciṟu arici kōtta oru kaḻuttaṇi.
25. (1) nellikkāy mālai (2) picciyarumpu mālai (3) kottamalli mālai.
26. cuttumaṇi. itil kōrkkappaṭṭa ovvoru maṇikkum taṉittaṉip peyarkaḷ uṇṭu. cuttumaṇiyiṉ naṭuvil tāli iṭam peṟṟirukkiṟatu. itu civaliṅka vaṭivil amaintirukkiṟatu. cuttu maṇi nūlil allatu poṉ koṭiyil kōrkkappaṭṭirukkum. iru puṟamum iru tāyittukkaḷiṉ uḷḷē mantirat takaṭukaḷ vaikkap peṟṟirukkum. tāyittukkaḷai aṭuttup pala ciṉṉaṅkaḷ uḷḷa maṇikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. (1) peruñcīraka vitaiyiṉ oru kottu (2) poṭṭu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum mantirat taṉmaikaḷ uḷḷa vaṭṭamāṉa 4 maṇikaḷ (3) kāykaḷ (4) pū allatu paḻam (5) tuḷaci māṭam (6) cantaṉak kumpā allatu pāttiram (7) arakkuk - kulīcam - itu kōyiliṉ koṭumuṭiyiṉ oru uruva amaippu (8) vātumaik koṭṭaikaḷ 6, (9) aṉṉācip paḻam (10) mulāmpaḻam (11) cātik kāy, (12) kattarikkāy (13) uṇavu uṇṇum pāttiram, (14) paṉṉīrcempum (15) tiṭṭavaṭṭamākak kūṟamuṭiyāta tīṅkaṉik kottu, (16) miḷakāykaḷ, (17) campaṅkikaḷ 2 (18) aṭaiyāḷam kāṇamuṭiyāta paḻakkulai, (19) kottamallikkāyk kottu, (20) vilva ilai, (21) māṅkāykaḷ 2, (22) vāḻaippaḻaṅkaḷ, (23) tēṅkāy, (24) tāmarai mukai, (25) peyar kūṟamuṭiyāta ilai, (26) peyar kūṟa muṭiyāta paḻam.
27. (1, 2, 3) kaḻuttaiyoṭṭiyaṇiyum aṭṭikai pōṉṟa poṉ kaḻuttaṇi (4) veḷḷik kāppukaḷ.
28. maṇikaḷ patitta patakkaṅkaḷum, poṉmaṇi mālaiyum.
29. puya aṇi :  (1) irattiṉa vaṅki (a) irattiṉa vaṅkiyiṉ pakkattōṟṟam (2) poṉ vaṅki (3) pācippantu. itu āṇum peṇṇum aṇiyum aṇikalaṉ.
30. muṉkaiyaṇi : (1) kaiccari
31. kālaṇikaḷ : (1) kālcari - pū vēlaippāṭukaḷ ceyyap paṭṭa veḷḷi aṇi.
32. (1) veḷḷikkāppu (nērppārvaiyum, pakkap pārvaiyum) (2, 2 a) kāppiṉ nērppārvaiyum pakkap pārvaiyum (3) kāppu (4, ē) kāppiṉ palavakait tōṟṟam.
33. (kīḻ varicai) (1) kāppu (2) kolucu (3) kaṭakam
(mēl varicai) (4) muṭakku mōtiram (neḷivu) itu vaṇakkuṟu mōtiram eṉavum kūṟappaṭum. (5) muṭakku mōtiram (pakkap pārvai) (6) oṟṟaikkal patitta mōtiram (7) civantippū mōtiram.
34. nucuppaṇikaḷ : (1) mēkalai (2) kāñci (3) kalāpam (4) tāmam (5) viricikai ( oru pakkat tōṟṟam)
35.  (1, 2) taṇṭaikaḷ (veḷḷiyaṇikaḷ) (3, 3 a) taṇṭaiyiṉ pakkap pārvaiyum nērppārvaiyum (vivacāyikaḷiṉ aṇi)
36. (1, 2, 3, 4) taṇṭaiyiṉ palvēṟu tōṟṟaṅkaḷ.
37. (1) vēlaṅkāyk kolucu (2) attikkāyk kolucu (3, 4, 4a, 5) pātacaraṅkaḷ (caṅkili), piṟkālakāl aṇikaḷ
38. pātac cilampu
39. kāl viral aṇi
(1, 2) muñci (3) calaṅkai meṭṭi (4) meṭṭi (5) pillaṇai (6) viralaṇi (7, 7a, 7ā) mīṉ meṭṭiyiṉ palavakait tōṟṟam. ivai iṭatu kāliṉ 2vatu, 3vatu viralkaḷil aṇipavai. 3vatu viralil aṇiyum mīṉ ciṉṉam civaṉiṉ oru aṭaiyāḷam.
40. (1, 1a) kāl viralaṇi (nērp pārvaiyum pakkap pārvaiyum (2) pīliyiṉ pakkappārvai (mēlēyuḷḷa vaḷaiyam viralil māṭṭuvataṟkuriyatu) (2a) pīliyiṉ mēṟpuṟat tōṟṟam (3, 3a) calaṅkai miñciyiṉ pakkappārvaiyum, mēṟpārvaiyum.
